# htmx and Alpine.js lint rules: research for #451

Research for the feature request in [#451 (comment)](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/djangofmt/issues/451#issuecomment-5797376489), carried out on 2026-09-23.

- **Versions examined:**
  - htmx 4.0.0 (`4195bc0d`) and 2.0.11 (`fa978b24`);
  - Alpine.js 3.17.4 (`20ee0e92`) with every official plugin, and Async Alpine 2.0.3;
  - markuplint 5.0.1 (`7c3c510a`) and 4.18.3;
  - Django 6.1.1 and `main` (`951d13c7`);
  - djangofmt 1.0.0 on markup_fmt `c5ebe002`.
- **How to read this note:**
  - §1–§7 are the synthesis and recommendations.
  - Appendices A–E hold the detailed findings, with each claim pinned to a permalink or a docs anchor.
- **Reproductions:** claims marked *(jsdom)*, *(ran)* or *probe* were reproduced by running the published libraries in throwaway harnesses (jsdom, Node, parse5, Django 6.1.1). The harnesses are not part of this note.

## Summary

- **Worth doing.** Most of the requested checks catch bugs that neither library reports:
  - Alpine silently ignores an unknown `x-*` directive.
  - A mistyped `x-on` modifier makes a click or key handler never fire.
  - htmx 4's value parser accepts any typo without failing.
  - A few statically detectable mistakes stop Alpine initialising every later component on the page.
- **The request needs some correction** (§6):
  - Three checks need narrowing: `hx-vals` "JSON or `js:`" is right for htmx 2 only; the one-root rule applies only to a `<template>` that carries `x-for`, `x-if` or `x-teleport`; and `x-for` without `:key` is a recommendation, not a bug.
  - Three claims in the comment are wrong: `escapejs` has worked with Alpine's CSP build since 3.17.0; `json_script` can never go in an attribute; and `unlocalize` escapes nothing.
- **Design.**
  - Classify each raw attribute name once, into framework, directive, argument and modifiers.
  - Then look it up in a per-directive registry: allowed elements, argument set, modifier set, and whether the value is JavaScript.
  - This is markuplint's name resolution without its "directives skip every rule" behaviour.
- **Data.** This is the "generate from the library source" the maintainer asked for:
  - Generate names from first-party files pinned by version. For htmx, that is the web-types JSON and `upgrade-check.py` inside the `htmx.org` npm tarball. For Alpine, it is Alpine's own source inside the `@alpinejs/*` tarballs.
  - Hand-write the value grammars and value kinds, guarded by drift tests.
- **markuplint.**
  - Reuse its HTML data (`@markuplint/html-spec`, MIT) for the planned `djangofmt_html_spec` crate and for #425, and reuse its name-resolution design.
  - Do not reuse its Alpine or htmx data. That data is hand-written, covers no plugins and no htmx 4, checks no modifiers or values, and regressed in v5.
- **htmx needs a version setting.** 4.0.0 shipped on 2026-08-28, but npm `latest` stays 2.x until early 2027. Several attributes changed meaning or were removed, and real templates use htmx 2 names.
- **The security rule is real, but needs care.**
  - Autoescape does not protect JS-evaluated attributes: the browser decodes `&#x27;` before the code runs. This was reproduced in both Alpine builds, and htmx's own docs say the same.
  - However, 78% of the `{{ }}` values in Alpine JS attributes across 56 Django repos have no filter at all.
  - So the rule must judge each value by its position (inside a quoted string, or bare) and by its last filter, and it must ship in preview.
- **Nothing in djangofmt blocks this today.**
  - The parser already exposes every htmx and Alpine attribute as a raw name/value pair.
  - `<template>` children are ordinary nodes.
  - The formatter keeps htmx values byte-for-byte. That matters, because htmx 4 does not trim `hx-vals`.

## 1. The request, check by check

| #  | Requested check                                                 | Verdict                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Evidence                                                      | Proposed home (§3.2)                                            |
| -- | --------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1  | Unknown attributes (`x-cheeseburger`)                           | **Real bug, never reported.** Alpine runs `directiveHandlers[type] \|\| noop`. Third-party plugins, `Alpine.directive()` calls and non-Alpine attributes (Popper's `x-placement`, `x-webkit-*`) share the prefix, so the list must be extensible | §A "Unknown directives at runtime"; §4                        | `alpine-unknown-directive`                                      |
| 2  | Modifiers on `x-on` and `x-model`                               | **Real bug, worse than dead code.** On events named `keydown`/`keyup`, or containing `click`, `mouse` or `contextmenu`, an unknown modifier is read as a key filter, so `@click.prevnt` never fires *(jsdom)*                                    | §A "x-on modifiers and event names"                           | `alpine-invalid-modifier`                                       |
| 3  | Official plugins and Async Alpine                               | Plugin directives are registered in source and can be generated. Async Alpine adds 2 attributes and 5 strategy names; a misspelled strategy silently loads eagerly *(jsdom)*                                                                     | §A "Official plugins", "Async Alpine"                         | rows 1–2 plus a config list                                     |
| 4  | htmx 4 attributes and `<hx-partial>`                            | 36 core attributes plus extension attributes. Names are open-ended: `data-hx-`, `:inherited`/`:append`, the `hx-on` forms, `hx-status:NNN`. `<hx-partial>` also exists in 2.0.11                                                                 | §B "htmx 4 attribute inventory"                               | `htmx-unknown-attribute`                                        |
| 5  | Values of `hx-swap`, `hx-method`                                | `hx-swap`: a closed core set (12 styles plus 4 aliases), case-sensitive, and an unknown style throws in v4. Extensions add styles, so this needs an option. `hx-method`: the docs list 6 verbs, while the source accepts any token               | §B "Value grammars (4.0.0)"                                   | `htmx-invalid-value`                                            |
| 6  | Template values in JS-evaluated attributes need a "safe" filter | **Real XSS**, but a filter allowlist alone is wrong. `escapejs` only protects inside a quoted JS string; `json_script` breaks the tag; `unlocalize` passes strings through. The check must be position-aware                                     | §C (all); §A "CSP build"                                      | `unsafe-js-attr-value`                                          |
| 7  | `<template>` has one root child                                 | **Narrower than asked**: only templates with `x-for`, `x-if` or `x-teleport`. Zero roots throws; extra roots are dropped, and only `x-for` warns *(jsdom)*                                                                                       | §A "Structural rules, verified"                               | `alpine-template-directive`                                     |
| 8  | `x-for` must be on `<template>`                                 | **Real bug.** The same applies to `x-if`, and to `x-teleport`, whose failure aborts initialisation of the whole page                                                                                                                             | §A "Structural rules, verified"                               | `alpine-template-directive`                                     |
| 9  | No `x-html` on `<template>`                                     | **Real bug**: nothing renders and no error is raised, because innerHTML on a template fills its inert content                                                                                                                                    | §A "Structural rules, verified"                               | `alpine-template-directive`                                     |
| 10 | Warn when `x-for` lacks `:key`                                  | **A recommendation**: without it the key is the index, so per-row state drifts. Separately, a `:key` placed on the loop child instead of the template is a **real bug**, because it is silently ignored                                          | §A "Structural rules, verified"                               | missing key: opt-in; misplaced key: `alpine-template-directive` |
| 11 | Capital letters in `x-on` event names                           | **Real bug when the intended event is camelCase**: the HTML parser lowercases attribute names, so `@myEvent` listens for `myevent`. The same happens with `.pageDown`, `:viewBox` and `hx-on:myEvent`                                            | §A "Attribute-name grammar"; §B "Attribute-name grammar (v4)" | `mixed-case-event-name`                                         |
| 12 | `hx-vals` is JSON or starts with `js:`                          | **htmx 2 only.** In v4, brace-less HCON (`hx-vals="tenant:acme"`) is documented as valid. Only a value starting with `{` must be strict JSON, and leading whitespace silently switches the value to HCON                                         | §B "The requester's claims"                                   | `htmx-invalid-value` (version-aware)                            |

These checks were not requested but turned up during the research:

- **Mistakes that abort Alpine initialisation** for every later component on the page, all statically detectable *(jsdom)*:
  - `x-on` with no event;
  - `x-teleport` on anything other than `<template>`;
  - `x-transition="…"` with no stage;
  - an `x-for` value without `in`/`of`;
  - an `x-id` value that is not an array (§A "Other runtime failures").
- **htmx exact-string traps:**
  - `hx-boost="False"` enables boosting.
  - A bare `hx-validate` turns validation off.
  - `hx-push-url="False"` pushes the URL `False`.
  - Any `hx-encoding` other than `multipart/form-data` is ignored (§B value table).
- **htmx 2 names used under htmx 4:**
  - `hx-disable` changed meaning.
  - `hx-disabled-elt`, `hx-vars`, `hx-ext` and `hx-params` are gone.
  - v2's `hx-on::after-request` never fires (§B "htmx 2 → 4 diff").
- **`js:` in `hx-get` … `hx-action` (htmx 4)** runs JavaScript instead of sending the request. The existing `javascript-url` rule could cover it (§B "JS-evaluated attributes").

## 2. What djangofmt already provides (verified locally)

- **Every htmx and Alpine attribute is a raw name/value pair.**
  - In Django mode, any attribute that is not a template tag is parsed as `Attribute::Native(NativeAttribute { name, value, quote })` ([markup_fmt parser.rs L712-L753](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/markup_fmt/blob/c5ebe002d53736f1945ac8ec2f20df49878d9070/markup_fmt/src/parser.rs#L712-L753), [ast.rs L247-L251](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/markup_fmt/blob/c5ebe002d53736f1945ac8ec2f20df49878d9070/markup_fmt/src/ast.rs#L247-L251)).
  - So `@click.outside.prevent`, `:class`, `x-on:keyup.enter.debounce.500ms`, `hx-on::after-request` and `hx-target:inherited` all reach rules verbatim.
  - They keep the author's case, so `@myEvent` is still visible even though the browser would lowercase it.
- **Names can embed template syntax** ([parser.rs L774](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/markup_fmt/blob/c5ebe002d53736f1945ac8ec2f20df49878d9070/markup_fmt/src/parser.rs#L774)).
  - `x-on:{{ event_name }}="go()"` is a single attribute. This settles the matching "unverified" note in §C.
  - The classifier must bail out on names containing `{{`/`{%`, just as rules already skip values via `contains_interpolation` (`crates/djangofmt_lint/src/rules/helpers.rs:13`).
- **`{% if %}` between attributes** becomes `Attribute::JinjaBlock`, and `native_attrs` (`helpers.rs:37`) already walks its branches. This settles the other "unverified" note in §C.
- **Quotes inside `{{ … }}` are safe.** In `x-data="{ n: '{{ v|default:"n/a"|escapejs }}' }"` the value stays intact: a following duplicate `data-a` is still reported by `duplicate-attr` at the right column. This closes an open point in §C.
- **`<template>` children are ordinary nodes.** Only `script`, `style`, `pre` and `textarea` bodies are raw text ([parser.rs L1150](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/markup_fmt/blob/c5ebe002d53736f1945ac8ec2f20df49878d9070/markup_fmt/src/parser.rs#L1150)), so root counting is possible. `<hx-partial>` parses as a normal element.
- **Today these attributes parse cleanly and nothing checks them.**
  - Formatting and `check --select category:all --preview` were run on a sample using every syntax above.
  - The sample parsed, and no rule reported anything.
  - The only nod to frameworks is the `invalid-attr-value` doc, which promises "framework dialects such as HTMX or Alpine.js" pending `djangofmt_html_spec` (`crates/djangofmt_lint/src/rules/correctness/invalid_attr_value.rs:12`, `:73`).
- **The formatter keeps htmx values byte-for-byte.** Multi-line, leading-space and long `hx-vals`/`hx-headers`/`hx-config`/`hx-method` values were all tested. Its only change is `'…'` → `"…"` when the value contains no `"`.
  - This must stay true: htmx 4 does not trim these values (§B "Formatter caution").
  - A formatter fixture should lock it in.
- **Ecosystem coverage.** 8 of the 36 `ecosystem-check` projects use these attributes (Sourcegraph, 2026-09-23):

  | Project                            | Attributes | Files | Framework                  |
  | ---------------------------------- | ---------- | ----- | -------------------------- |
  | Misago                             | 267        | 61    | htmx                       |
  | djangopackages                     | 231        | 28    | htmx                       |
  | django-unfold                      | 133        | 32    | Alpine (101) and htmx (32) |
  | pretalx                            | 93         | 16    | htmx                       |
  | netbox                             | 89         | 27    | htmx                       |
  | wger                               | 41         | 5     | htmx                       |
  | django-admin-inline-paginator-plus | 28         | 2     | htmx                       |
  | django-debug-toolbar               | 1          | 1     | htmx                       |

  So `just ecosystem-check-lint-dev` exercises the htmx rules well, but the Alpine rules only through django-unfold. Ground the Alpine fixtures with Sourcegraph samples (`.agents/skills/add-lint-rule/research.md`).

## 3. Proposed design

### 3.1 One attribute classifier, then small rules

Parse each `NativeAttribute` name once, in `visit_native_attribute` (`crates/djangofmt_lint/src/checker.rs:274`), and dispatch on the result (the "classify once" pattern in `.agents/skills/add-lint-rule/checker-gating.md`):

```rust
enum FrameworkAttr<'s> {
    Alpine { directive: &'s str, arg: Option<&'s str>, modifiers: SmallVec<[&'s str; 4]>, shorthand: bool },
    Htmx { base: &'s str, arg: Option<&'s str>, inherited: bool, append: bool, data_prefix: bool },
    None,
}
```

- **Grammars.** The classifier follows each library's own parser:
  - Alpine's type is `^x-([^:^.]+)`, its argument `:([a-zA-Z0-9\-_:]+)` and its modifiers `\.[^.\]]+`; `@` becomes `x-on:` and `:` becomes `x-bind:` (§A "Attribute-name grammar").
  - For htmx: the `hx-`/`data-hx-` prefixes, `:inherited` then `:append` in that order only, the `hx-on`, `hx-on:` and `hx-on::` forms, and `hx-status:DDD|DDx|Dxx` (§B "Attribute-name grammar (v4)").
- **Registry.** A static entry per directive or attribute holds:
  - the elements it is allowed on (`template` only, for three Alpine directives);
  - its argument: required, a closed set, or open;
  - its modifiers: a closed set, plus key names only on `keydown`/`keyup`;
  - its value kind: JS expression, JS statements, CSS selector, class list, plain name, none, or a special grammar;
  - deprecations.
- **Three departures from markuplint's model** (§D.2):
  1. A directive is not exempt from rules. markuplint skips every directive in every attribute rule, so it cannot even ban `x-html`.
  2. The raw name is kept for case and duplicate checks. markuplint lowercases names, which hides `@myEvent`, and it reports `@click` plus `@click.prevent` as duplicates (and its autofix deletes one).
  3. "The value is JavaScript" is an explicit flag. The security rule needs it, and markuplint's `isDynamicValue` does not mean that.
- **Scope.** Only names under a framework prefix are examined, as in every surveyed tool (§E "Takeaways").
  - On `@event` and `:attr` only the modifiers can be checked, because the argument is an open set.
  - A general unknown-attribute check belongs to the HTML-spec work, not here.

### 3.2 Rules, in phases

All rules below start as `preview_since` (`.agents/skills/add-lint-rule/SKILL.md:98`) and are promoted after `just ecosystem-check-lint-dev` and a Sourcegraph pass. The names follow the framework-first precedent of `django-static-url` and `django-url-pattern`.

| Phase | Rule                        | Checks                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Category    | Data                                      |
| ----- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| 1     | `alpine-unknown-directive`  | Unknown `x-*` directive; bad argument where the set is closed (`x-transition:<stage>`, plugin arguments); `x-on`/`@` with no event; v2 syntax such as `x-spread` with an upgrade hint. Option `extend-directives`                                                                                                                                                                                               | correctness | generated + overlay                       |
| 1     | `alpine-invalid-modifier`   | Unknown modifier per directive; key names on non-key events; valued-modifier formats (`.debounce.1s` is invalid, `.debounce.10ms` is fine); `.away` → `.outside`. Option `extend-modifiers`                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | correctness | generated + overlay + UI Events key names |
| 1     | `alpine-template-directive` | `x-for`/`x-if`/`x-teleport` off `<template>`; a template with ≠ 1 root element, counted per Django branch and skipped when it contains `{% for %}`; `x-html` on a template; `:key` not on the `<template x-for>`                                                                                                                                                                                                | correctness | overlay (3 names)                         |
| 1     | `htmx-unknown-attribute`    | Unknown `hx-*`/`data-hx-*` name after stripping the prefix and suffixes; suffix misuse or wrong order; htmx 2 names under v4, with rename hints; dead v2 `hx-on` forms under v4. Option `extend-attributes`                                                                                                                                                                                                     | correctness | generated (web-types, upgrade-check)      |
| 1     | `mixed-case-event-name`     | Uppercase letters in `x-on`/`@`/`hx-on` event names, in Alpine key modifiers, and in `x-bind` arguments on SVG                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | suspicious  | none                                      |
| 2     | `htmx-invalid-value`        | `hx-swap` style, modifiers and case (option `extra-swap-styles`); `hx-sync`; `hx-method` (v4); `hx-encoding`; exact `true`/`false` and bare forms of `hx-boost`/`hx-validate`/`hx-push-url`/`hx-replace-url`; strict JSON when a value starts with `{`; leading whitespace on untrimmed attributes; `js: {`. Skip any token containing template syntax. `hx-trigger` comes later, because it needs an HCON port | correctness | hand-written + drift test                 |
| 2     | `alpine-invalid-value`      | `x-for` without `in`/`of`; `x-id` that is not an array literal; the `x-transition` class form with no stage; Async Alpine strategy names (when enabled)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | correctness | hand-written                              |
| 3     | `unsafe-js-attr-value`      | Template values in JS-evaluated attributes, judged by position and last filter; always report `json_script`, `\|safe`/`\|safeseq`, `{% csrf_token %}` and direct `{% translate %}` output there (§C "Rule design recommendation"). Option `safe-filters`, which extends the defaults                                                                                                                            | suspicious  | hand-written context table                |
| 3     | extend `javascript-url`     | `js:`/`javascript:` in htmx 4 URL attributes (`hx-get` … `hx-action`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | suspicious  | hand-written                              |

- **Deferred or opt-in:**
  - a missing `:key` on `x-for`. The requester wants it on by default, but Alpine only recommends it, so it would go in `pedantic` or behind an option;
  - an Alpine CSP-build mode. Banning `x-html` and directives on `<iframe>`/`<script>` is trivial, but checking the expression subset means porting the roughly 1000-line CSP parser;
  - htmx "missing `:inherited`" heuristics. These use upgrade-check's algorithm and are prone to false positives, because templates extend each other.
- **Fixtures.** Every fixture needs the Django-specific cases:
  - `{% if %}` between attributes;
  - `{% for %}` inside a `<template x-for>`;
  - interpolated names (`x-on:{{ evt }}`);
  - quotes inside `{{ … }}`.

### 3.3 Settings

- **Rule options go under `[tool.djangofmt.lint.<rule>]`**, as with `unsorted-tailwind-classes` (`crates/djangofmt/src/pyproject.rs:197-203`). So the requester's `[tool.djangofmt.lint] safe-js-filters` becomes:

  ```toml
  [tool.djangofmt.lint.unsafe-js-attr-value]
  safe-filters = ["escape_alpine"]  # extends the defaults (escapejs, length, …)
  ```

- **Framework facts are shared by several rules**: the htmx version, prefixes, and loaded extensions and plugins. They are a property of the project, not of one rule.
  - `settings.rs` notes "djangofmt has no plugin grouping to key it by" (`crates/djangofmt_lint/src/settings.rs:8`). So one shared table per framework would be a new concept.
  - The alternative is to repeat these options in every rule. The shared tables below are the recommended shape:

  ```toml
  [tool.djangofmt.lint.htmx]
  version = "4"                  # "2" | "4"
  extensions = ["sse", "ws"]     # gates extension attributes and swap styles
  extra-attributes = ["hx-container"]

  [tool.djangofmt.lint.alpine]
  plugins = ["intersect", "focus", "collapse", "mask", "anchor", "sort", "resize"]
  extend-directives = ["load", "load-src"]  # Async Alpine, in-house directives
  csp = false
  ```

- **htmx version default.**
  - Recommended: copy `target-version`'s behaviour, where "Rules that depend on it stay disabled until it is known" (`pyproject.rs:153`). Unset, the name rule accepts the union of v2 and v4 names, and version-specific checks stay off.
  - Defaulting to either version would be wrong for many projects. npm `latest` stays 2.x until early 2027 while four.htmx.org documents 4 (§B "Status and where things live"), and real templates use v2 names (§4).
  - Inference is weak. django-htmx serves both versions from one package, and the only signal inside a template is `{% htmx_script version=4 %}` ([django-htmx 1.28.0 changelog L5-L16](https://github.com/adamchainz/django-htmx/blob/a94f71d6df93506235be334d48bba20f32f517e1/docs/changelog.rst#L5-L16)).

### 3.4 Data: generate what upstream publishes, hand-write the rest

| Table                                                                                                                     | Pinned source                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | How                                                                                         | Licence             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| htmx names, name patterns and events (2.x and 4.x)                                                                        | `htmx.web-types.json` in the `htmx.org` npm tarball (4.0.0 `dist/editors/jetbrains/`, 2.0.11 `editors/jetbrains/`). htmx's CI checks it against the docs (§B "Machine-readable sources")                                                              | generate                                                                                    | 0BSD                |
| htmx 2 → 4 renames and removals                                                                                           | the dict literals in `upgrade-check.py`, shipped in `dist/scripts/` in 4.0.0                                                                                                                                                                          | generate                                                                                    | 0BSD                |
| htmx value grammars                                                                                                       | Hand-written. Seed from markuplint 4.18.3's deleted `htmx-parser/src/spec.ts` (§D.1) and the htmx source. The docs cannot serve as a source: they disagree with the source or web-types in 16 places (§B "Where docs, source and web-types disagree") | hand-written + a drift test over the pinned `htmx.js` (swap styles, verbs, sync strategies) | –                   |
| Alpine directives, arguments and modifiers (core and plugins)                                                             | Alpine's `src/`, shipped in every `@alpinejs/*` tarball: `directive('…')` calls, `modifiers.includes('…')`, and `nonKeyModifiers` in `on.js`. A 25-line regex prototype recovered all 34 directive names (§A "Machine-readable sources")              | generate + overlay                                                                          | MIT                 |
| Alpine value kinds, template-only directives, valued-modifier grammars, transition stages, anchor placements, key aliases | Not expressed anywhere in the source                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | hand-written overlay                                                                        | –                   |
| Key names for `x-on` key modifiers                                                                                        | W3C UI Events key values                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | generate (scrape the spec)                                                                  | W3C                 |
| Async Alpine                                                                                                              | 2 attributes, 5 strategy names                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | hand-written                                                                                | Apache-2.0 upstream |
| HTML elements, attributes, booleans and enums (future `djangofmt_html_spec`, #425, `invalid-attr-value`)                  | `@markuplint/html-spec` `index.json`. Merge its categories (about 40 lines) and drop the MDN descriptions, which are CC-BY-SA (§D.3, §D.6)                                                                                                            | generate                                                                                    | MIT                 |

- **Generator shape.**
  - A `djangofmt_dev` subcommand downloads the pinned tarballs from registry.npmjs.org and emits checked-in Rust tables, each with a header naming its source and version.
  - A `just` recipe runs it.
  - CI fails when regenerating changes anything, as with markuplint's idempotency check (§D.4). It can also warn when a newer upstream release exists, as htmx-django-intellisense's `check-pins` does (§E).
- **Checked in, not `build.rs`.** Builds stay offline and diffs stay reviewable.

## 4. Real-world usage (Sourcegraph sample)

**Method.** Queries went through the Sourcegraph stream API on 2026-09-23.

- They matched `file:templates/.*\.html$` in repositories with a `manage.py` (`repo:has.file(path:manage\.py$)`), using regexp patterns.
- Results were capped at 3,000–5,000 matches per query.
- Names and `{{ }}` expressions were extracted with regexes, so treat the numbers as indicative.

**Template values in JS-evaluated attributes:**

| Attributes                                                                                                                             | Repos | Files | `{{ }}` values | With no filter |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- | -------------- | -------------- |
| Alpine JS directives (`x-data`, `x-init`, `x-show`, `x-text`, `x-html`, `x-model`, `x-if`, `x-effect`, `x-on:*`/`@*`, `x-bind:*`/`:*`) | 56    | 319   | 1,050          | 818 (78%)      |
| `hx-vals`                                                                                                                              | 34    | 192   | 679            | 352 (52%)      |
| HTML `on*` handlers                                                                                                                    | 359   | 1,267 | 3,752          | 3,581 (95%)    |

- **The 818 unfiltered Alpine values:**
  - 299 look numeric (`.pk`, `.id`, counts);
  - 275 sit inside a quoted JS string;
  - 244 are bare, e.g. `x-data="{{ state_var }}"` or `@click="{{ action }}"`.
- **The most common last filters in Alpine attributes:** `escapejs` (73), `tojson` (46, from Jinja projects), `yesno` (23), `slugify` (16), `default` (10), `date` (8).
- **In `hx-vals` the most common filter is `default` (237)**, which is not a sanitiser (§C).
- **What this means for the rule.** A plain filter allowlist would flag about four in five real values. Two things make it usable:
  - judging each value by position;
  - the `|stringformat:"d"` hint for ids.

  Preview is mandatory.

**Attribute names in use:**

- **`x-*` (2,937 occurrences, 23 names):** these are almost all Alpine core:
  - text 874, show 847, transition 278, model 220, data 169, for 126, if 89, cloak 86, bind 79;
  - ref 43, on 35, init 25, html 19, teleport 4, trap 4, effect 3, resize 1.

  The names that are not Alpine: `x-axis` 23 (2 repos, unidentified), `x-placement` 7 (Popper.js v1, 4 repos), `x-editable` 2, `x-webkit-speech` 1, `x-webkit-airplay` 1, `x-axiscrepresents` 1.
- **`hx-*` (2,999 occurrences, 28 names):** these are htmx 1/2 names:
  - target 623, get 591, swap 507, post 291, trigger 249, indicator 118, push-url 113, swap-oob 90, include 87;
  - vals 74, boost 49, delete 44, on 32, headers 32, confirm 31, select 22, replace-url 7, preserve 5, encoding 5;
  - v2-only names: `disabled-elt` 12, `ext` 5, `params` 4;
  - custom or extension names: `hx-container` 3, `hx-loading` 1, `hx-post-streaming` 1, `hx-target-4xx` 1 (the response-targets extension).
- **What this means for the name rules.**
  - False positives come from foreign `x-*` attributes and from custom or extension `hx-*` names. The `extend-*` options are needed from day one.
  - Tables with v4 names only would flag v2 names that are still correct for most projects. Hence the version setting, with the union as the default.

## 5. Reuse verdict

| Candidate                                                                                                          | Reuse?                                     | Why                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `@markuplint/html-spec` data                                                                                       | **Yes**, for the HTML side                 | Pinned, MIT, and self-contained apart from the category merge. Its 32 boolean names match WHATWG's 30 plus 2. It has 41 enums, which could replace the hard-coded `form[method]` (§D.3, §D.6) |
| markuplint's name-resolution design                                                                                | **Yes**, with the three departures in §3.1 | Maps `:href` → `href`, so `:hreff` is still caught, and marks `@click` as an event directive (§D.2)                                                                                           |
| `@markuplint/alpine-spec` and `@markuplint/htmx-spec`                                                              | **No**                                     | Hand-written; no Alpine plugins, no Async Alpine, no htmx 4 names or suffixes; no modifier or value checks; v5 regressions, and `hx-boost="true"` becomes a false positive (§D.1)             |
| markuplint 4.18.3 `htmx-parser/src/spec.ts`                                                                        | **Seed only**                              | The richest MIT htmx value grammars (`hx-vals` as JSON or `js:`, selector-or-keyword targets, `true`/`false` enums), written for htmx 2 (§D.1)                                                |
| markuplint v6 Rust crates (`markuplint-types`)                                                                     | **Watch**                                  | Rust URL, BCP47, MIME, JSON and CSS checkers, but unreleased at version 0.0.0 (§D.6)                                                                                                          |
| htmx web-types and `upgrade-check.py`                                                                              | **Yes**                                    | First-party, pinned, 0BSD, and in 4.x checked by htmx's own CI (§B, §E)                                                                                                                       |
| Biome's Vue rules (`noVueUndeclaredDirectives` with `globals`, `useVueValidVOn` with `modifiers`, `useVueVForKey`) | **Design reference**                       | The closest implemented Rust rules and option shapes (§E)                                                                                                                                     |
| Alpine.js Toolkit (VS Code)                                                                                        | **Test ideas**                             | The only prior art for the template placement and one-root checks (§E)                                                                                                                        |
| htmx-django-intellisense                                                                                           | **Precedent**                              | Pinned upstream generation plus a `check-pins` CI step (§E)                                                                                                                                   |
| Reading VS Code custom data, web-types or CEM files as user input                                                  | **Not now**                                | Plain `extend-*` lists in pyproject cover third-party libraries. web-types is the only format expressive enough if one is ever needed (§E)                                                    |

One piece of history: UnknownPlatypus's Datastar spec PR for markuplint (#3353) was closed, and the markuplint maintainer advised external packages for fast-moving frameworks (§D.5). That is one more reason for djangofmt to own these tables.

## 6. Corrections worth sharing on the issue

- **`escapejs` and the CSP build.** It works with `@alpinejs/csp` 3.17.0 and later, released 2026-08-30 ([PR #4881](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/pull/4881)). Up to 3.16.3, the CSP parser dropped the backslash, so `escapejs`'s `'` displayed as `u0027`. A custom `escape_alpine` filter is only needed on those older builds (§A "CSP build").
- **`escapejs` is not enough on its own.** It protects only inside a quoted JS string, not in bare expression position or in template literals (§C "Results by position").
- **`json_script` never belongs in an attribute.** It renders a `<script>` element, and its own docstring says it is safe "anywhere except for inside a tag attribute" (§C).
- **`unlocalize` escapes nothing.** Listing it only asserts that the value is numeric (§C).
- **`hx-vals` in htmx 4.** Brace-less HCON is valid; only a value starting with `{` must be strict JSON (§B "The requester's claims").
- **The one-root rule** applies only to a `<template>` carrying `x-for`, `x-if` or `x-teleport`. **`<hx-partial>`** also exists in htmx 2.0.11 (§A, §B).
- **The option belongs under `[tool.djangofmt.lint.<rule>]`**, not directly under `[tool.djangofmt.lint]` (§3.3).

## 7. Open questions for the maintainer

1. **Shared framework tables.** Should there be `[tool.djangofmt.lint.htmx]` and `[tool.djangofmt.lint.alpine]` tables, a new concept, or per-rule options only?
2. **htmx version when unset.** The options are the union of v2 and v4 (recommended), v2, or v4.
3. **Rule granularity and names.** Should rules come in `alpine-*`/`htmx-*` families? Should the case rule span both frameworks?
4. **When Alpine checks apply.** Should they always run, scoped to the prefix, or only once Alpine is enabled?
   - Vue in-DOM templates share `@`/`:`, so Vue modifiers such as `.native`/`.exact` would be flagged as unknown Alpine modifiers.
   - Excluding the rule with `ignore` may be enough.
5. **Where the tables live.** In `djangofmt_lint` now, or start `djangofmt_html_spec` with framework dialect modules, as `invalid_attr_value.rs` foreshadows?
6. **Security rule scope.** HTML `on*` handlers are the same class of bug but far noisier: 3,752 values in 359 repos, 95% of them unfiltered. Should they be included, perhaps behind an option? Should `{% url %}` with variable arguments stay a documented false negative?
7. **Alpine CSP mode.** Is it worth porting a JavaScript-subset parser for it?

---

# Appendices: detailed findings

## A. Alpine.js

Versions examined (2026-09-23): `alpinejs` and every official `@alpinejs/*` package at **3.17.4** (npm, published 2026-09-21; GitHub release [v3.17.4](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/releases/tag/v3.17.4)). Source read at tag `v3.17.4` = commit `20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d` (all `al-*` links below are pinned to it). Also: `async-alpine` **2.0.3** (npm, 2025-08-25; repo `main` @ `5df98f0`, no `src/` or `dist/` change since tag `2.0.3`, checked via the compare API), `@types/alpinejs` 3.13.11, `@markuplint/alpine-spec` 5.0.1.

Runtime claims marked **(jsdom)** were checked by running the published CDN builds (`alpinejs@3.17.4`, `@alpinejs/csp@3.17.4`, `async-alpine@2.0.3`) in jsdom 30.1.1 on Node 26.4.0. The harness was a throwaway script and is not kept. jsdom has no layout engine and no IntersectionObserver, so only non-layout behaviour was tested.

### Version and repository layout

- The monorepo is `packages/<name>/{src,builds}`, and every package is released in lockstep at 3.17.4. Core lives in `packages/alpinejs`: one file per directive in `src/directives/x-*.js`, registered from [`src/directives/index.js`][al-idx], plus `src/magics/$*.js` and `src/utils/`. The CDN build auto-starts on a microtask ([builds/cdn.js][al-cdn]).
- Plugin packages: `anchor`, `collapse`, `focus`, `intersect`, `mask`, `morph`, `persist`, `resize`, `sort`, `ui`.
- `csp` is an alternate build. It reuses `alpinejs/src`, swaps in a new evaluator and overrides `x-html` ([csp/src/index.js][al-csp-idx]).
- `docs` holds the markdown behind alpinejs.dev. It is also published to npm as `@alpinejs/docs@3.17.4`.
- `navigate` and `history` are one-line stubs ("This plugin has been moved into the livewire/livewire repository", [navigate/src/index.js][al-nav]) and are not published to npm.
- Tests live in `tests/cypress/integration/**` and `tests/vitest/` (including `csp-parser.spec.js`).
- Every official `@alpinejs/*` npm tarball ships `src/` (checked for `alpinejs`, `csp`, `focus`, `ui`, `sort`, `mask`, `intersect`, `collapse`, `anchor`, `resize`, `persist`, `morph`). A generator can therefore read pinned source straight from the registry. `async-alpine` ships only `dist/` (unminified `async-alpine.esm.js`).
- License: MIT (Alpine), Apache-2.0 (Async Alpine).
- The alpinejs.dev home page advertises "15 attributes, 6 properties, and 2 methods". Its directive list leaves out `x-modelable`, `x-id` and `x-teleport`, so it is **not** the complete set. The complete set is the 18 files in `src/directives/` ([alpinejs.dev](https://alpinejs.dev/), fetched 2026-09-23).

### Attribute-name grammar (as Alpine parses it)

Parsing happens in [`directives.js`][al-dirs-177], applied to every attribute of every element under an `[x-data]` or `[x-init]` root ([lifecycle.js][al-life]):

1. **Shorthand rewrite.** `mapAttributes(startingWith('@', into(prefix('on:'))))` rewrites `@` to `x-on:` ([x-on.js L6][al-on]). `mapAttributes(startingWith(':', into(prefix('bind:'))))` rewrites `:` to `x-bind:` ([x-bind.js L7][al-bind]).
   - The target is computed **at import time**, while the prefix is still `x-`. With `Alpine.prefix('data-x-')` the shorthands therefore stop working: in a (jsdom) run, `@click` did not fire while `data-x-on:click` did.
   - The only upstream test for a custom prefix covers `data-x-data` and `data-x-text` (`tests/cypress/integration/custom-prefix.spec.js`).
2. **Prefix filter.** An attribute is Alpine's only if it matches `new RegExp(`^${prefixAsString}([^:^.]+)\\b`)`. The prefix defaults to `x-` and is changed by `Alpine.prefix()` ([L6-14][al-dirs-6], [L177-181][al-dirs-177]).
   - The directive **type** is everything up to the first `:` or `.`. So `x-load-src` has type `load-src`, not `load`, and `x-on-click` has type `on-click` (unknown).
3. **Argument (`value`)** is `name.match(/:([a-zA-Z0-9\-_:]+)/)`, i.e. the text after the first colon. It may contain `-`, `_`, digits and more colons, so `@htmx:after-request` gives the event `htmx:after-request`. A `.` or any other character ends it.
4. **Modifiers** come from `name.match(/\.[^.\]]+(?=[^\]]*$)/g)`, i.e. every `.segment`. Segments followed later in the name by a `]` are skipped; upstream's rationale for that is **unverified** and no test covers it.
   - **Valued modifiers are not a grammar feature.** They are simply the *next* modifier in the list, read positionally by each directive, e.g. `modifiers[modifiers.indexOf('debounce')+1]` ([on.js L92-106][al-onjs-db]).
5. `if (name === value) value = ''` normalises backend-rendered boolean attributes such as `x-on:click.stop="x-on:click.stop"` ([L187][al-dirs-177]).
6. Directives run in the fixed order `ignore, ref, id, data, anchor, bind, init, for, model, modelable, transition, show, if, DEFAULT, teleport` ([L206-222][al-dirs-206]).
7. **The HTML tokenizer lowercases attribute names before Alpine sees them.** The spec says "ASCII upper alpha: Append the lowercase version of the current input character … to the current attribute's name" ([HTML §13.2.5.33][html-attrname]).
   - SVG foreign content only re-cases exact names from a fixed table such as `viewbox` → `viewBox` ([adjust SVG attributes][html-svg]), so `:viewBox` stays `:viewbox`.
   - A later attribute with the same (lowercased) name is dropped as a `duplicate-attribute` parse error.
   - djangofmt sees the author's original casing, so it can flag these before the browser rewrites them.

### Unknown directives at runtime

An unknown `x-foo` is **silently ignored**: `let handler = directiveHandlers[directive.type] || noop` ([directives.js L130][al-dirs-127]). (jsdom): `x-cheeseburger` and the removed-in-v3 `x-spread` produced no console output. The only feedback Alpine gives for directive names:

- `x-collapse`, `x-intersect`, `x-trap` and `x-mask` have placeholder handlers that `warn("You can't use [x-…] without first installing the "…" plugin")` ([directives/index.js L23-31][al-idx]). `$focus` and `$persist` have the magic equivalents ([magics/index.js L14-20][al-magics]).
- On `start()`, a one-off check warns `found "[x-anchor]", but missing anchor plugin` for `[x-dialog], [x-popover]`, `[x-anchor]` and `[x-sort]` ([lifecycle.js L128-146][al-life-miss]).
- Everything else (resize, the other ui directives, typos, third-party plugins that are not loaded) produces nothing. Directives on elements outside any `x-data`/`x-init` subtree are never even parsed.

So "unknown `x-*` attribute" is a real dead-code or typo bug that Alpine never reports. The allowlist must be extensible, though: third-party plugins (e.g. Async Alpine's `x-load`/`x-load-src`), project `Alpine.directive()` calls, and non-Alpine vendor `x-*` attributes (e.g. `x-webkit-airplay`, `x-apple-data-detectors`; unverified examples) all use the prefix.

**Uncaught errors abort page initialisation.** Directive handlers run without a try/catch ([directives.js L83-103, L136-144][al-dirs-83]); only expression evaluation is wrapped, by `tryCatch` → `handleError` ([evaluator.js][al-eval], [error.js][al-err]).

- A TypeError thrown directly in a handler stops the remaining handlers **and every later root on the page** from initialising. In (jsdom) runs, a following `<p x-data x-text="'next root ok'">` stayed empty after each case marked "aborts init" below.
- Errors raised inside an expression callback are only logged as `Alpine Expression Error: …` and rethrown asynchronously; the page keeps working.

#### x-on modifiers and event names ([utils/on.js][al-onjs])

- **Closed set**, cross-checked against the `nonKeyModifiers` array ([L134-145][al-onjs-nonkey]): `prevent`, `stop`, `once`, `self`, `capture`, `window`, `document`, `outside`, `away`, `passive` (with an optional `.false`), `camel`, `dot`, `debounce[.N(ms)]`, `throttle[.N(ms)]`.
  - `away` is v2 and deprecated: the upgrade guide says "`.away` should be replaced with `.outside`" ([upgrade-guide L314-327][al-doc-upg-away]).
  - `preserve-scroll` is in the array but is "specifically for Livewire and is not used by Alpine".
- **Key names (open set)** apply only when the event is exactly `keydown` or `keyup` (`isKeyEvent`). Any `KeyboardEvent.key` value, kebab-cased, is accepted ([docs L71][al-doc-onkeys]).
  - Aliases: `ctrl slash space spacebar cmd esc up down left right period comma equal minus underscore` ([keyToModifiers L200-228][al-onjs-map]).
  - System keys: `ctrl shift alt meta cmd super`.
  - The named-key universe is the ~284 values in the W3C Recommendation [UI Events KeyboardEvent key Values (2025-04-22)][uievents], plus any single printable character.
- **Unknown modifiers are not inert.** Every modifier outside the lists above is treated as a key filter by `isListeningForASpecificKeyThatHasntBeenPressed` ([L147-198][al-onjs-keys]) whenever the event is `keydown`/`keyup` or its name *contains* `click`, `mouse` or `contextmenu` (`isClickEvent`, a substring test). (jsdom):
  - `@click.prevnt`, `@click.foo.prevent`, `@mouseenter.typo` and `@myclick.foo` (a custom event) **never fire**.
  - `@input.foo` fires (the modifier is ignored).
  - `@keypress.enter` fires for *any* key, because key modifiers are ignored on `keypress`.
  - `@keyup.enter.debounce.1s` never fires. `1s` is not `N` or `Nms`, so it falls through as a key filter; `.debounce.10ms` fires.
- **Case.** The docs say "`x-on` can only listen for events with lower case names … use the `.camel` helper" ([on.md L16][al-doc-on16]). `.camel` lowercases and then camelises `a-b` → `aB`; `.dot` turns `-` into `.` ([L108-114][al-onjs]). (jsdom):
  - `@myEvent` became the attribute `@myevent` and missed a `myEvent` CustomEvent; `@my-event.camel` caught it.
  - `@keydown.pageDown` (lowercased to `pagedown`) missed PageDown; `.page-down` worked.
  - `:viewBox` set `viewbox` on `<svg>`; `x-bind:view-box.camel` set `viewBox`.
- (jsdom): `x-on="…"` with no event arg throws `Cannot read properties of null (reading 'includes')` and **aborts init**.

#### Docs vs source mismatches (3.17.4)

- **Accepted by source but undocumented:**
  - x-on `.away` (upgrade guide only);
  - x-model `.trim`, `.unintrusive`, `.parent`;
  - x-show `.immediate`;
  - x-ignore `.self`;
  - x-teleport `.prepend`/`.append`;
  - x-bind `.camel`;
  - x-transition `.in`/`.out` (upgrade-guide and test usage only);
  - x-mask `:function` (alias of `:dynamic`);
  - x-sort `:key` (backward-compat alias of `:item`) and `.group.<name>`.
- **Documented but wrong** ([docs/advanced/csp.md L123-124][al-doc-csp]): the CSP docs list "Property assignments: `user.name = 'John'`" as unsupported. The parser has supported member assignment (except on DOM objects) since v3.15.12 ([PR #4807][pr4807], [parser L893-912][al-csp-assign]).
- **On the docs site:** x-on's click-modifier list says "`context`" where the code matches `contextmenu`.
