| `@alpinejs/sort`                                                                                           | `x-sort` (JS handler with `$item`/`$key`/`$position`), `:item`/`:key` (JS), `:config` (JS object), `:group` (**plain string**), `:handle` (none), `:ignore` (none; read by selector, documented)                                                                                                                                                                                      | `ghost`, `group.<name>`                                                                                            | see args                                                                                              | [L5-45, L65-76, L94-99, L175-186][al-sort]                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `@alpinejs/resize`                                                                                         | `x-resize`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `viewport`, `document`                                                                                             | JS with `$width`/`$height`                                                                            | [L2-20][al-resize]                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `@alpinejs/persist`                                                                                        | no directive; `$persist` magic (`.as()`, `.using()`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | –                                                                                                                  | –                                                                                                     | [L40-47][al-persist]                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `@alpinejs/morph`                                                                                          | no directive; `Alpine.morph()` reads the plain `key` attribute (`:key` is swallowed by x-bind, see below)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | –                                                                                                                  | –                                                                                                     | [morph.js L69][al-morph]                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `@alpinejs/ui` (MIT on npm; docs sit behind the paid [Alpine Components](https://alpinejs.dev/components)) | `x-combobox[:input\|button\|label\|options\|option]`, `x-dialog[:overlay\|panel\|title\|description]`, `x-disclosure[:button\|panel]`, `x-listbox[:label\|button\|options\|option]`, `x-menu[:button\|items\|item]`, `x-popover[:button\|panel\|overlay\|group]`, `x-radio[:option\|label\|description]`, `x-switch[:group\|label\|description]`, `x-tabs[:list\|tab\|panels\|panel]` | –                                                                                                                  | expression unused                                                                                     | [combobox][al-ui-combobox] [dialog][al-ui-dialog] [disclosure][al-ui-disclosure] [listbox][al-ui-listbox] [menu][al-ui-menu] [popover][al-ui-popover] [radio][al-ui-radio] [switch][al-ui-switch] [tabs][al-ui-tabs] |

UI arg typos behave differently per directive. For combobox, dialog and switch, an unknown arg falls through to `handleRoot`, so `x-dialog:panel` becomes a second dialog root. For the others an unknown arg is ignored. The sort plugin hard-codes `x-` in its selectors (`[x-sort\\:handle]`, `x-sort:config`), and so do combobox/listbox (`x-combobox:option`), so these ignore a custom prefix.

### Structural rules, verified

| Rule                                                      | Source behaviour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Result (jsdom)                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Static?                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `x-for` must be on `<template>`                           | Docs: "`x-for` MUST be declared on a `<template>` element" ([for.md L52-55][al-doc-for]). Source has **no** tag check: `templateEl.content.children` ([x-for.js L115][al-for-root])                                                                                                                 | `Alpine Expression Error: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'children')`, and the children then fail with `i is not defined`. Nothing renders; the error is caught and the rest of the page continues | Yes (tag name)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `x-if` must be on `<template>`                            | `warn('x-if can only be used on a <template> tag')`, then `el.content.cloneNode` ([x-if.js L10, L17][al-if])                                                                                                                                                                                        | warning, then a caught `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'cloneNode')`                                                                                                                               | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `x-teleport` must be on `<template>`                      | same warning ([x-teleport.js L9, L13][al-tele])                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | warning, then an **uncaught** TypeError that aborts init                                                                                                                                                         | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `<template x-for>` needs exactly one root **element**     | `if (templateEl.content.children.length > 1) warn('x-for templates require a single root element, additional elements will be ignored.')`, then `.firstElementChild` ([L115-118][al-for-root])                                                                                                      | 2 elements: warning plus only the first is rendered. 0 elements (text only): `Cannot set properties of null (setting '_x_dataStack')`. Text and comments around a single root are silently dropped               | Yes. Count element children per Django branch (`{% for %}` inside the template multiplies roots)                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `<template x-if>`: one root                               | `.firstElementChild`, **no warning** ([x-if.js L17][al-if])                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | 2 elements: only the first rendered, silently. 0: TypeError when the condition is truthy                                                                                                                         | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `<template x-teleport>`: one root                         | `.firstElementChild`, no warning ([x-teleport.js L13][al-tele])                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | 2: only the first is teleported, silently. 0: TypeError at init (source reading)                                                                                                                                 | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| "`<template>` can only have one root" (as in the request) | **Imprecise.** It only applies to `<template>` elements carrying `x-for`, `x-if` or `x-teleport`. Plain templates are unconstrained                                                                                                                                                                 | –                                                                                                                                                                                                                | –                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `x-html` on `<template>`                                  | `el.innerHTML = value` ([x-html.js L12][al-html]). For a template, innerHTML "set[s] target to the template element's template contents" ([HTML innerHTML setter][html-inner]), which is inert (`template { display: none }` in the [UA stylesheet][html-hidden]). `initTree(el)` finds no children | Content lands in `template.content`; nothing is rendered and there is no error. `x-text`/`x-show` on a template are the same kind of no-op (source/spec reading)                                                 | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `x-for` without `:key`                                    | Not required. The default key is the index: `el._x_keyExpression \|\| 'index'` ([x-for.js L14-17][al-for]). Docs: "important to specify unique keys … if you are going to be re-ordering items" ([for.md L57-74][al-doc-for])                                                                       | After `items.shift()`, per-row state stays tied to the index: the unkeyed list shows `a,b`, the keyed one `b,c`                                                                                                  | Yes, but it is a **recommendation**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `:key` on the loop child instead of the template          | `if (value === 'key') return storeKeyForXFor(el, expression)` ([x-bind.js L23][al-bind]) stores the key on whichever element carries it, and never sets a DOM attribute                                                                                                                             | Behaves as if unkeyed (`a,b`)                                                                                                                                                                                    | Yes. A Vue habit and a **real bug**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `:key` evaluates to an object                             | `warn('x-for key cannot be an object …')` ([L75][al-for-loop])                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | warning                                                                                                                                                                                                          | No (runtime value)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Capital letters in `x-on` event names                     | HTML lowercases names; see the grammar subsection and [on.md L16][al-doc-on16]                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `@myEvent` never matches `myEvent`. `@Click` still works, because `click` is lowercase anyway                                                                                                                    | Yes. It is a bug for camelCase event names (custom events, htmx 2 `htmx:afterSwap`) and misleading style otherwise. The same applies to multi-word key modifiers (`.pageDown`) and to `x-bind` args on SVG/MathML (`:viewBox`). On HTML elements attribute names are case-insensitive |
| `x-model` on non-form elements                            | Legitimate. Docs: "instances where you want to allow `x-model` on a non-input element", shown with `<div x-model>` and `_x_model` ([model.md L440-455][al-doc-model-prog]). Also `x-modelable` and custom elements (`ui-checkbox`, …)                                                               | –                                                                                                                                                                                                                | **Not a bug**; not decidable statically                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

#### Other runtime failures a static checker can catch

- **`x-transition="classes"` with no stage, or with a stage outside the six names.** The code calls `directiveStorageMap[stage](classString)` ([L18-31][al-trans]). (jsdom): **uncaught** `directiveStorageMap[stage] is not a function`, which **aborts init** of later components. The literal `x-transition="false"` also takes this path (source reading).
- **`x-for` value without `in`/`of`.** (jsdom): uncaught `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'items')`, which **aborts init**.
- **`x-id` whose value is not an array.** (jsdom): uncaught `names.forEach is not a function`, which **aborts init**. A static check is only heuristic (require `[…]`).
- **`x-on`/`@` without an event name.** Uncaught, aborts init (see above).
- **v2 syntax that is silently dead in v3:** `x-spread` (now `x-bind="obj"`), `x-show.transition…` and `x-if.transition` (now `x-transition`), and binding `:x-ref` ([upgrade guide][al-doc-upg]).
- **CSP build:**
  - `x-html` anywhere → `Using the x-html directive is prohibited in the CSP build` ([csp x-html.js][al-csp-html]; jsdom).
  - Any evaluated directive on `<iframe>` or `<script>` throws ([csp evaluator.js L50-57][al-csp-eval]).
  - Unsupported expression syntax (next section).
- **Official plugin directive used but the plugin is not installed.** Only collapse, intersect, trap, mask, anchor, sort, dialog and popover warn at runtime; the rest are silent. This needs a `plugins = [...]` config.

### Async Alpine (2.0.3)

- **Attributes.** It registers one directive, `load`, with `.before('ignore')`, and reads the attribute `x-load-src` via `Alpine.prefixed('load-src')` ([async-alpine.js L9-15, L108-110][aa-main]). The inline handler immediately sets `x-ignore` and `_x_ignore` on the element ([L78-86][aa-main-sync]).
  - `activate()` removes `x-ignore` and re-inits, but skips re-init if an ancestor still has `[x-ignore]` ([L161-172][aa-act]). An `x-load` element inside an `x-ignore` subtree is never processed. On activation, a user-authored `x-ignore` on the same element is removed together with the plugin's own (source reading, not run).
- **Component name** is taken from the same element's `x-data`: `split(/[({]/)[0]`. An inline object or a missing `x-data` gives a synthetic `_x_async_N` name and no download ([L118-132, L184-190][aa-prep]).
- **`x-load-src`** is only read for `x-load` elements. (jsdom): without `x-load` it is inert.
- **Strategy grammar** (the `x-load` value; default `eager`, [L12-15][aa-main]; [docs](https://async-alpine.dev/docs/strategies/)):
  - methods `eager`, `idle`, `visible[(rootMargin)]`, `media(<query>)` (wrapped as `matchMedia(`(${arg})`)`) and `event[(name)]` ([strategies.js L75-81][aa-strat]);
  - operators `&&`, `||`, and a legacy `|` meaning AND;
  - parentheses for grouping, but the argument itself cannot contain parentheses. The tokenizer regex is `/\s*([()])\s*|\s*(\|\||&&|\|)\s*|\s*((?:[^()&|]+\([^()]+\))|[^()&|]+)\s*/g` ([requirements.js L62-108][aa-tok]).
- **Unknown strategy names count as already met.** `if (!strategies[requirements.method]) return false` ([L31][aa-req]). (jsdom): the typo `x-load="visible"` loaded immediately.
  - The intended `immediate` → `eager` alias is dead code: it tests `token.method` on a string ([L98-101][aa-tok]). `immediate` still "works" only through this same fallback.
- **Docs** mention only `x-load` and `x-load-src` ([usage](https://async-alpine.dev/docs/usage/)) and do not mention the `x-ignore` interplay.
- **Core alternative:** Alpine 3.17.0 added `Alpine.deferInit(el, promise)` as a JS API with no attribute ([extending.md L385][al-doc-ext]).

### CSP build (`@alpinejs/csp` 3.17.4) and Django's `escapejs`

- **Evaluator.** The standard build compiles every expression to `new AsyncFunction(["__self","scope"], "with (scope) { __self.result = ${expr} } …")`, i.e. full JS ([evaluator.js L94-97][al-eval]). The CSP build replaces this with a hand-written tokenizer, parser and tree-walking evaluator ([parser.js][al-csp-str]).
- **What the CSP parser accepts** (source, confirmed by running the parser):
  - a **single** expression with an optional trailing `;` ([L240-253][al-csp-parse]);
  - literals: decimal numbers only (`0x10`, `1e3` and `.5` are rejected); strings in `'` or `"` only ([L50][al-csp-tok]); `true`, `false`, `null`, `undefined`;
  - identifiers, resolved **only in the Alpine scope** (`Undefined variable: …`, [L704-719][al-csp-ident]);
  - `.`, `[]` and calls;
  - unary `! - +` and `++`/`--`;
  - binary `+ - * / % == != === !== < > <= >= && ||`;
  - ternary `?:`;
  - plain `=` on an identifier or member, with DOM targets refused ([L893-912][al-csp-assign]);
  - array and object literals. Object keys must be followed by `:`, so there are no shorthand properties or methods ([L565-611][al-csp-obj]).
- **What it rejects:** arrow functions, template literals, `??`, `?.`, `typeof`, `new`, `in`, `instanceof`, compound assignment, multiple statements, and spread. It also blocks globals such as `console`, `window` and `JSON`, and the names `constructor`, `__proto__`, `setAttribute`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, … ([L949-996][al-csp-danger]).
  - Silent mis-decodes: `'\x41'` becomes `x41`, and `\b`/`\f` become `b`/`f`. JSON emitted by Python with exponent floats (`1e-05`) fails to parse.
- **`\uXXXX` in string literals is decoded since v3.17.0.** From [readString L154-165][al-csp-str]:
  ```js
  case 'u': {
      const codeUnit = this.input.slice(this.position + 1, this.position + 5);
      if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$/.test(codeUnit)) {
          value += String.fromCharCode(parseInt(codeUnit, 16));
  ```
  - This was added by [PR #4881][pr4881] ("fix(csp): parse Unicode string escapes", merged 2026-08-25, fixing [#4879][is4879], which reported `tes't` → `tesu0027t` "when using laravel's `Js::from()`"). It first shipped in [v3.17.0][rel3170] (2026-08-30; `compare v3.16.3...v3.17.0` lists it).
  - ≤3.16.3 handled only `\n \t \r \\ \<quote>`, and `default: value += char` dropped the backslash ([3.16.3 parser L124-159][al-csp-old]).
- **Django's `escapejs` output.** It emits only `\uXXXX` escapes (upper-case hex), for `\ ' " > < & = - ; `` ` ``, U+2028/2029 and U+0000-001F, 007F-009F ([django/utils/html.py L65-90][dj-js]).
  - I ran a Django-equivalent `escapejs` over `It's <b>"x"</b> a-b; c=d \ `t`⏎newline` and parsed the result. The **3.16.3 parser mangled it** (`Itu0027s u003Cb…`); **3.17.4 round-trips it exactly**. (jsdom) CSP build: `x-data="{ s: 'It's <b>' }"` renders `It's <b>`.
- **Verdict on the request's claim.** "escapejs does not work with the CSP build" was **true for @alpinejs/csp ≤ 3.16.3 and false from 3.17.0**. The requester posted on 2026-09-23, after 3.17.0; they may be pinned to an older build (unverified). A custom `escape_alpine` filter is only needed on ≤3.16.3.
- **Why interpolation needs a JS-level filter at all.** Django autoescape emits `&#x27;` for `'` ([escape() → html.escape][dj-esc]). The HTML parser decodes character references inside attribute values ([§13.2.5.36][html-attrval]).
  - So `x-data="{ s: '{{ v }}' }"` lets `v` close the JS string. (jsdom), standard build: `window.pwned = 1` executed.
  - (jsdom), CSP build: the injected `$dispatch('boom')` fired, because magics are in scope. **The CSP build narrows the impact but does not prevent injection.**
- **Semantics of the "safe" filters in the request:**
  - `escapejs` is only safe "as a whole JavaScript string literal, within single or double quotes" and not in template literals ([Django 6.1 docs][dj-docs]), so the lint must also check that it sits inside a quoted JS string.
  - `json_script` produces a `<script>` element. Its own docstring says it is "safe to be output anywhere except for inside a tag attribute" ([html.py L100-117][dj-json]), so it can never appear in an Alpine attribute. The CSP build also blocks the usual `JSON.parse(document.getElementById(…).textContent)`.
  - `unlocalize` escapes nothing; it is only meaningful for numbers.
  - An autoescaped (not `|safe`) JSON string from a custom `json.dumps` filter, placed in expression position, is actually safe: HTML-escaping handles the attribute layer and JSON the JS layer. This is a false-positive source for a filter-allowlist rule.

### Machine-readable sources, ranked for generating Rust tables

1. **Alpine's own source at a pinned tag or npm tarball.** Most robust. A 25-line regex prototype over `packages/*/src/**/*.js` (`(?:Alpine\.)?directive\('…'`, `modifiers.includes('…')`/`indexOf('…')`, `modifierValue(modifiers,'…'`, `value === '…'`, `[…].includes(value)`) recovered:
   - **all** 18 core + 7 plugin + 9 ui directive names;
   - most args and modifiers.

   Gaps that need a checked-in overlay:
   - x-transition stages (keys of `directiveStorageMap`);
   - intersect `enter`;
   - anchor placements (a `positions` array), transition origin values, intersect `margin`;
   - the x-on system keys and key aliases (arrays in `on.js`);
   - `passive.false` and all valued-modifier value grammars;
   - x-model inheriting every `on()` modifier;
   - `x-sort:ignore` (selector-based);
   - two false positives from `typeof value === '…'` matches.

   Value kinds (JS / selector / class list / name / none) are not expressed anywhere in source and must be hand-maintained. The `nonKeyModifiers` array ([on.js L134-145][al-onjs-nonkey]) is the best single anchor: upstream keeps it as "Every modifier x-on and x-model consume themselves". Recommended: `djangofmt_dev` fetches the tarballs for a given version, regenerates the tables, and prints any new string not covered by the overlay.
2. **Docs markdown** (`packages/docs/src/en/{directives,plugins}/*.md`, also on npm as `@alpinejs/docs`).
   - One file per core directive (18) and per plugin (9).
   - Modifier headings (`### .prevent`, ``### `.lazy` ``, `#### .inert`) are extractable for on, model, collapse, focus, intersect and resize. Show, transition, anchor and sort bury their modifiers in prose or lists.
   - The docs lag the source (see the mismatch list above). Useful for rule docs and for a "documented vs accepted" severity split.
3. **W3C UI Events key values** ([REC 2025-04-22][uievents]): scrape the `td.key-table-key` cells (~284 named keys) and kebab-case them like Alpine's `kebabCase`. This is the only way to validate key modifiers.
4. **markuplint `@markuplint/alpine-spec` 5.0.1** (MIT, [npm][mlspec]): hand-written, core directives only, no plugins, no modifier lists.
5. **inxilpro/IntellijAlpine** (MIT, plugin 0.7.3, [AttributeUtil.kt L12-133][ij]): hand-maintained Kotlin lists that are stale. They lack `dot`, `capture` and `away`; their model modifiers are only `lazy number debounce throttle`; they still list `x-spread`. Not a generation source.
6. **VS Code "Alpine.js IntelliSense"** (AdrianWilczynski/AlpineIntelliSense, MIT, last commit 2020-11-04, v1.2.0, [directives.json][vsc]): Alpine v2 era, missing `x-effect`, `x-teleport`, `x-id`, `x-modelable`, `x-ignore`. Unusable.
7. **TypeScript types.** Alpine has no in-repo `.d.ts`. `@types/alpinejs` 3.13.11 ([npm][types]) types only the JS API (`DirectiveData { type, value, modifiers: string[], expression, original }`), with no directive or modifier lists.
8. **JetBrains/web-types** has no Alpine package (`packages/` and `registry.json` checked via the GitHub API on 2026-09-23).

**Derivable automatically:** directive names, UI and plugin arg names, most modifier names. **Hand-maintained:** value kinds, valued-modifier grammars, which modifiers each directive inherits, template-only directives, deprecations, key names (spec-scraped), and the Async Alpine surface (2 attributes and 5 strategy names).

Give `x-spread` its own message. `@…` and `:…` args are open sets and can't be "unknown"                                                                                                                     |

Not JS: `x-ref`, `x-teleport`, `x-transition:*`, `x-mask`, `x-sort:group`, `x-load`, `x-load-src` |

## B. htmx

Scope: htmx **4.0.0** (tag `v4.0.0` = `4195bc0d`, 2026-08-28; its `src/` is byte-identical to the `four` branch HEAD `c2ecb87a` of 2026-09-22) against htmx **2.0.11** (tag `v2.0.11` = `fa978b24`, 2026-09-22). Code links point at `4195bc0d` (v4) or `fa978b24` (v2). Doc links point at `four` HEAD `c2ecb87a`, the source of https://four.htmx.org. Unreleased `four-dev` (`372c6e32`, 2026-09-17) is mentioned only where it changes behaviour. "Probe" means I ran htmx 4.0.0's own `HCON`, `__parseSwapSpec` and `__parseTriggerSpecs` code, copied verbatim out of `src/htmx.js`, in Node 26 on the value shown. Everything not marked as a probe comes from reading the source.

### htmx 4 attribute inventory

**Core set.** The 36 attribute docs, the 36 core entries in web-types, and the attributes the source reads all agree, and a CI job enforces the docs/web-types part ([check_web_types.py L159-163](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/content/check_web_types.py#L159-L163), [ci.yml L57-61](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/.github/workflows/ci.yml#L57-L61)). Two of the 36, `hx-preload` and `hx-pending`, are documented as core but only work with an extension.

| Group                   | Attributes (value kind)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read at (v4.0.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `:inherited`/`:append`?               |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| Request                 | `hx-get` `hx-post` `hx-put` `hx-patch` `hx-delete` `hx-query` (URL), `hx-action` (URL), `hx-method` (verb)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | [L142](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L142), [L152](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L152), [L328-346](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L328-L346)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | yes (read through `__attributeValue`) |
| Request context         | `hx-swap`, `hx-target`, `hx-select`, `hx-select-oob`, `hx-confirm`, `hx-validate`, `hx-push-url`, `hx-replace-url`, `hx-config`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | [L381-425](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L381-L425)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | yes                                   |
| Data                    | `hx-vals`, `hx-headers`, `hx-include`, `hx-encoding`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | [L487](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L487), [L444-448](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L444-L448), [L1804](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1804), [L535](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L535)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | yes                                   |
| Behaviour               | `hx-trigger` [L725](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L725), `hx-boost` [L948](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L948), `hx-sync` [L679-695](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L679-L695), `hx-indicator` [L1741](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1741), `hx-disable` [L1768](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1768), `hx-status:<code>` [L2261](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2261) | as listed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | yes                                   |
| Scripting               | `hx-on:<evt>`, `hx-on::<evt>`, bare `hx-on`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | [L153](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L153), [L1704-1738](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1704-L1738)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | no: the name is the event             |
| Response-side           | `hx-swap-oob` (read via `__attrName`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | [L1112-1116](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1112-L1116)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | no                                    |
| Presence-only           | `hx-ignore` (`closest('[hx-ignore]')`), `hx-preserve` (needs `id`), `hx-history-elt`, `hx-morph-skip`, `hx-morph-skip-children` (matched through `config.morphSkip*` selectors)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | [L237-240](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L237-L240), [L1018-1024](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1018-L1024), [L1634](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1634), [L199-200](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L199-L200), [L2169](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2169), [L2178](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2178) | no                                    |
| Ext, documented as core | `hx-preload` (preload extension), `hx-pending` (hx-pending extension)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | see the extensions table                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | yes                                   |

Two other names are read by the source but are not in the attribute docs:

- `hx` + `type` on `<template>`, the alternative form of `<hx-partial>` ([L1143-1146](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1143-L1146)).
- `<meta name="htmx-config" content=…>`, which is HCON or JSON ([L207-210](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L207-L210)).

htmx also sets `data-htmx-powered` itself ([L351](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L351)); it is internal state, not a user attribute.

**`<hx-partial>`** only appears in responses.

- **Rewrite step.** Before parsing a response, `__makeFragment` rewrites the raw text: `<hx-([a-z]+)(\s+|>)` becomes `<template hx type="$1"$2` and `</hx-[a-z]+>` becomes `</template>` ([L1046-1047](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1046-L1047)). So every `<hx-foo>` turns into a template. Types other than `partial` go to extensions as `htmx:process:<type>` ([L1162-1164](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1162-L1164)); hx-upsert handles `upsert`.
- **Attributes.** `hx-target`, an extended selector resolved from the triggering element ([L1147](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1147), [L1151](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1151)); or `id`, used as `#id`; plus `hx-swap`, which defaults to `config.defaultSwap` ([L1150](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1150)). They are read with `__attr`, so `data-hx-*` works but `:inherited` does not. A partial with neither `hx-target` nor `id` is dropped without any warning ([L1147-1148](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1147-L1148), [L1165](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1165)); the docs say one of them is required ([tag page L21-27](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/reference/06-tags/01-hx-partial.md#L21-L27)).
- **Case.** The captured type keeps its case, and `type === 'partial'` is a case-sensitive check, so `<HX-PARTIAL>` is ignored.
- **Swap behaviour.** Partials always suppress an empty main swap ([L1262](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1262)) and swap after the main content ([whats-new L72-80](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L72-L80)).
- **htmx 2.** `<hx-partial>` is not v4-only: 2.0.11 backported it ([v2 CHANGELOG L5](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/CHANGELOG.md#L5)), and v2 documents partials as executing **before** the main swap ([v2 hx-partial.md](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/attributes/hx-partial.md)).

**Attribute-name grammar (v4).** Names are ASCII case-insensitive, because the HTML parser lowercases them.

```
name    = prefix base [":inherited"] [":append"]   ; suffixes only on names read via __attributeValue
prefix  = "hx-" | config.prefix                    ; default "data-hx-"; configurable; "" disables
hx-on   = prefix "on" ( "" | ":" event | "::" htmx-event-rest )
status  = prefix "status:" ( DDD | DDx | Dxx ) [":inherited"]
ext     = "sse:connect|close" | "ws:connect|send" | "multipart:connect|close" | "live" [":" binding] | …
```

- **Prefix.** `__attr` tries `name`, then `name.replace('hx-', prefix)` ([L242-245](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L242-L245)). The default is `prefix: "data-hx-"` ([L187](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L187)). The docs call it "a secondary attribute prefix recognised alongside the always-active `hx-` prefix… Set to `""` to disable" ([docs.md L1987](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1987)). Setting a custom prefix replaces `data-hx-` ([upgrade skill L119-130](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/skills/htmx-upgrade-from-htmx2.md#L119-L130)).
  - Exception: `hx-morph-skip` and `hx-morph-skip-children` are matched with the unprefixed config selectors `'[hx-morph-skip]'` and `'[hx-morph-skip-children]'` ([L199-200](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L199-L200)). So `data-hx-morph-skip` is not recognised. This comes from reading the source; there is no test for it.
- **Inheritance.** `__attributeValue` reads `name`, then `name:inherited`, on the element. Failing that, it looks for the closest ancestor with `name:inherited` (or with plain `name` when `implicitInheritance` is on). `:append` and `:inherited:append` concatenate parent and child with `,` after removing `{}` ([L285-317](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L285-L317)).
  - Only the order `:inherited:append` is read. `:append:inherited` has no effect.
  - The default is `implicitInheritance: false` ([L203](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L203)).
  - Suffixes cannot apply to `hx-on*`, `hx-swap-oob`, `hx-ignore`, `hx-preserve`, `hx-history-elt`, `hx-morph-skip*`, or the attributes of `<hx-partial>`, because none of these go through `__attributeValue`.
- **`metaCharacter`.** When set, every `:` in attribute and event names is replaced by it ([L2340-2346](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2340-L2346)). For example, `metaCharacter = "-"` gives `hx-target-inherited`, `hx-on-click` and `hx-ws-connect` ([whats-new L366-375](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L366-L375)). It must be set through the meta tag ([docs.md L2013](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L2013)).
- **`hx-on`.** An XPath query selects every element that has an attribute starting with `hx-on` or `data-hx-on` ([L153](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L153)). Each such attribute is then handled as follows:
  - Nothing after the prefix: the bare extended form `event-spec -> code`, with parts split on the `;` that precedes a `->` ([L1724-1731](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1724-L1731)).
  - Otherwise the rest must start with the metaCharacter (default `:`) or the attribute is ignored ([L1733](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1733)). So the v2 dash forms `hx-on-click` and `hx-on--after-request` are silently dead.
  - `::x` means `htmx:x` ([L1735](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1735)).
  - The event part itself goes through the trigger-spec parser ([L1736](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1736)), so it may contain colons: `hx-on:htmx:after:swap`, `hx-on::sse:after:message`.
- **Case in `hx-on`.** `hx-on:myEvent` listens for `myevent` ([hx-on.md L270](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/11-hx-on.md#L270)). As a result, `hx-on::before:viewTransition` can never fire: htmx dispatches `htmx:before:viewTransition` ([L2290](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2290)).
- **`hx-status`.** htmx tries `hx-status:` + the exact code, then `NNx`, then `Nxx` ([L2252-2267](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L2252-L2267)). Web-types models this with the regex `hx-status:(\d{3}|\d{2}[xX]|\d[xX]{2})` ([web-types L246-248](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L246-L248)).
- **Templating inside names.** htmx's own upgrade checker replaces `{{…}}` with `X` and drops `{%…%}` before parsing ([upgrade-check.py L241-250](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L241-L250)). That is a precedent for handling Django/Jinja inside names and values.

**Bundled extensions.** An extension is loaded by including its script; there is no `hx-ext` attribute. `config.extensions` is an allow-list of *registration* names, which differ from the file names: `hx-preload.js` registers `preload`, `hx-sse.js` registers `sse` ([upgrade skill L291-293](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/skills/htmx-upgrade-from-htmx2.md#L291-L293)). `htmax.js` bundles 11 of them ([package.json L28](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/package.json#L28)).

| File (registered name)                                       | Attributes, tags and values it reads                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Code                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

Legacy `sse-connect`/`sse-close` are copied to the new names with a warning. `sse-swap` is removed (warning only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | [L9-24](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-sse.js#L9-L24), [L291](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-sse.js#L291), [L329-339](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-sse.js#L329-L339), [L363-374](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-sse.js#L363-L374), [L409-428](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-sse.js#L409-L428) |

**Where docs, source and web-types disagree.** Each row is a place where a generator or a rule would go wrong if it trusted the docs.

### htmx 2 → 4 diff

Sources for this section: [whats-new L139-154, L276-287](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L139-L154); [upgrade-check.py L21-35, L103-109](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L21-L35) (on `four-dev` it also has `preload` → `hx-preload`, [L104](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/372c6e3293f0d1c82d8f692ae873120aacb8fb85/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L104)); and both code bases.

| htmx 2.0.11                                                                                     | htmx 4.0.0                                                                                                                                                                                               | Kind                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `hx-disable` (skip htmx processing)                                                             | `hx-ignore`                                                                                                                                                                                              | renamed. `hx-disable` still exists but now means v2's `hx-disabled-elt`: **same name, different meaning** ([whats-new L141](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L141)) |
| `hx-disabled-elt`                                                                               | `hx-disable`                                                                                                                                                                                             | renamed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `hx-vars`                                                                                       | `hx-vals="js:…"`                                                                                                                                                                                         | removed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `hx-params`                                                                                     | `htmx:config:request` event                                                                                                                                                                              | removed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `hx-request`                                                                                    | `hx-config`                                                                                                                                                                                              | replaced                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `hx-prompt`                                                                                     | hx-prompt extension                                                                                                                                                                                      | moved to an extension                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `hx-ext`, `hx-inherit`, `hx-disinherit`                                                         | none (scripts are included directly; `:inherited` replaces inheritance control)                                                                                                                          | removed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `hx-history="false"`                                                                            | hx-history-cache extension only                                                                                                                                                                          | removed from core                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| extension attributes `preload`, `sse-connect`, `sse-close`, `sse-swap`, `ws-connect`, `ws-send` | `hx-preload`, `hx-sse:connect`, `hx-sse:close`, (removed), `hx-ws:connect`, `hx-ws:send`                                                                                                                 | renamed ([preload docs L97-100](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/extensions/05-hx-preload.md#L97-L100))                                                                                         |
| none                                                                                            | `hx-action`, `hx-method`, `hx-query`, `hx-config`, `hx-ignore`, `hx-status:*`, `hx-morph-skip(-children)`, `hx-pending`, `hx-multipart:*`, `hx-live*`, `:inherited`, `:append`, bare `hx-on="evt -> js"` | new                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `<hx-partial>` (2.0.11 only)                                                                    | `<hx-partial>`                                                                                                                                                                                           | exists in both; swap order differs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Same attribute name, different grammar or semantics:

- **Inheritance.** v2 inherits implicitly: `getClosestAttributeValue` walks up the ancestors, and `hx-inherit`/`hx-disinherit` control it ([v2 L466-498](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L466-L498)). v4 requires an explicit `:inherited`; `implicitInheritance` restores the old behaviour.
  - This is the typical Django break: the old CSRF recipe `<body hx-headers='{"X-CSRFToken": "{{ csrf_token }}"}'>` no longer reaches any request. The docs now write `hx-headers:inherited` ([docs.md L1900-1911](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1900-L1911)), and upgrade-check special-cases CSRF values ([L376-438](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L376-L438)).
- **`hx-boost`.** In v2 only the exact value `"true"` enables boosting ([v2 L3008](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3008)). In v4 any value other than exactly `"false"` enables it, and HCON config such as `swap:… target:… select:…` is merged into the request context ([L947-958](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L947-L958), [L410-412](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L410-L412)).
- **`hx-on`.** v2 accepts `hx-on:e`, `hx-on-e`, `hx-on::e`, `hx-on--e` and `hx-on-htmx-e` ([v2 L2970-2994](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2970-L2994)), and dispatches each event under both a camelCase and a kebab-case name ([v2 L3089-3091, L3171-3175](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3171-L3175)). v4 keeps only `hx-on:e`, `hx-on::e` and the bare form, and renames events to `htmx:phase:action` ([whats-new L156-192](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L156-L192)).
  - So v2's documented `hx-on::after-request` never fires in v4.
  - v4 runs the handler as an async function with `event.detail` in scope and the htmx API available as bare names ([L1710-1713](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1710-L1713), [L896-910](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L896-L910)).
- **`hx-trigger`.** v2 uses a tokenizer that knows `once changed consume delay throttle from target queue root threshold` and raises `htmx:syntax:error` on unknown tokens ([v2 L2288-2376](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2288-L2376)). v2 also accepts `every 1s [f]` and `from:closest form`. v4 parses with HCON:
  - `queue:` is removed ([whats-new L82-93](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L82-L93)).
  - `prevent`, `stop`, `halt`, `capture`, `passive` and `rootMargin` are new.
  - Selectors that contain spaces or commas must be quoted ([whats-new L95-108](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L95-L108)).
  - Unknown modifiers are silently ignored.
- **`hx-swap` styles.** v2 has innerHTML, outerHTML, textContent, beforebegin, afterbegin, beforeend, afterend, delete and none; an unknown style silently falls back to the default ([v2 L1838-1842](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L1838-L1842)). v4 adds innerMorph, outerMorph, outerSync and the aliases before/after/prepend/append, and an unknown style **throws** ([L1434-1449](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1434-L1449)).
- **`hx-swap` modifiers.** v2: `swap: settle: transition: ignoreTitle: scroll:[sel:]pos show:[sel:]pos focus-scroll:`, and an unknown modifier is logged ([v2 L3818-3870](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3818-L3870)). v4: `scrollTarget`/`showTarget` replace the `sel:pos` form ([whats-new L289-300](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L289-L300)), `focusScroll`, `target`, `strip` and `swapEmpty` are added, the JSON form is accepted, and unknown keys are silently ignored.
- **`hx-sync`.** The strategy used when none is given was `drop` in v2 ([v2 L4393](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L4393)) and is `queue first` in v4 ([L680-682](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L680-L682)). A bare `queue` meant `last` in v2 ([v2 L4409](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L4409)) and means first in v4 ([L110-113](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L110-L113)).
- **`hx-vals` / `hx-headers`.** v2 trims the value, treats `unset` specially, accepts `js:`/`javascript:`, adds braces if missing, then runs `JSON.parse`; on a parse error it logs and drops the value ([v2 L3971-4014](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3971-L4014)). v4 does not trim; it handles `js:`, then parses as JSON **only if the first character is `{`**, and otherwise as HCON ([L1856-1874](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1856-L1874)). A JSON error aborts the request. v2's `unset` becomes a literal parameter `unset=true` (confirmed by probe).
- **`hx-swap-oob`.** v2 accepts `true | style | style:selector`. v4 also accepts `style modifiers…` including `target:`, but 4.0.0 only splits `style:selector` when the value contains no space ([L1078-1096](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1078-L1096)).
- **Selector keywords.** v2 had `root` ([v2 L1183](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L1183)) and the `inherit` list keyword for hx-include, hx-indicator and hx-disabled-elt ([v2 L1349-1373](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L1349-L1373)). v4 has neither (`:append` replaces `inherit`) and adds `findAll` and `<sel/>`.
- **Eval kill-switch.** v2's `htmx.config.allowEval` turned off every eval feature ([v2 L4022-4029](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L4022-L4029)). v4 removed it ([whats-new L206](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L206)); the hx-csp extension gates eval per element instead.
- **`data-hx-`.** v2 always accepts `data-` + name ([v2 L417-419](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L417-L419)). v4 accepts a configurable prefix whose default, `data-hx-`, gives the same result.
- **Non-syntax changes.** `hx-delete` no longer sends the enclosing form, and 4xx/5xx responses are now swapped ([whats-new L45-61](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L45-L61)). When an `hx-target` matches nothing, v4 prints a `console.warn` and the main swap falls back to the source element ([L450-460](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L450-L460), [L1330](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1330)); this is from reading the source and no test covers it.

**Verdict: djangofmt needs an htmx-version setting.** Many valid v2 values are wrong in v4 and vice versa: `hx-disable`, `hx-on::after-request`, `from:closest form`, `focus-scroll:`, `show:#x:top`, `queue:all`, and `hx-boost`/`hx-headers` placed on a parent. npm `latest` stays 2.x until early 2027, and v2 is supported indefinitely. `<hx-partial>` is valid in both 2.0.11 and 4.

### Value grammars (4.0.0)

**HCON** ([L10-88](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L10-L88), [hcon-guide](https://four.htmx.org/docs/hcon-guide)) is the format behind hx-trigger modifiers, hx-swap modifiers, hx-vals, hx-headers, hx-config, hx-status, hx-boost config and the meta config.

- `parse`: an empty string gives `{}`. If `startsWith('{')`, the value goes to `JSON.parse`, which can throw.
- Otherwise a regex extracts `key[:value]` pairs separated by whitespace or commas. Keys are bare, `"…"` or `'…'`. Values are `"…"`, `'…'`, `<…/>` or bare `[^\s,]+`. A missing value means `true`. Values that JSON-parse become numbers, booleans or quoted arrays. A bare dotted key `a.b:1` nests. `__proto__` is dropped.
- Nothing is trimmed before the `{` test. Probe: `' {"a":1}'` → `{'{"a"': '1}'}`.
- `split` breaks on top-level commas, keeping `[]`, `()`, `<…/>` and quotes intact ([L60-62](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L60-L62)).
- Consequence: outside the JSON branch, HCON never fails. Typos, legacy syntax and missing quotes are accepted silently, which is exactly what a static checker can catch.

Invalid value →                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Static check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | Or JSON `{"style":…}` ([hx-swap.md L23-356](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/08-hx-swap.md#L23-L356), [hcon-guide L129-140](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/hcon-guide.md#L129-L140)) | `__parseSwapSpec` [L1129-1138](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1129-L1138): the first token is the style unless it contains `:`. ### The requester's claims

1. **"hx-vals should be valid JSON or `js:`".** Accurate for v2 and too strict for v4.
   - In v4, only a value whose first character is `{` must be strict JSON: no single quotes, unquoted keys or trailing commas, as confirmed by the probe on `{'a':1}` and `{a:1}`.
   - Brace-less values are **HCON, and the docs present that as valid** ([hcon-guide L26, L208-219](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/hcon-guide.md#L208-L219); [docs.md L687-690](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L687-L690)). There is no relaxed JS-object syntax without `js:`.
   - With `js:`, braces are optional ([L1860-1865](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1860-L1865); tests `javascript:i1:'test'` in [hx-headers.js L59-66](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/test/tests/attributes/hx-headers.js#L59-L66)).
