   - v2 adds braces when missing (so brace-less JSON also worked) and treats `unset` specially ([v2 L3982-3995](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3982-L3995)).
   - A rule that enforced "JSON or `js:`" would flag documented v4 usage.
2. **"hx-swap has a closed value set".** True for core only.
   - The core styles form a closed, case-sensitive set: 12 styles plus 4 aliases, so 16 names. The modifiers are closed too.
   - Extensions add styles: `upsert` and `download` in v4; `morph`, `morphdom` and `multi:…` among v2 extensions ([alpine-morph L4](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx-extensions/blob/135823253877cbe989f0abd7fcf5703dc993cc7c/src/alpine-morph/alpine-morph.js#L4), [morphdom-swap L4](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx-extensions/blob/135823253877cbe989f0abd7fcf5703dc993cc7c/src/morphdom-swap/morphdom-swap.js#L4), [multi-swap L10](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx-extensions/blob/135823253877cbe989f0abd7fcf5703dc993cc7c/src/multi-swap/multi-swap.js#L10)). So the check needs an "extra styles" option.
3. **"hx-method has a closed value set".** The docs list six verbs; the source accepts any token and uppercases it. Linting against the documented set is still reasonable.
4. **"`<hx-partial>` is a new htmx 4 tag".** 2.0.11 backported it.

### Machine-readable sources, ranked by robustness

1. **Web-types JSON (first-party).**
   - Location: `src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json`, shipped in npm as `dist/editors/…` via the `web-types` field ([package.json L10](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/package.json#L10)), and hosted at https://four.htmx.org/js/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json (HTTP 200, `version: 4.0.0` on 2026-09-23).
   - Contents for v4: 36 core and 13 extension attributes; template patterns for `hx-on:`/`hx-on::`; regexes for `hx-status` and `hx-live:*`; the `hx-partial` element; 6 CSS classes; 58 htmx event names (28 core).
   - Missing: **no value enums**, no prefix or suffix grammar, incomplete extensions (hx-multipart).
   - Why it is robust: the core names, events and tags are CI-checked against the docs, and `version` must equal the package version ([check_web_types.py L147-180](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/content/check_web_types.py#L147-L180)).
   - v2: the 2.0.11 package ships its own copy at `editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json` (36 attributes; it wrongly lists `hx-partial` as an attribute and marks `hx-vars` deprecated).
   - **Best source for attribute names, patterns and event names.**
2. **`upgrade-check.py` (first-party, shipped in `dist/scripts/`).** Python dict and set literals `REMOVED_ATTRS`, `RENAMED_ATTRS`, `INHERITABLE_ATTRS`, `EVENT_RENAMES`, `REMOVED_EVENTS`, SSE/WS event renames, `EXT_ATTR_RENAMES` and `REMOVED_CONFIG` ([L21-168](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L21-L168)). These can be read with `ast.literal_eval`. Nothing checks them in CI, and they contain the small inconsistencies in rows 12 and 14 of the discrepancy table. **Good source for migration tables.**
3. **Regexes over `src/htmx.js`.** Workable for the swap styles (`swapStyle === '(\w+)'` at [L1392-1433](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1392-L1433), plus the aliases at [L270-275](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L270-L275)), the verbs ([L142](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L142)), the sync strategies ([L100-110](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L100-L110)), the config defaults ([L185-206](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L185-L206)) and the dispatched events. **Brittle** for modifiers, because `spec.*` and `swapSpec.*` mix user keys with internal fields (`spec.listeners`, `spec.handler`, `spec.observer`, …). Use it as a drift test, not as the table source.
4. **Docs Markdown.** One file per attribute, but the front matter holds only ``title`` and ``description``; values live in prose under ``### \``name\``` headings. There are known errors (see the discrepancy table), and the docs at the release tag had parser-contradicting examples that were fixed later. Use the docs only for URLs (``https://four.htmx.org/reference/attributes/<name>`).
5. **`htmx.d.ts`.** Covers config, context and event types. Swap is typed `string`, request options are `[key: string]: any`, and `HtmxEventMap` has 30 keys, including two extension-only hooks (`after:implicitInheritance`, `process:partial`) that are never dispatched as DOM events. No attribute data.
6. **VS Code custom data.** There is no first-party file: `editors/vscode/README.md` only points to `atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit`, an MIT project with hand-written TypeScript tables that cover htmx 2 only as of `09846fee`.
7. **ThePrimeagen/htmx-lsp** (Rust, MIT, [`a05bf01d`](https://github.com/ThePrimeagen/htmx-lsp/blob/a05bf01d33710f1e86e3e4165badda9826a7a9bb/lsp/src/htmx/mod.rs#L61-L200), last commit 2025-10-18; crates.io `htmx-lsp` 0.1.0 from 2023-10-03).
   - It holds a `phf` map plus one Markdown file per attribute or value: 32 attributes of the htmx 1/2 era, 8 swap styles (no textContent), trigger values including `queue:`, and a list of v1 `hx-ext` names.
   - The htmx 4 docs themselves say its completions "predate htmx 4" ([editor-support.md L84-85](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/editor-support.md#L84-L85)).
   - **Not usable for v4.** At most, prior art for keeping these tables in Rust.
8. **bigskysoftware/htmx-extensions (v2 extensions, `13582325`).** Useful only for a v2 profile. Its attribute names are response-targets' `hx-target-<code\|NNx\|Nxx\|*\|error…>` ([L5-51](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx-extensions/blob/135823253877cbe989f0abd7fcf5703dc993cc7c/src/response-targets/response-targets.js#L5-L51)), `hx-disable-element`, `sse-*`, `ws-*`, `preload`, `classes`, `remove-me`, `path-deps`, `data-loading*` and `*-template`.

### JS-evaluated attributes, and the security guidance

All evaluation goes through `__executeJavaScript`. It builds a `Function` or `AsyncFunction` whose parameters are the htmx API methods plus the scope ([L896-910](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L896-L910)); hx-csp can substitute nonce-gated constructors ([L165-169](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L165-L169), [hx-csp.js L130-166](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-csp.js#L130-L166)).

| Context                    | Attributes (v4)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Code kind                                                                     | Source                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Event handler              | `hx-on:<evt>`, `hx-on::<evt>`, the right side of `->` in bare `hx-on`, and the `data-hx-on*` variants                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Statements, async, run inside `with(event.detail)`, with `this` = the element | [L1706-1738](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1706-L1738)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Trigger filter             | `evt[expr]` in `hx-trigger`, on the left side of bare `hx-on`, and in `hx-trigger` on sse/ws/multipart elements                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Expression, sync, with the event's properties as bare names                   | [L800-804](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L800-L804)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `js:`/`javascript:` values | `hx-vals`, `hx-headers`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Object expression, async                                                      | [L1856-1874](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1856-L1874)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
|                            | `hx-confirm`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Expression, async; a truthy result means "confirmed"                          | [L564-565](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L564-L565)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
|                            | `hx-get` … `hx-query`, `hx-action`. Also reachable indirectly: `hx-config` HCON is merged into `ctx.request` and `hx-boost` config into `ctx`, so `hx-config="action:js:…"` sets a `js:` action. A probe confirmed the merge step; execution then follows from the code order L419-421 → L513-517, which I did not run in a browser | Statements, async, run **instead of** the request                             | [L513-517](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L513-L517), [L419-421](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L419-L421), [L410-412](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L410-L412) |
| Extensions                 | `hx-live`, `hx-live:<binding>`, `:<binding>` when enabled; `js:` in `hx-vals` for hx-ws and hx-preload                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Expressions                                                                   | [hx-live.js L719-770](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-live.js#L719-L770)                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

These are **not** evaluated: hx-swap, the selectors, and the HCON literals themselves (`<…/>` values are plain strings).

For comparison, v2 evaluates `hx-on*`, trigger filters ([v2 L2209-2236](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2209-L2236)), `js:` in `hx-vals`/`hx-headers`/`hx-request`, and `hx-vars` always ([v2 L3971-4014](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3971-L4014)), all gated by `allowEval`.

What the htmx docs say about security:

- **Escaping does not protect JS contexts.** "**Never put user-controlled data in JS-evaluated attributes.** `hx-on:*`, `hx-vals js:`, `hx-confirm js:`, and trigger filters are evaluated as JavaScript. HTML escaping does not protect you here — the browser decodes HTML entities *before* passing the string to the JS engine, so an attacker can break out of a string literal even if your template engine has escaped the value." ([hx-csp.md L49](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/extensions/14-hx-csp.md#L49))
- **`js:` in URL attributes survives escaping.** `js:`/`javascript:` values in URL attributes "survive entity encoding" ([L47](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/extensions/14-hx-csp.md#L47)), and hx-csp blocks them ([hx-csp.js L195-213](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-csp.js#L195-L213)).
- **What uses eval.** The "htmx & Eval" section lists the features that use `new Function()`: event filters, `hx-on`, and `js:`/`javascript:` values ([docs.md L1873-1881](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1873-L1881)).
- **Escape user content.** "Rule 1: Escape All User Content" says to rely on autoescaping, and when injecting raw HTML to scrub `hx-`/`data-hx` attributes ([L1776-1798](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1776-L1798)).
- **`hx-ignore` for raw user HTML** ([L1807-1822](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1807-L1822)).
- **`hx-config` cannot widen `mode`** ([L1842-1847](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs.md#L1842-L1847)).
- **`js:` warnings** on the `hx-vals` and `hx-headers` pages ([hx-vals.md L36](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/12-hx-vals.md#L36), [hx-headers.md L39-44](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/18-hx-headers.md#L39-L44)).
- **No kill-switch.** v4 has no `allowEval` ([whats-new L206](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/c2ecb87aff2775ce92ee03c8a0c18ff10c255f33/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L206)).

My own conclusions from the same entity-decoding behaviour (the docs do not state these):

- **Parameter injection.** `{{ var }}` inside a JSON or HCON string in `hx-vals`/`hx-headers` lets a value containing `"` inject extra parameters. Django turns `"` into `&quot;`, and the browser decodes it back before htmx parses the value. Probe: HCON parses `name:"x" admin:true ""` as `{name:"x", admin:true, …}`.
- **Prefix control.** A `{{ var }}` at the *start* of `hx-confirm`, `hx-vals`, `hx-headers` or a URL attribute lets the data choose the `js:` prefix.

### What djangofmt could check, and from which data source

**Settings.** The htmx behaviour that matters here is global, not per rule, so it needs one shared table: `htmx-version` ("2" | "4"), `prefix` (default `data-hx-`), `meta-character` (default `:`), `implicit-inheritance` (default false), `extensions` (a list that gates extension names and styles), and `extra-attributes`, `extra-swap-styles` and `extra-events` for third-party extensions. Rule-specific options stay under `[tool.djangofmt.lint.<rule>]`.

- djangofmt already has a precedent for version gating: [`DjangoVersion` / `target-version`](https://github.com/UnknownPlatypus/djangofmt/blob/39c41a2ac942522d78dd31584c43fe1846acc9df/crates/djangofmt_lint/src/django_version.rs#L1-L50).
- The existing `invalid-attr-value` rule skips interpolated values. The `javascript-url` rule covers only standard URL attributes, so a v4 extension of it could cover `hx-get`… and `hx-action`.

| Check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Versions               | Data source                                                                                                                                    | False-positive risk                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Unknown `hx-*`/`data-hx-*` attribute, after stripping prefix and suffixes; understands `hx-on*`, `hx-status:`, `hx-live:*`, `hx-sse:*` etc.; offers did-you-mean and v2 rename hints                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | 2, 4                   | **Generated** from web-types (names and patterns), plus hand-written extras (`hx-multipart:*`, legacy extension names, v2 extension names)     | Low (`hx-` is htmx's namespace); `extra-attributes` covers the rest |
| htmx-2 leftovers under v4: `hx-disabled-elt`, `hx-vars`, `hx-params`, `hx-ext`, `hx-(dis)inherit`, `hx-request`, `hx-history` (without history-cache), `sse-*`, `ws-*`, `preload`. Bare `hx-disable` with no value (a v2 "ignore") → suggest `hx-ignore`                                                                                                                                               | 4                      | **Generated** from upgrade-check.py dicts, plus hand-written: `focus-scroll`, `show:sel:pos`, `queue:`, `unset`, the `inherit`/`root` keywords | Low                                                                 |
| `:inherited`/`:append` on attributes that cannot inherit; `:append:inherited` order; `:merge`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | 4                      | Hand-written from the list of names read through `__attributeValue`                                                                            | Very low                                                            |
| `hx-on` names: uppercase in the event part; `hx-on::x` where `htmx:x` is not a known event (catches v2 kebab names); v2 dash forms; bare `hx-on` value with no `->`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | 2 (case only), 4       | **Generated** from web-types `js/htmx-events`, plus `extra-events`                                                                             | Low                                                                 |
| Missing `:inherited`: `hx-boost` on elements other than `a`/`form`; request-config attributes on `<html>`/`<body>`, or on an element with no request attribute whose descendants have one (upgrade-check's algorithm, [L393-443](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L393-L443)). Off when `implicit-inheritance = true` | 4                      | Hand-written, seeded from `INHERITABLE_ATTRS`                                                                                                  | Medium, because templates extend each other: ship as preview        |
| `hx-swap` grammar (style, modifiers, enums, case). Applies to `hx-swap`, `hx-swap-oob`, `<hx-partial hx-swap>`, and `swap:` in hx-status and hx-boost                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | 2, 4 (separate tables) | Hand-written tables, with a drift test against a regex over the pinned `htmx.js`                                                               | Low                                                                 |
| `hx-trigger` grammar: modifiers, intervals, brackets, a space before `[`, unquoted selectors with spaces, `queue:` and `every … [f]` under v4                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | 2, 4                   | Hand-written; v4 needs a small HCON port                                                                                                       | Low to medium                                                       |
| Enums: `hx-sync`, `hx-method` (v4), `hx-encoding`, exact `true`/`false` for push-url, replace-url, validate and boost; bare `hx-boost`/`hx-validate`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | 2, 4                   | Hand-written (docs and source agree)                                                                                                           | Low                                                                 |
| JSON/HCON checks on `hx-vals`, `hx-headers`, `hx-config`, `hx-status` and the meta config: strict JSON when the value starts with `{`; leading/trailing whitespace; `js: {`; `mode` in `hx-config`; the `hx-status` name pattern. Replace `{{…}}` with a placeholder (`0` outside strings, as-is inside) or skip values containing `{%`                                                                | 2, 4                   | Hand-written                                                                                                                                   | Low                                                                 |
| `<hx-partial>`: needs `hx-target` or `id`; no nesting; `<template hx-type>` should be `<template hx type="partial">`. `hx-preserve` needs an `id`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | 2.0.11+, 4             | Hand-written                                                                                                                                   | Low                                                                 |
| Django `{{ }}`/`{% %}` inside a JS context (see the JS table) unless it passes through a configurable escaping filter; also interpolation at the start of a value that accepts `js:`. The URL-attribute part should be optional, because `hx-get="{% url … %}"` is ubiquitous                                                                                                                          | 2, 4                   | Hand-written context table                                                                                                                     | Medium; make it configurable                                        |
| `js:`/`javascript:` in `hx-get`… and `hx-action` (an extension of the existing `javascript-url` rule)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | 4                      | Hand-written                                                                                                                                   | Low                                                                 |

**Generator.** A `djangofmt_dev generate-htmx` command could work like this:

1. Download the pinned npm tarballs from `registry.npmjs.org`: 2.0.11 and 4.0.0 today.
2. Extract `htmx.web-types.json` and `upgrade-check.py` from them.
3. Emit Rust `&[&str]`/`phf` tables for names, patterns, events and migration maps, recording the version.
4. Keep the grammar module hand-written: swap styles and modifiers, trigger modifiers, sync strategies, the inheritable set and the JS-context table. A dev test regex-scans the pinned `htmx.js` for `swapStyle === '…'`, `#verbs` and the sync strategies, and fails on drift.
5. Hand-maintain what web-types lacks: `hx-multipart:*` names and events, the prefix and suffix grammar, and all value enums.

**Formatter caution (djangofmt formats as well as lints).** htmx 4 does **not** trim `hx-vals`, `hx-headers` or `hx-config`: the `{`/`js:` test runs on the raw value. It also does not trim `hx-method`, `hx-encoding`, `hx-push-url`/`hx-replace-url`, `hx-boost` or `hx-validate`, all of which use exact comparisons. So the formatter must never add leading or trailing whitespace to these values, nor wrap a value so that it starts with a newline. `hx-swap`, `hx-trigger`, `hx-on` and `hx-sync` do trim ([L1130](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1130), [L321](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L321), [L1728](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1728), [L681](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L681)).

## C. Template values in JS-evaluated attributes

Security semantics and prior art for the issue #451 proposal "never allow Django tags in `x-data`/`x-init`/`x-on` without a safe filter, with a `safe-js-filters` setting".

### Sources and versions examined

- **Django**: source read at `main` [`951d13c`](https://github.com/django/django/tree/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1) (6.2 alpha, 2026-09-23). Behaviour checked at runtime on **Django 6.1.1**, the current stable release, from PyPI. The code cited below is the same in both: `defaultfilters.py`, `l10n.py`, `i18n.py`, `formats.py` and `defaulttags.py` are byte-identical, and `html.py`, `base.py` and `resolvers.py` differ only in code this section does not cite. Rendered docs: `docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/`.
- **WHATWG HTML Living Standard**, "Last Updated 22 September 2026". **OWASP CheatSheetSeries** [`394fe51`](https://github.com/OWASP/CheatSheetSeries/tree/394fe51fe08c5c53e99eee5781f28b886056fa25).
- **Alpine** `main` [`20ee0e9`](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/tree/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d) (= v3.17.4; npm `@alpinejs/csp@3.17.4` was published 2026-09-21). **htmx** v2.0.11 [`fa978b2`](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/tree/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1) and v4.0.0 [`4195bc0`](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/tree/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3).
- **Linters**: semgrep-rules `a84ff9c`, djLint `3522886`, curlylint `4c36043` (last commit 2022-03-30), Go `html/template` `ab269a4`, Jinja `5ef7011`, Bandit `68ebe11`, Brakeman `71f8f69`, lit-analyzer `a518bf7`, eslint-plugin-lit `2d71ea0`, HTMLHint `3cf648e`, markuplint `7c3c510`.
- **Runtime experiments** (throwaway scripts, not kept) followed these steps:
  1. Render templates with Django 6.1.1, using `LANGUAGE_CODE="fr"` and `USE_THOUSAND_SEPARATOR=True`.
  2. Parse the output with **parse5 7.3.0**, which implements the WHATWG tokenizer.
  3. Evaluate each attribute value in Node 26 (V8). Alpine directives are built the way Alpine's [`evaluator.js` L94-97](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/evaluator.js#L94-L97) builds them, and `on*` attributes as a `FunctionBody`. The Alpine CSP build is tested with its own `packages/csp/src/parser.js`.

### What Django's escaping and filters actually produce

- **Autoescape is HTML-only.** It escapes exactly five characters: `<` `>` `'`→`&#x27;` `"`→`&quot;` `&` ([language.txt L681-688](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/ref/templates/language.txt#L681-L688); [rendered](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/ref/templates/language/#automatic-html-escaping)). The implementation is `html.escape` ([html.py L52-62](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/utils/html.py#L52-L62)), applied after all filters in `render_value_in_context` ([base.py L1140-1153](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/base.py#L1140-L1153)).
  - Django's own security docs state the limit: "Django's built-in escaping is intended to protect HTML output. If you are using the template system to output something other than HTML, the characters and strings that require escaping might be entirely different." ([security.txt L87-89](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/topics/security.txt#L87-L89); [rendered](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/topics/security/#cross-site-scripting-xss-protection))
- **`escapejs`** translates this fixed set to `\uXXXX`: backslash, `'`, `"`, `>`, `<`, `&`, `=`, `-`, `;`, `` ` `` (backtick), U+2028, U+2029, and every C0/C1 control (0x00-0x1F, 0x7F-0x9F). It returns `mark_safe(...)` ([html.py L65-90](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/utils/html.py#L65-L90)).
  - It does **not** escape `$ { } ( ) + / . , :`, space, letters or digits.
  - Docs: "Escapes characters for use as a whole JavaScript string literal, within single or double quotes … This filter does not make the string safe for use in *'JavaScript template literals'* … Any other uses not listed above are not supported. It is generally recommended that data should be passed using HTML `data-` attributes, or the `json_script` filter" ([builtins.txt L2094-2099](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/ref/templates/builtins.txt#L2094-L2099); [rendered 6.1](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/ref/templates/builtins/#std-templatefilter-escapejs)).
  - Its output contains no `"`, `'`, `&`, `<` or `>`. It therefore also stays inside either HTML attribute quote, and under `{% autoescape off %}` too (verified).
- **`json_script` cannot be used inside an attribute.** It returns a whole `<script id="…" type="application/json">…</script>` element, with only `<`, `>` and `&` escaped ([html.py L93-117](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/utils/html.py#L93-L117)). Its docstring says the value "is safe to be output anywhere **except for inside a tag attribute**" (L101-104).
  - Rendering `x-data="{{ v|json_script:'d' }}"` gives `x-data="<script id="d" type="application/json">{"a": 1}</script>"`. The first inner `"` closes the attribute, so the parser sees `x-data="<script id="` followed by junk attributes (verified).
  - **The issue's `safe-js-filters` example is wrong to list `json_script`.** It is the right tool only *outside* the tag: render it once, then read it with `JSON.parse(document.getElementById(id).textContent)` ([builtins.txt L2319-2351](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/ref/templates/builtins.txt#L2319-L2351); [rendered](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/ref/templates/builtins/#std-templatefilter-json_script)).
- **`unlocalize` is not an escaper.** It is `str(formats.localize(value, use_l10n=False))`, registered with `is_safe=False` ([l10n.py L15-20](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/templatetags/l10n.py#L15-L20)). `localize` treats each type differently ([formats.py L193-215](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/utils/formats.py#L193-L215)):
  - `str` is returned unchanged, then HTML-autoescaped as usual. So `'{{ v|unlocalize }}'` with `v = "'+alert(1)+'"` executes (verified).
  - `bool` becomes `"True"` or `"False"`, which are not JavaScript booleans.
  - `int`, `float` and `Decimal` become `str(value)`. Examples: `1234.5`, `inf`, `nan`.
  - Dates are formatted with the settings formats.
  - Its real job is **correctness for numbers**. Localized numbers break JavaScript: `{{ 1234.5 }}` renders `1\xa0234,5` under `fr` with thousand separators, which raises `SyntaxError`. Under a `,` decimal locale, `x = 1234,5` silently evaluates as a comma expression, so `x` is 1234.
  - Django locales use only `,`, `.`, a space, NBSP or nothing as thousand separators; no locale uses a quote (checked every `conf/locale/*/formats.py`).
  - **Treating `unlocalize` as "safe" is only an assertion, by the user, that the value is numeric.**
- **`safe`, `safeseq`, `mark_safe` and `{% autoescape off %}` make things worse.** They only switch off HTML escaping ([defaultfilters.py L511-525](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L511-L525); `mark_safe` is "safe for (HTML) output purposes", [utils.txt L863-866](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/ref/utils.txt#L863-L866)). In an attribute they add an HTML breakout on top of the JavaScript one.
  - Example: `x-data="{{ data_json|safe }}"` with a JSON string value containing `" onmouseover=alert(1) b="`. The parser produces a real `onmouseover="alert(1)"` attribute (verified).
- **`escape` and `force_escape` are HTML-only** ([L457-482](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L457-L482)), so they do no better than autoescape (verified).
- **Other built-ins, tested on the attacker string `');alert(1)//`:**
  - **Safe in any JavaScript position:** these filters produce only digits, a sign, a decimal point, or template-author literals, whatever the input.

    | Filter                                   | Output for the attacker string                                                                                          | Source                                                                                                                                 |
    | ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    | `length`                                 | an int                                                                                                                  | [L633-639](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L633-L639) |
    | `wordcount`                              | an int                                                                                                                  | —                                                                                                                                      |
    | `floatformat`                            | `""` for non-numeric input. Otherwise a string that `Decimal` could parse; localized unless the arg has the `u` suffix. | [L141-182](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L141-L182) |
    | `stringformat:"d"`                       | `""`                                                                                                                    | [L277-294](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L277-L294) |
    | `yesno:"true,false"` (arg with 2+ parts) | one of the literals                                                                                                     | [L861-892](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L861-L892) |
    | `add:<int>`                              | `""` for non-numbers                                                                                                    | —                                                                                                                                      |

  - **Safe only inside a quoted string** (the output contains no quotes or backslashes):
    - `urlencode` with no argument: `quote(value)`, so only `[A-Za-z0-9_.~/-]` and `%XX` (L365-379).
    - `slugify`: `[a-z0-9_-]`.
    - `date` and `time`: return `""` for a string input (L784-809).
  - **Pass the value through unchanged, so they are not sanitizers:**
    - `default` (L841-844).
    - `add` with a string argument, which concatenates (L746-755).
    - `stringformat:"s"`; `stringformat:"r"` is a Python repr.
    - `yesno` with fewer than 2 parts, which returns the value.
    - `get_digit` on invalid input, which returns the value.
    - `first`, `upper`, `truncatechars` and the like.
- **Only the last filter counts.** A filter after `escapejs` can bring back raw content.
  - `'{{ v|escapejs|default:w }}'` with `v=""` and `w="'+alert(1)+'"` executes (verified).
  - `|escapejs|safe` is harmless (verified). `|escapejs|upper` corrupts the escapes but does not inject.
  - Parse the chain with Django's grammar ([base.py L703-737](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/base.py#L703-L737)):
    - The token is a quoted constant, `_("…")`, or a variable made of `[\w.+-]`.
    - It is followed by `\s*|\s*name` and an optional `:arg`.
    - A quoted argument may contain `|`, so do not split on a bare `|`.
- **Template-author literals are not escaped.** Constants such as `{{ "It's" }}` and string arguments to filters are `mark_safe`d ([base.py L923](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/base.py#L923), [L825](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/base.py#L825); docs: "All string literals are inserted **without** any automatic escaping", [language.txt L812](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/ref/templates/language.txt#L812)). They are author-controlled, so they are not an XSS vector.
- **Translations are not escaped either.**
  - `{% translate %}` resolves a `mark_safe`d literal ([i18n.py L72-101](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/templatetags/i18n.py#L72-L101)). `'{% translate "It's here" %}'` renders a raw `'` and raises `SyntaxError` (verified), and any apostrophe in a `.po` file does the same.
  - `{% blocktranslate %}` inserts the translation raw and HTML-escapes only its `{{ vars }}` ([i18n.py L180-201](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/templatetags/i18n.py#L180-L201)), so a variable inside it is exploitable (verified).
  - The escapable form is `{% translate "…" as label %}` followed by `{{ label|escapejs }}` (verified).

### Why HTML escaping does not protect JS-evaluated attributes

1. **The tokenizer decodes entities before JavaScript sees the value.**
   - In the attribute value state, `&` switches to the character reference state. See [§13.2.5.36](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/parsing.html#attribute-value-(double-quoted)-state), its single-quoted and unquoted twins (§13.2.5.37-38), and [§13.2.5.77](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/parsing.html#character-reference-state).
   - For `&#x27;`, the [numeric character reference end state (§13.2.5.84)](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/parsing.html#numeric-character-reference-end-state) puts the code point in the temporary buffer. It then "flush[es] code points consumed as a character reference", which "append[s] the code point … to the current attribute's value" ([definition](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/parsing.html#flush-code-points-consumed-as-a-character-reference)).
   - The DOM attribute value therefore holds `'`, not `&#x27;`. parse5 turns `x-data="{ name: '&#x27;+alert(1)+&#x27;' }"` into the value `{ name: ''+alert(1)+'' }`.
2. **Every consumer reads the decoded DOM value and compiles it as code:**
   - **HTML event handlers** "must contain valid JavaScript code which … would match the FunctionBody production" ([webappapis §event-handler-content-attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handler-content-attributes)). The body is compiled in [getting the current value of the event handler](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#getting-the-current-value-of-the-event-handler).
   - **Alpine** walks `el.attributes` ([lifecycle.js L108](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/lifecycle.js#L108)) and passes `attr.value` as `expression` ([directives.js L183-199](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives.js#L183-L199)). The expression is spliced into ``new AsyncFunction(["__self","scope"], `with (scope) { __self.result = ${expression} }…`)`` ([evaluator.js L94-97](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/evaluator.js#L94-L97)).
   - **htmx 2** reads with `elt.getAttribute(name)` ([htmx.js L396-398](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L396-L398)). It compiles with `new Function('event', code)` for `hx-on` ([L2958](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2958)) and with `Function('return (' + str + ')')` for `js:` values ([L3985-3999](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L3985-L3999)).
   - **htmx 4's own docs** make the same point: "HTML escaping does not protect you here — the browser decodes HTML entities *before* passing the string to the JS engine" ([hx-csp.md L49](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/extensions/14-hx-csp.md#L49)).
3. **OWASP agrees:**
   - "attributes that accept JavaScript, such as `onClick`, are **NOT safe** to use with untrusted attribute values" ([XSS CS L82](https://github.com/OWASP/CheatSheetSeries/blob/394fe51fe08c5c53e99eee5781f28b886056fa25/cheatsheets/Cross_Site_Scripting_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.md#L82)).
   - "the only 'safe' location for placing variables in JavaScript is inside a 'quoted data value'", with the example `<div onmouseover="'$varUnsafe'"`, JS-encoded (L88-98).
   - Event handlers are listed as a dangerous context: "Don't place variables into dangerous contexts as even with output encoding, it will not prevent an XSS attack fully" (L157-160).
   - "JavaScript encoding does not stop attacks within an execution context", so "avoid including untrusted data in this context" ([DOM XSS CS L117-121](https://github.com/OWASP/CheatSheetSeries/blob/394fe51fe08c5c53e99eee5781f28b886056fa25/cheatsheets/DOM_based_XSS_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.md#L117-L121)).
   - Nested interpreters need "N-levels" of encoding (L321-360).

**Results by position** (Django 6.1.1 output → parse5 → V8; payload `v = "'+alert(1)+'"` unless stated):

| Template (attribute value)                                                                    | Rendered (excerpt)                       | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `x-data="{ name: '{{ v }}' }"`                                                                | `'&#x27;+alert(1)+&#x27;'`               | **executes**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `onclick="greet('{{ v }}')"` (and `…\|safe`)                                                  | same, or raw                             | **executes**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `x-data="{ name: '{{ v\|escapejs }}' }"`                                                      | `'\u0027+alert(1)+\u0027'`               | inert: the string `'+alert(1)+'`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `x-init="count = {{ v\|escapejs }}"`, `v="alert(1)"`                                          | `count = alert(1)`                       | **executes**: bare position, nothing to escape                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ``x-text="`Hi {{ v\|escapejs }}`"``, `v="${alert(1)}"`                                        | `` `Hi ${alert(1)}` ``                   | **executes**: `$` and `{` are not escaped                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `'{{ v\|unlocalize }}'`, `'{{ v\|force_escape }}'`, `'{% firstof v w %}'`, `'{{ v\|add:w }}'` | `&#x27;…`                                | **executes**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `'{% blocktranslate %}Hi {{ v }}{% endblocktranslate %}'`                                     | `Hi &#x27;…`                             | **executes**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `@click="go('{% url 'item' v %}')"`, `<str:name>`, `v="');alert(1);('"`                       | `go('/items/&#x27;);alert(1);(&#x27;/')` | **executes**. `reverse()` keeps RFC 3986 sub-delims `!$&'()*+,;=` unencoded ([resolvers.py L809-813](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/urls/resolvers.py#L809-L813), [http.py L40](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/utils/http.py#L40); `StringConverter` matches `[^/]+`, [converters.py L15-22](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/urls/converters.py#L15-L22)), and the tag only HTML-escapes ([defaulttags.py L520-522](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaulttags.py#L520-L522)). `<int:>` args are safe. |
| `'{% filter escapejs %}{% url 'item' v %}{% endfilter %}'`                                    | `\u0026#x27\u003B…`                      | inert, but double-encoded: the URL now holds `&#x27;`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `x-data="{{ state }}"`, `state = json.dumps(…)` in the view                                   | `{&quot;name&quot;: …}`                  | inert: the exact JSON literal                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `x-data="{ open: {{ flag }} }"` / `{{ flag\|yesno:'true,false' }}`                            | `True` / `true`                          | `ReferenceError` / correct                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `x-data="{ t: '{% csrf_token %}' }"` / `'{{ csrf_token }}'`                                   | `<input type="hidden" …>` / `abc123`     | broken markup / safe (`CSRF_ALLOWED_CHARS` = letters and digits, [csrf.py L46](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/middleware/csrf.py#L46))                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `hx-vals='{"q": "{{ v }}"}'`, `v='", "role": "admin'`                                         | `&quot;, &quot;role…`                    | JSON key injection: `JSON.parse` yields `{"q":"","role":"admin"}`. With `\|escapejs` the value stays in `q`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

**Where `escapejs` works and where it does not:**

- **It protects** a value inside a `'…'` or `"…"` literal. This is the only position the docs support.
- **Bare expression position:** `count = {{ n }}`, `f({{ x }})`, `x-data="{{ name }}"`, `x-model="{{ path }}"`, the collection in `x-for`. Nothing needs escaping to inject code here.
- **Template literals:** `${…}` interpolation is not escaped.
- **Regex literals:** `/` is not in the table (by inspection; not tested).
- **Comments:** `*/` is not escaped.
- **Strings passed on to a second interpreter:**
  - `x-html` sets `el.innerHTML` ([x-html.js L12](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives/x-html.js#L12)), and its docs say "never on user-provided content" ([html.md L8-11](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/directives/html.md#L8-L11)).
  - `setTimeout` or `eval` strings.
  - `location = '…'` with a `javascript:` URL.

**Context-aware engines draw the same boundaries.** Go `html/template` has three JavaScript sub-contexts:

- `jsValEscaper` produces "a JS Expression … that has neither side-effects nor free variables" by JSON-encoding the value. For `<a onx='f({{.}})'>` it outputs a quoted `"O\x27Reilly…"`.
- `jsStrEscaper` covers a value "between quotes … or in an HTML5 event handler attribute such as onclick".
- `jsTmplLitEscaper` additionally escapes `$`, `{` and `}`.

See [doc.go L133-145](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/ab269a42a408b8acaf038c811354a68139df38ba/src/html/template/doc.go#L133-L145) and [js.go L150-152, L249-262, L353-374](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/ab269a42a408b8acaf038c811354a68139df38ba/src/html/template/js.go#L150-L152). Django has only the second of these.

**The Alpine CSP build narrows the impact of injection but does not prevent it.**

- It interprets expressions with its own tokenizer and evaluator. At 20ee0e9 (and in the published 3.17.4 `dist`) the tokenizer decodes `\uXXXX` in string literals ([parser.js L154-163](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/csp/src/parser.js#L154-L163)). So `escapejs` output round-trips: running `parser.js` gives the literal string `'+deleteAccount()+'`.
- It blocks globals, `Document` objects, iframes and scripts, `constructor`/`prototype`, `setAttribute`, `insertAdjacentHTML` and similar ([L949-996](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/csp/src/parser.js#L949-L996)). An injected `alert(1)` fails with "Undefined variable".
- It still evaluates injected code against the component scope. `{ name: ''+deleteAccount()+'' }` called the component's `deleteAccount()`, and so did the bare `count = deleteAccount()` (verified).
- The history of the `\u` handling, which bears on the issue's claim that "escapejs does not work", belongs to the Alpine inventory.

### Safe patterns for passing server data (primary sources)

1. **`data-*` plus `dataset`.** The value stays in an ordinary HTML-attribute context, so autoescape is enough when the attribute is quoted.
   - Django recommends it ([escapejs docs](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/ref/templates/builtins/#std-templatefilter-escapejs)).
   - htmx 4: "The only safe pattern is to keep user data out of the attribute entirely and pass it via `data-*`", with `<button data-name="{{ user.name }}" hx-on:click="doThing(this.dataset.name)">` ([hx-csp.md L51-63](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/extensions/14-hx-csp.md#L51-L63)). The same file warns that Django's `escapejs` must be applied "correctly every time" and "may not protect against every injection vector".
   - Alpine's documented idiom: `<div x-data data-message="Hello World!"><button @click="alert($root.dataset.message)">` ([magics/root.md L11-15](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/magics/root.md#L11-L15)).
   - Spec: [`dataset`](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/dom.html#dom-dataset) and [custom data attributes](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/dom.html#embedding-custom-non-visible-data-with-the-data-*-attributes).
   - In the CSP build, reading a DOM property is allowed; only *assignment* to DOM objects throws ([parser.js L841-843](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/csp/src/parser.js#L841-L843), L904-906). Reading `$root.dataset` at runtime in the CSP build is unverified.
2. **`json_script` outside the tag, plus `Alpine.data()`.**
   - Render `{{ user|json_script:"user-data" }}`, and read it in a registered component: `Alpine.data('profile', () => ({ user: JSON.parse(document.getElementById('user-data').textContent) }))`. Use it as `x-data="profile"`.
   - Sources: Django [json_script docs](https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/ref/templates/builtins/#std-templatefilter-json_script) ("compatible with a strict Content Security Policy"); Alpine [alpine-data.md L12-30, L60-72](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/globals/alpine-data.md#L60-L72).
   - The CSP build cannot call `JSON.parse` or `document` *inside* an attribute ([csp.md L140-151](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/advanced/csp.md#L140-L151)), so the parse must live in script code. Alpine's CSP docs push logic into `Alpine.data()` anyway (L162-187).
3. **`|escapejs` as the last filter, inside a quoted JavaScript string literal.** This is Django's documented use. The attribute's HTML quote does not matter.
4. **A server-side JSON document output with autoescape on and without `|safe`**, e.g. `x-data="{{ state_json }}"` or `x-data="widget({{ config_json }})"`.
   - A JSON text is a side-effect-free JavaScript expression. This is the same approach as Go's `jsValEscaper`.
   - Autoescape protects the HTML boundary, and the decoded value is exactly the JSON (verified).
   - htmx's own `hx-vals` docs show this entity-encoded form: `hx-vals="{&quot;myVal&quot;: &quot;value&quot;}"` "also works" ([hx-vals.md L60](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/attributes/hx-vals.md#L60)).
   - Django has no built-in `json` filter; `json_script` is the only JSON filter ([defaultfilters.py L85-91](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaultfilters.py#L85-L91)). Projects add a custom filter, which is what the `safe-filters` setting is for.
5. **htmx `hx-vals` without `js:`.** The value is data, not code: it goes through `parseJSON(str)` ([htmx.js L4005](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L4005)), and the docs say "It is **not** dynamically computed" ([hx-vals.md L65-70](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/attributes/hx-vals.md#L65-L70)). A string interpolated into it still needs `|escapejs` (whose `\uXXXX` is valid JSON) or pattern 4, or it allows JSON key injection.
6. **Jinja profile: `|tojson`.** It escapes `< > & '` and marks the result safe. It is "safe to render in HTML documents and `<script>` tags. The exception is in HTML attributes that are double quoted; either use single quotes or the `|forceescape` filter" ([utils.py L646-651](https://github.com/pallets/jinja/blob/5ef70112a1ff19c05324ff889dd30405b1002044/src/jinja2/utils.py#L646-L651), [filters.py L1690-1715](https://github.com/pallets/jinja/blob/5ef70112a1ff19c05324ff889dd30405b1002044/src/jinja2/filters.py#L1690-L1715)). It is a complete literal, so it is safe in any JavaScript position. Jinja has no `escapejs`.
7. **Numbers and booleans:** use `|stringformat:"d"` or `|floatformat:"u"` for numbers, and `|yesno:"true,false"` for booleans. `{{ int }}` is only digits without `USE_THOUSAND_SEPARATOR`, but floats get the locale's decimal separator.

### Prior-art lint rules

**Summary: no surveyed linter checks template values inside `on*`, Alpine or htmx attributes.** No rule anywhere knows `escapejs`: grepping semgrep-rules finds it only in a Python test import.

**semgrep-rules, generic HTML templates** (`languages: generic`, `*.html|*.mustache|*.hbs|*.twig`):

- [`var-in-script-tag`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/generic/html-templates/security/var-in-script-tag.yaml#L44-L48)
  - Matches any `{{ ... }}` inside `<script …>…</script>`, except scripts that have any quoted attribute.
  - No filter exemption, so `{{ x|escapejs }}` is still reported.
  - `confidence: LOW`, `subcategory: audit`. Its message suggests `json_script` for Django.
  - Tests: `const mydata = {{ mydata_json|safe }};` is reported; `<script src="./{{ … }}" …>` is ok ([.html L5-18](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/generic/html-templates/security/var-in-script-tag.html#L5-L18)).
- [`var-in-href`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/generic/html-templates/security/var-in-href.yaml#L44-L54)
  - Matches `href="{{ … }}"` on `<a>`. It exempts `url_for(...)` and values that start with `/`; the Django tests also accept `{% url 'login' %}` as ok.
- [`unquoted-attribute-var`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/generic/html-templates/security/unquoted-attribute-var.yaml#L35-L44)
  - Matches `{{ … }}` inside a tag but outside `="…"` or `='…'`, with a quoting autofix. It is the attribute counterpart of Django's own `<style class={{ var }}>` warning ([security.txt L59-80](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/docs/topics/security.txt#L59-L80)).
- [`var-in-script-src`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/generic/html-templates/security/var-in-script-src.yaml#L41-L47).

**semgrep-rules, Django-specific:**

- [`template-autoescape-off`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/python/django/security/audit/xss/template-autoescape-off.yaml#L27-L33): regex `{%\s+autoescape\s+off\s+%}`.
- [`template-var-unescaped-with-safeseq`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/python/django/security/audit/xss/template-var-unescaped-with-safeseq.yaml#L34).
- `template-translate-as-no-escape` and `template-blocktranslate-no-escape`, both at severity INFO: "Translated strings will not be escaped … translators could include malicious script tags". They exempt only `force_escape` ([yaml](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/python/django/security/audit/xss/template-blocktranslate-no-escape.yaml#L1-L27)).
- Flask's [`template-unescaped-with-safe`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/python/flask/security/xss/audit/template-unescaped-with-safe.yaml#L33).
- Vue's `avoid-v-html`, the analogue of `x-html`.

**semgrep-rules, Rails [`var-in-script-tag`](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/ruby/rails/security/audit/xss/templates/var-in-script-tag.yaml#L39-L43).** This is the one rule that exempts a JavaScript escaper (`j` / `escape_javascript`), and it does so regardless of position. Its test marks the bare `var message = <%= j message %>;` as ok ([.erb L14-17](https://github.com/semgrep/semgrep-rules/blob/a84ff9cc2453ca91d581380de4b8b3f272f6f4be/ruby/rails/security/audit/xss/templates/var-in-script-tag.erb#L14-L17)). **That is the false negative to avoid.**

**djLint (63 rules):**

- No rule covers template variables in JavaScript contexts.
- [H019](https://github.com/djlint/djLint/blob/3522886b0ee5211f7c91891efa8ee00a6878a529/src/djlint/rules.yaml#L150-L157) flags `javascript:` in `href`/`data-url` on `a|div|span|input` and in `form` `action`. djangofmt's `javascript-url` mirrors it exactly.
- H008 and H011 cover quoting.

**curlylint:** a11y and indent rules only ([rules/](https://github.com/thibaudcolas/curlylint/tree/4c36043873f973aa49ed471c238a1de17d603db5/curlylint/rules)); the last commit was in 2022.

**Content-agnostic inline-handler bans:**

- HTMLHint [`inline-script-disabled`](https://github.com/htmlhint/HTMLHint/blob/3cf648ea19cb6db9cfb16bf26246cfcc49aedffd/src/core/rules/inline-script-disabled.ts#L12-L36) uses a fixed list of 22 `on*` names, plus `javascript:` in `src`/`href`.
- markuplint [`no-event-handler-attr`](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/@markuplint/rules/src/no-event-handler-attr/index.ts#L17-L50) matches `^on(.+)`. It explicitly exempts framework directives (`@click`, `x-on:`, `hx-on:`) because "they never reach the DOM as a literal inline-handler attribute". That is true for the event binding, but their *value* is still compiled as code.

**Go `html/template`** has the best-specified heuristic ([attr.go L140-175](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/ab269a42a408b8acaf038c811354a68139df38ba/src/html/template/attr.go#L140-L175)):

- It strips `data-` and any `ns:` prefix, then treats any remaining name that starts with `on` as JavaScript ("Treat partial event handler names as script", L156-159).
- As a result `x-on:click` becomes `click`, which is plain text: Go does not model Alpine or htmx.

**Python-side, Ruby-side and type-based checks:**

- Bandit is Python-side only: B308 blacklists `mark_safe`, and [B703](https://github.com/PyCQA/bandit/blob/68ebe11ef79263be27ec223184b94a4fc394a622/bandit/plugins/django_xss.py#L241-L250) checks what is passed to `mark_safe`. It does not read templates.
- Brakeman's XSS check "Checks for unescaped output in views" ([L17](https://github.com/presidentbeef/brakeman/blob/71f8f69a2e1e9df7e3c51c734c5f6feed083f640/lib/brakeman/checks/check_cross_site_scripting.rb#L17)). Escaped output is assumed safe whatever the HTML context.
- lit-analyzer's `securitySystem: "ClosureSafeTypes"` requires allow-listed *types* for `iframe/script/source src`, `a href`, `img src` and `style` ([L42-49](https://github.com/runem/lit-analyzer/blob/a518bf7c84ac338a7e8ab3db9dad006c06144426/packages/lit-analyzer/src/lib/rules/util/type/is-assignable-binding-under-security-system.ts#L42-L49)). It does not cover event handlers, but a configurable allowlist of trusted sanitizers is the same design as `safe-filters`.
- eslint-plugin-lit has no equivalent among its 26 rules ([docs/rules](https://github.com/43081j/eslint-plugin-lit/tree/2d71ea081dbec159f472c6905d16ddf24c2aa0e7/docs/rules)).

**Reusable test cases, adapted from these rules:**

- `on*` handler with `'{{ v }}'` → report.
- `'{{ v|escapejs }}'` → ok.
- `{{ v|escapejs }}` in bare position → report (the Rails lesson).
- `|safe` inside a JavaScript attribute → report.
- `href="{% url 'login' %}"`-style literal-only tags → ok.
- `<script src="…{{ x }}">`-style non-JavaScript attributes on the same element → ok.
- Unquoted `x-data={{ v }}` → report (the unquoted-attribute-var lesson).

### Design input for djangofmt

**Attributes to treat as JavaScript-evaluated.** Compare names case-insensitively; HTML lowercases attribute names. The Alpine and htmx sections give the full inventories.

- **HTML:** any `on[a-z]+`.
  - The HTML spec defines 96 handlers (`onabort` … `onwheel`, extracted from [webappapis](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#event-handlers-on-elements,-document-objects,-and-window-objects)). Other specs add more, such as pointer, animation and transition events.
  - The prefix test has no collisions: none of the 180 entries in the spec's "List of attributes (excluding event handler content attributes)" starts with `on` ([indices](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#attributes-3)).
  - Go and markuplint use the same prefix test.
  - Exclude `data-on…`, as Go's `data-` strip does.
  - `javascript:` URLs are the existing `javascript-url` rule.
- **Alpine core** (from `evaluate`/`evaluateLater` calls in `packages/alpinejs/src/directives/*.js`):
  - Evaluated: `x-data` (an empty value means `{}`), `x-init`, `x-show`, `x-if`, `x-text`, `x-html` (which is also an HTML sink), `x-effect`, `x-modelable`, `x-id`, `x-for` (the collection side), `x-model` (built as the l-value `${expression} = __placeholder`, [x-model.js L21](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives/x-model.js#L21)), `x-on:*` and `@*`, `x-bind:*` and `:*` (the shorthands are mapped in [x-on.js L6](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives/x-on.js#L6) and [x-bind.js L7](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives/x-bind.js#L7)), and the object form of `x-bind`.
  - **Not evaluated:** `x-ref`, `x-cloak`, `x-ignore`, `x-teleport` (a selector), and `x-transition:*`, which takes class strings and is evaluated only when the expression is a function supplied by an `x-bind` object ([x-transition.js L8-16](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives/x-transition.js#L8-L16)).
  - The prefix can be changed with `Alpine.prefix()` ([directives.js L6-14](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives.js#L6-L14)).
- **htmx 2** (each also with a `data-` prefix, [L417-419](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L417-L419)):
  - Always JavaScript: `hx-on:*` and `hx-on-*` ([L2806-2807](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2806-L2807)), and `hx-vars` ([L4038](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L4038)).
  - JavaScript when prefixed with `js:` or `javascript:`: `hx-vals`, `hx-headers` and `hx-request` (L3759, L4048, L4516).
  - The event filter `[…]` in `hx-trigger` ([L2209-2228](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2209-L2228)).
  - The docs' `allowEval` list, as confirmation: [docs.md L1674-1678](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/docs.md#L1674-L1678).
- **htmx 4:**
  - `hx-on:*`, plus the bare `hx-on="evt -> code"` form ([L1704-1737](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/htmx.js#L1704-L1737)).
  - `js:` or `javascript:` values on `hx-vals` and `hx-headers` (L445, L487, L1856-1872), `hx-confirm` (L563-565), `hx-get|post|put|patch|delete|query|action` (which *run the code instead of the request*, L513-517), and trigger filters (L803).
  - `hx-vars` was removed ([4.0.0 announcement L179](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/announcements/2026-08-28-htmx-4.0.0-is-released.md#L179)).
  - The prefix is configurable through `config.prefix` (L242-260).
  - So in htmx 4, a template value at the *start* of a verb attribute works like a `javascript:` URL. The hx-csp extension blocks it at runtime ([hx-csp.md L47](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/extensions/14-hx-csp.md#L47)).

**How to classify `{{ }}` and `{% %}` inside such a value:**

- **Variables `{{ var|f1|f2 }}`:** judge by the last filter, after stripping any trailing `|safe` that follows a sanitizer. Constants such as `{{ "…" }}` and `{{ 42 }}` are author-controlled, so skip them. Treat `{{ _("…") }}` like `{% translate %}`.
- **Known-safe variables:**
  - `forloop.counter`, `counter0`, `revcounter` and `revcounter0` are always ints ([defaulttags.py L218-222](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaulttags.py#L218-L222)).
  - `forloop.first` and `last` are bools, so they render `True` and break JavaScript.
  - `csrf_token` contains only letters and digits.
- **Structural tags: ignore, and keep lexing through them.** `if/elif/else/endif`, `for/empty/endfor`, `with`, `spaceless`, `comment`, `localize`, `language`, `ifchanged`, `load`, `verbatim`.
