  - `{% filter escapejs %}…{% endfilter %}` counts as a sanitizer of what it wraps (verified inert, though it double-encodes tag output).
  - `{% autoescape off %}` changes nothing about the JavaScript-context verdict.
- **Value-emitting tags:**
  - Safe:
    - `url` and `static` with literal-only arguments.
    - `now`, `widthratio` (always an int), `lorem` and `templatetag`.
    - `querystring`, which is URL-encoded ([defaulttags.py L1286-1349](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaulttags.py#L1286-L1349)) and therefore safe inside quotes only.
  - Unsafe:
    - `firstof`/`cycle` with variable arguments: they HTML-escape only, [L158](https://github.com/django/django/blob/951d13c77bc120be385c46fc701a1ad93694edd1/django/template/defaulttags.py#L158).
    - `translate`/`trans` without `as`, and `blocktranslate`/`blocktrans` without `asvar`.
    - `url` with variable arguments. It is exploitable only through `str`/`path` converters, which the linter cannot see, so there is a high false-positive risk.
  - Misuse: `csrf_token` renders an `<input>`.
  - Unknown: custom tags and `include`.

**Position model.** Scan the value with a small JavaScript lexer that treats `{{…}}`, `{%…%}` and `{#…#}` spans as opaque atoms.

- First decode `&quot; &#39; &#x27; &apos; &amp;` in the literal text, because the browser does.
- Track `'…'` and `"…"` strings with `\` escapes, template literals with `${}` nesting, and comments.
- Treat everything else, including regex literals that the lexer cannot tell apart from division, as bare position.
- Only a quoted string position is "string-safe".

**Default allowlist.**

- **String positions:** `escapejs`, `urlencode` with no argument, and `slugify`.
- **Any position:** filters whose output can never contain code: `length`, `wordcount`, `floatformat` (it still needs the `u` suffix to produce valid JavaScript under a localized decimal separator), `stringformat:"d"` (and `i`/`f`), and `yesno:"a,b"` with a literal argument.
- **Jinja profile:** `tojson` in any position, but only in `'`-quoted attributes or when followed by `forceescape`; also `int`, `float`, `length`/`count` and `wordcount`.
- **Not defensible as defaults:**
  - `unlocalize`: strings pass through unchanged.
  - `json_script`: report it as misuse instead.
  - `escape`, `force_escape`, `safe`, `safeseq`, `default`, `add`, `date`.

**False-positive traps:**

- `x-data="{{ component_name }}"` or `x-data="{{ name }}(…)"`: a server-chosen identifier in bare position. `escapejs` cannot help here; suppress it, or branch with `{% if %}` over literals.
- Integer IDs in bare position, such as `{{ obj.pk }}` or `{{ page.number }}`: the rule cannot know the type. The fix `|stringformat:"d"` documents it.
- `{% url 'x' obj.pk %}` inside a quoted string.
- Non-JavaScript Alpine attributes (`x-transition:*`, `x-ref`, `x-teleport`, and `x-mask`, which is a literal pattern unless it has the `:dynamic` or `:function` modifier, [mask/src/index.js L3-12](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/mask/src/index.js#L3-L12)).
- `hx-vals` JSON without `js:`.
- `data-*` in general. Keep `data-hx-*` in scope for htmx 2.
- Quotes inside `{{ … }}` within a `"`-quoted value, e.g. `'{{ v|default:"n/a"|escapejs }}'`. Whether markup_fmt keeps the `NativeAttribute` value intact across the inner `"` is unverified; cover it with a fixture.

**False negatives to document:**

- Custom tags, `{% include %}`, and `mark_safe`d JSON coming from Python.
- Attribute names built from variables, such as `x-on:{{ evt }}`, and attributes inside `{% if %}` at attribute level (depends on the markup_fmt AST; unverified).
- Changed prefixes (`Alpine.prefix`, htmx `config.prefix`).
- Values that are `escapejs`'d but reach a second sink: `x-html`, `setTimeout('…')`, or `location = '…'` with a `javascript:` URL.
- Regex and comment contexts.
- JSON injection in string-interpolated `hx-vals` and `hx-headers` JSON.

### Rule design recommendation

- **Rule.** Name it something like `unsafe-js-attr-value`, in category `Suspicious`, shipped as **preview**. It checks only attributes in the JavaScript-evaluated list above, and hooks in like `javascript_url::check` on `NativeAttribute`.
- **Report** each `{{ }}` or unsafe value tag, with the span of that interpolation, in four cases:
  1. It sits in a quoted JavaScript string and its last filter is not string-safe. Autoescape alone does not count.
  2. It sits in bare position, a template literal or a comment, and its last filter is not safe in any position. This includes `escapejs`, which gets a dedicated message.
  3. It uses `json_script`, `safe`, `safeseq` or `{% csrf_token %}` inside a JavaScript attribute. These are misuse and are always reported.
  4. It is a `translate` or `blocktranslate` output without `as`/`asvar`.
- **Also report** `x-html` values containing a template value even when `escapejs` is applied, because the string becomes HTML.
- **Do not report** constants, `forloop.counter*`, `csrf_token`, structural tags, literal-only `url`/`static`/`firstof`/`cycle`, or `url` with variable arguments (document that last one as a false negative).
- **Configuration.** Per-rule options, following the `unsorted-tailwind-classes` precedent. This corrects the issue's `[tool.djangofmt.lint] safe-js-filters`:
  ```toml
  [tool.djangofmt.lint.unsafe-js-attr-value]
  safe-filters = ["escape_alpine", "to_json"]  # extends the built-in defaults; trusted in any position
  ```
  - Make user filters extend the defaults rather than replace them, so `escapejs` cannot be dropped by accident.
  - Honor them in any position. The user vouches for their semantics, as with lit-analyzer's allow-listed safe types. A second, string-only list is not needed until someone asks for it.
  - Document that `unlocalize` only belongs there when the value is guaranteed numeric, and that `json_script` never does.
  - The option is pyproject-only, like Tailwind's `prefix`, so no CLI flag is needed.
- **Messages** (short, in the style of `javascript-url`):

  | Case                        | Message                                                                         | Help                                                                                                                                          |
  | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | Autoescape only             | "`{{ … }}` is only HTML-escaped in JavaScript attribute `x-data`"               | "The browser decodes HTML entities before the code runs; pass the value via a `data-*` attribute, or use `\|escapejs` inside a quoted string" |
  | Bare position               | "`{{ … }}` is inserted as JavaScript code in `x-init`"                          | "Put it in a quoted string with `\|escapejs`, cast numbers with `\|stringformat:"d"`, or read it from a `data-*` attribute"                   |
  | `escapejs` outside a string | "`escapejs` only protects values inside a quoted string literal"                | —                                                                                                                                             |
  | `json_script`               | "`json_script` renders a `<script>` element and cannot be used in an attribute" | "Render it outside the tag and read it with `JSON.parse(document.getElementById(…).textContent)`"                                             |
  | Translations                | "Translated text is not escaped"                                                | "`{% translate "…" as label %}` then `{{ label\|escapejs }}`"                                                                                 |

- **Fixtures, one per behaviour:**
  - `invalid`:
    - `x-data="{ n: '{{ v }}' }"`
    - `onclick="f('{{ v }}')"`
    - `x-init="c = {{ v|escapejs }}"`
    - ``x-text="`{{ v|escapejs }}`"``
    - `'{{ v|escapejs|default:w }}'`
    - `x-data="{{ v|json_script:'d' }}"`
    - `@click="f('{{ v|safe }}')"`
    - `'{% translate "x" %}'`
    - `hx-on:click="f('{{ v }}')"`
    - `hx-vals='js:{a: "{{ v }}"}'`
    - `x-html="'{{ v|escapejs }}'"`
  - `valid`:
    - `'{{ v|escapejs }}'`, also with `&quot;` delimiters
    - `{{ items|length }}` in bare position
    - `{{ forloop.counter }}`
    - `'{% url 'home' %}'`
    - `data-name="{{ v }}" @click="f($el.dataset.name)"`
    - `x-transition:enter="{{ cls }}"`
    - `hx-vals='{"a": "{{ v|escapejs }}"}'`
    - a custom filter listed in `safe-filters`

## D. markuplint

Versions examined (2026-09-23):

- markuplint monorepo, default branch `dev` at [`7c3c510a`](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/tree/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946), with every package at **5.0.1** (npm, published 2026-09-22).
- The last v4 tag, `v4.18.3`, at [`c53026c2`](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/tree/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62).
- The Rust-port branch `v6` at [`15a4d001`](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/tree/15a4d00120c668beb654a61a1057ecfe12ac9256).

Framework sources used for comparison:

- Alpine 3.17.4 at [`20ee0e92`](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/tree/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d).
- htmx v2.0.11 at [`fa978b24`](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/tree/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1).
- htmx v4.0.0 at [`4195bc0d`](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/tree/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3).

Behaviour claims marked **(ran)** come from running the real CLIs on sample files. The two setups were:

- `markuplint@5.0.1` with `@markuplint/{alpine-parser,alpine-spec,htmx-spec}@5.0.1`.
- `markuplint@4.18.3` with `@markuplint/{alpine-parser,htmx-parser}@4.18.3`.

Both ran on Node 26.4.0, in a throwaway harness.

Shorthand used in the links below: `ML` = `https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint`.

### 1. Framework support: what exists for Alpine.js and htmx

| Package (5.0.1)             | Role                                                                                      | What the npm tarball holds                          |
| --------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `@markuplint/alpine-spec`   | `ExtendedSpec` data: 6 directive patterns, 22 global attributes, 4 element patches        | `lib/index.js`, 9,629 B. Compiled JS only, no JSON. |
| `@markuplint/alpine-parser` | Subclass of the HTML parser. Its only job is turning `<template x-for>` into a loop block | `lib/*.js`, about 2.5 KB                            |
| `@markuplint/htmx-spec`     | `ExtendedSpec` data: 3 `hx-on` patterns, 33 global attributes                             | `lib/index.js`, 9,323 B. Compiled JS only.          |
| `@markuplint/htmx-parser`   | **Removed in v5.** It used to resolve `hx-on`. The last release is 4.18.3.                | —                                                   |

**History.**

- Alpine support landed in PR [#1459](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/1459) (2024-02) and htmx support in PR [#1693](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/1693) (2024-05).
- In v4 both shipped as a *parser* with a `/spec` sub-export.
- v5 replaced this with "parser-less" spec packages: PR [#3264](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/3264) (merged 2026-02-20) added `directivePatterns`, deleted `htmx-parser` and cut `alpine-parser` down to `<template x-for>` handling.
- This implements RFC [#1789](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/1789): "HTMX and Alpine maintain HTML syntax but have unique handling of attributes. Therefore, it is necessary to clearly separate syntax parsing from attribute handling."
- The migration guide says: "`@markuplint/htmx-parser` is gone. Use `@markuplint/htmx-spec` (no parser entry)… Keep `@markuplint/alpine-parser` for `<template x-for>`" ([framework.md L10-29](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/website/docs/migration/v4-to-v5/framework.md#L10-L29)).
- Several specs can apply to one file. When two `specs` regex keys both match `.html`, the Alpine and htmx specs merge **(ran)**.

**What `alpine-spec` encodes** ([ML/alpine-spec/src/index.ts](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts)). All patterns are case-insensitive by default.

| Line                                                                                                                                                  | Pattern                                                                               | Metadata                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [L38](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L37-L40) | `^x-(?:data\|init\|show\|text\|html\|modelable\|effect\|ref\|id\|for\|if\|teleport)$` | `isDirective`                                                                                                                                                     |
| [L45](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L44-L48) | `^x-(?:ignore\|cloak)$`                                                               | `isDirective`, `valueType: 'boolean'`                                                                                                                             |
| [L54](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L53-L57) | `^x-model(?:$\|\.)` (any modifiers)                                                   | `isDirective`, `isDynamicValue`                                                                                                                                   |
| [L63](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L62-L68) | `^(?:x-bind:\|:)([^.]+)(?:\.[^.]+)?$`                                                 | `potentialName: '$1'`, `isDynamicValue`, `valueType:'code'`, `isDuplicatable: ['class','style']`. This is **not** a directive, so `:href` is validated as `href`. |
| [L74](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L73-L78) | `^(?:x-on:\|@)([^.]+)(?:\..+)?$` (any modifiers)                                      | `potentialName: 'on$1'`, `isDirective`, `isDynamicValue`                                                                                                          |
| [L84](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L83-L86) | `^x-transition(?:$\|:\|\.)`                                                           | `isDirective`                                                                                                                                                     |

- **Element patches** ([L88-L136](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L88-L136)):
  - `template` gets `x-for`, `x-teleport` and `x-if` (`NoEmptyAny`), plus `key` with `condition: '[x-for]'`.
  - `input` gets `x-model` with `condition: '[type=text i], [type=checkbox i], [type=radio i], [type=range i]'`.
  - `select` and `textarea` get `x-model`.
- **Global attributes** ([L137-L290](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L137-L290)):
  - The 22 names are `x-data … x-cloak`, including `x-transition:enter{,-start,-end}` and `x-transition:leave*`.
  - All are typed `Any`, except `x-ignore` and `x-cloak`, which are `Boolean`.
- **No value validation.** There are no enums and no JSON.
- **No modifier validation.** Any `.suffix` is accepted.
- **No deprecations.**

**What `htmx-spec` encodes** ([ML/htmx-spec/src/index.ts](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts)).

- `cites: ['https://htmx.org/reference/']` ([L30](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L30)).
- There are three `hx-on` patterns ([L31-L62](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L31-L62)):
  - `^hx-on([:-])htmx\1(.+)$` → `hx-on:htmx:$2`. This one uses a **backreference**.
  - `^hx-on[:-]{2}(.+)$` → `hx-on:htmx:$1`.
  - `^hx-on[:-]([a-z]+)$` → `on$1`.
  - All three are `isDirective` and `isDynamicValue`.
- The spec lists 33 `hx-*` global attributes, all typed `Any` except five `Boolean`s: `hx-boost` [L141](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L141-L143), `hx-disable` [L238](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L238-L240), `hx-history-elt`, `hx-preserve`, `hx-validate` [L268](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L268-L270).
- `hx-swap` and `hx-vals` are `Any`, with the value grammar only described in comments ([L117](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L112-L119), [L171](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-spec/src/index.ts#L168-L173)).
- There is no `<hx-partial>`, no extension attributes (`sse-connect`, `ws-connect`), and no `hx-vars`.

**The v4 data was richer, and v5 dropped it.**

- v4.18.3's `htmx-parser/src/spec.ts` ([445 lines](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-parser/src/spec.ts)) typed values:
  - `hx-boost`, `hx-validate`, `hx-push-url` and `hx-replace-url` were `{enum:['true','false']}`, and the last two also accepted a URL.
  - `hx-get`, `hx-post` and the other verbs were `URL`. `hx-headers` was `JSON`.
  - `hx-vals` was `['JSON', {directive: ['/^js:(?<token>.+)$/', '/^javascript:(?<token>.+)$/'], token: 'Any'}]` ([L414-L423](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-parser/src/spec.ts#L414-L423)). That is exactly the requester's "hx-vals must be JSON or `js:`" rule.
  - `hx-target` was selector-or-keyword: `<complex-selector-list>` \| `this`/`next`/`previous` \| `closest|find|next|previous <selector>` ([L375-L391](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-parser/src/spec.ts#L375-L391)). `hx-include`, `hx-indicator` and `hx-disabled-elt` used the same scheme.
  - `hx-ext` was a comma list over 22 known extensions plus `ignore:*` ([L11-L34](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-parser/src/spec.ts#L11-L34), [L155-L170](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/htmx-parser/src/spec.ts#L155-L170)).
  - `hx-swap` and `hx-trigger` were marked "Too complex to define".
- Likewise, v4's Alpine parser deliberately left `x-model`, `x-for`, `x-teleport` and `x-if` **non-directive**. The code says: "Treat as a normal attribute and allow only in template elements as defined in `spec`" ([alpine-parser L82-L105, L145-L159](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/c53026c2c600bb304f2088589f67303479e92e62/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-parser/src/parser.ts#L82-L105)). That is how the per-element specs were enforced.
- v5 lists these four names in the directive patterns *and* adds them as global attributes, so the constraints are no longer enforced (see the table below).
- PR #3264's description does not explain why the value types were dropped (rationale unverified).

**Behaviour on the requester's cases (ran).**

| Input                                                                                                                                                                                                    | v5.0.1 result                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | v4.18.3 result                                                             |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `<div x-cheeseburger>` / `<div hx-cheeseburger>`                                                                                                                                                         | reported by `no-unknown-attr`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | reported by `invalid-attr`                                                 |
| `@click`, `@click.prevent.stop`, `x-on:keyup.enter`, `@my-custom-event.window`, `x-transition:enter.duration.500ms`                                                                                      | accepted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | accepted                                                                   |
| `:hreff="u"` on `<a>`                                                                                                                                                                                    | "`hreff` … Did you mean `href`?"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | –                                                                          |
| `<div x-for>`, `<div x-if>`, `<template x-html>`                                                                                                                                                         | **not reported**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | x-for and x-if reported                                                    |
| `<div x-model>`, `<input type=email x-model>`                                                                                                                                                            | **not reported**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | both reported                                                              |
| `:key` on `<div>` (with or without x-for)                                                                                                                                                                | "`key` attribute is disallowed"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | –                                                                          |
| `<template x-for>` with 2 root elements                                                                                                                                                                  | not checked. A `<li>` inside `<div><template x-for>` is reported by `permitted-contents` only because the loop body is checked against the parent's content model                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | –                                                                          |
| `x-bind="trigger"` (the documented object form, [bind.md L160-L171](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/directives/bind.md#L160-L171)) | **false positive**: "`x-bind` … Did you mean `tabindex`?"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | –                                                                          |
| `x-intersect`, `x-trap`, `x-collapse`, `x-mask`, `x-anchor`, `x-sort`, `x-resize` (official plugins); `x-load`, `x-load-src` (Async Alpine)                                                              | **all reported as unknown**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | –                                                                          |
| `@click` and `@click.prevent` on one element                                                                                                                                                             | **false positive** from `no-duplicate-attr`, because both resolve to `onclick`. The rule's autofix deletes one ([no-duplicate-attr L14-L29](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-duplicate-attr/index.ts#L14-L29))                                                                                                                                                      | –                                                                          |
| `x-cloak="true"`, `hx-boost="true"`, `hx-validate="true"`                                                                                                                                                | `no-boolean-attr-value` warning. For hx-boost and hx-validate this is a **false positive**: htmx's docs use `hx-boost="true"` and `hx-validate="true"` ([hx-boost.md L26](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/attributes/hx-boost.md#L26), [hx-validate.md L11](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/www/content/attributes/hx-validate.md#L11)) | enum, accepted                                                             |
| `hx-swap="bogus"`, `hx-vals='{"a": 1'`, `hx-headers="{bad}"`, `hx-ext="nope"`                                                                                                                            | **not reported**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | hx-vals, hx-headers and hx-ext reported. hx-swap is `Any` in both versions |
| `hx-on:my-event`, `hx-on-custom-thing`                                                                                                                                                                   | **false positive** (unknown attribute)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | same false positive                                                        |
| `hx-on::before:request`, `hx-on:htmx:after:swap` (htmx 4 event syntax)                                                                                                                                   | accepted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | –                                                                          |
| `hx-on="load -> …"`, `hx-boost:inherited`, `hx-include:inherited:append` (htmx 4)                                                                                                                        | **false positives**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | –                                                                          |
| `hx-method`, `hx-action`, `hx-config`, `hx-status:404`, `hx-ignore`, `hx-optimistic`, `hx-preload` (htmx 4 names; `hx-optimistic` was a guess and is not in the 4.0.0 docs)                              | all reported as unknown                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | –                                                                          |
| `<hx-partial hx-cheeseburger foo>`                                                                                                                                                                       | not reported: hyphenated names count as custom elements and are skipped                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | –                                                                          |

On the `hx-on:my-event` row: htmx 2 accepts any event name after `hx-on:` or `hx-on-` ([htmx.js L2970-L2995](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/src/htmx.js#L2970-L2995)). markuplint's `[a-z]+` rejects dashed names.

**Coverage against upstream.**

- **Alpine core.** Alpine 3.17.4 registers 18 directives: `bind cloak data effect for html id if ignore init model modelable on ref show teleport text transition` ([packages/alpinejs/src/directives/](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/tree/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives)). markuplint covers all 18.
- **Alpine plugins.** None are covered: `anchor`, `collapse`, `intersect`, `mask`, `sort`, `resize` and `trap` are each registered via `Alpine.directive(...)`, e.g. [intersect/src/index.js L2](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/intersect/src/index.js#L2) and [focus/src/index.js L99](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/focus/src/index.js#L99). Async Alpine is not covered either.
- **Alpine element rules not checked in v5:**
  - "`x-for` MUST be declared on a `<template>` element. That `<template>` element MUST contain only one root element" ([for.md L52-L55](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/directives/for.md#L52-L55)).
  - The same rules for `x-if` ([if.md L10, L24](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/directives/if.md#L10-L24)).
  - The element list for `x-model` ([model.md L58-L65](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/docs/src/en/directives/model.md#L58-L65)).
- **htmx 2.0.11** has 36 attribute doc pages. markuplint lacks only `hx-vars` (deprecated) and the `<hx-partial>` tag, which 2.0.11 backported from 4.0 ([CHANGELOG L5](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/CHANGELOG.md#L5)).
- **htmx 4.0.0, missing from markuplint:** `hx-action hx-config hx-ignore hx-method hx-morph-skip hx-morph-skip-children hx-pending hx-preload hx-query hx-status`, bare `hx-on`, and the `:inherited`/`:append` suffixes ([whats-new-in-htmx-4.md L13-L41](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L13-L41)).
- **htmx 4.0.0, present in markuplint but removed or renamed upstream:** `hx-disabled-elt hx-disinherit hx-ext hx-history hx-inherit hx-params hx-prompt hx-request` ([L126-L139](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/whats-new-in-htmx-4.md#L126-L139)).
- In htmx 4, `hx-disable` now takes a selector (the old `hx-disabled-elt` role), so markuplint's `Boolean` typing is wrong for htmx 4 ([hx-disable.md L6-L11](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/25-hx-disable.md#L6-L11)).

### 2. How markuplint parses and resolves framework attributes

- **AST fields.** `MLASTHTMLAttr` ([ML/ml-ast/src/types.ts L318-L352](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-ast/src/types.ts#L318-L352)) carries:
  - raw tokens for the name, `=`, the quotes and the value;
  - `isDynamicValue?`, "Whether the value is a dynamic expression";
  - `isDirective?`, "a framework directive (e.g. `v-if`, `@click`)";
  - `potentialName?`, "Vue's `:class` resolves to `class`, `@click` to `onclick`";
  - `potentialValue?`;
  - `valueType?: 'string'|'number'|'boolean'|'code'`;
  - `candidate?`, "A candidate attribute name for auto-correction";
  - `isDuplicatable`.
- **Resolution order** ([ML/ml-core/src/ml-dom/node/attr.ts L139-L188](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-core/src/ml-dom/node/attr.ts#L139-L188)):
  1. A `potentialName` set by the parser wins.
  2. Otherwise the raw name is run through the spec's `directivePatterns`.
  3. Otherwise the raw name is used.
  4. Last comes IDL name mapping (`className`→`class`), but only if `acceptedAttrNames` is set and the attribute is not a directive.

  `attr.name` then *returns the resolved name*, so every rule sees `href` for `:href` and `onclick` for `@click`.
- **Directive pattern semantics** ([ML/ml-spec/src/utils/directive-resolver.ts L14-L73](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/utils/directive-resolver.ts#L14-L73)):
  - Patterns are JS `RegExp` strings with flags `'i'` by default.
  - The **first match wins**, and merged specs *concatenate* their pattern lists.
  - `$n` capture groups are substituted **lowercased** (L52-L54).
  - `isDuplicatable` can list resolved names.

  Vue's spec warns that new patterns "must be inserted before the generic `^v-` catch-all" ([ML/vue-spec/src/index.ts L26-L82](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/vue-spec/src/index.ts#L26-L82)). Svelte uses the same mechanism for `bind:`, `on:`, `class:` and `style:` ([svelte-spec L58-L96](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/svelte-spec/src/index.ts#L58-L96)).
- **What parsers still do.** Since v5, parsers only handle syntax:
  - The Vue parser's header says directive resolution "is intentionally not performed by this parser" ([vue-parser/src/parser.ts L12-L32](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/vue-parser/src/parser.ts#L12-L32)).
  - The Alpine parser only rewrites `<template x-for>` into a `psblock` with `blockBehavior: {type: 'each'}` ([alpine-parser/src/index.ts L34-L68](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-parser/src/index.ts#L34-L68)). The loop body is therefore checked against the *parent's* content model: `<dl><template x-for><dt>` requires a `dd` ([permitted-contents test L1686-L1720](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/permitted-contents/index.spec.ts#L1686-L1720)).
- **Template-engine code in values.** Masked `{{ }}`/`{% %}` blocks inside a value set `isDynamicValue: true` ([parser-utils/src/ignore-block.ts L196-L216](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/parser-utils/src/ignore-block.ts#L196-L216)), which is the same skip as djangofmt's `contains_interpolation`.
- **Template-engine code between attributes.** A block tag between attributes (`<input {% if a %}disabled{% endif %}>` with `nunjucks-parser`) produced a bogus attribute: "The "<!" attribute is disallowed" **(ran)**. markup_fmt's Django AST handles that case natively.

**How rules use the flags:**

| Flag                              | Effect                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `isDirective`                     | The attribute is skipped entirely by `no-unknown-attr`, `no-disallowed-attr`, `no-invalid-attr-value`, `no-restricted-attr` and `no-event-handler-attr` ([no-unknown-attr L54-L57](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-unknown-attr/index.ts#L54-L57), [no-restricted-attr L62-L64](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-restricted-attr/index.ts#L62-L64), [no-event-handler-attr L44-L50](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-event-handler-attr/index.ts#L44-L50)). As a result, `disallowAttrs: ["x-html"]` cannot ban `x-html` **(ran)**.                                                                                               |
| `isDynamicValue`                  | Value checks are skipped ([no-invalid-attr-value L99-L101](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-invalid-attr-value/index.ts#L99-L101), [no-boolean-attr-value L17-L19](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-boolean-attr-value/index.ts#L17-L19)). `tokenList` is `null` ([attr.ts L288-L290](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-core/src/ml-dom/node/attr.ts#L288-L290)). Attribute `condition` selectors that reference a dynamic attribute are treated as undecidable ([rules/src/helpers.ts L22-L44](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/helpers.ts#L22-L44)). |
| `potentialName` (not a directive) | The resolved name is validated against the element's spec, which is why `:hreff` is caught.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `isDuplicatable`                  | `no-duplicate-attr` skips the attribute.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `candidate`                       | Parser- or core-supplied "Did you mean" for JSX IDL names. Spec typos use a Levenshtein ratio ≥ 0.5 over *all* attribute names ([types/src/get-candidate.ts L28-L35](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/types/src/get-candidate.ts#L28-L35)). This produced odd hints such as `x-trap`→`x-text` and `x-resize`→`onresize` **(ran)**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

**Two consequences of resolving names:**

- `case-sensitive-attr-name` skips any attribute whose raw name differs from its resolved name ([L31-L33](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/case-sensitive-attr-name/index.ts#L31-L33)). `@myEvent` is silently lowercased and never warned about, although the requester wants a warning for capitals in `x-on` event names.
- `isDynamicValue` does not mean "evaluated as JS". `x-data`, `x-init`, `x-show` and `x-text` are only `isDirective`, while the `x-transition:*` values are CSS classes. markuplint has no flag a "safe filter" rule could rely on.

### 3. Spec data model

- **Types** ([ML/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts)):
  - `MLMLSpec {cites, def, specs, directivePatterns?, acceptedAttrNames?}` ([L11-L27](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts#L11-L27)).
  - `SpecDefs {'#globalAttrs', '#aria' (1.1/1.2/1.3), '#contentModels'}` ([L123-L135](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts#L123-L135)).
  - `ElementSpec` ([L147-L237](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts#L147-L237)) has these fields: name, namespace, cite, description, experimental, obsolete (`true|{alt}`), deprecated, nonStandard, categories, contentModel, omission, globalAttrs, attributes, aria, possibleToAddProperties.
  - `Attribute` ([L253-L270](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts#L253-L270)) has `type` plus the flags `caseSensitive`, `experimental`, `obsolete`, `deprecated` and `nonStandard`.
  - The JSON-schema fields are `defaultValue`, `required` (bool or selector), `requiredEither`, `noUse`, `condition` (CSS selector), `ineffective` and `animatable` ([attributes.ts L1649-L1663](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/attributes.ts#L1649-L1663)).
- **`index.json` contents** (@5.0.1, 1,946,364 B):
  - 237 element specs: 141 HTML, 64 SVG, 32 MathML.
  - 20 global-attribute categories. For example `#HTMLGlobalAttrs` has 37 entries and `#GlobalEventAttrs` has 94.
  - Elements pick categories with `true` (all) or a subset array. For example `button.globalAttrs['#HTMLFormControlElementAttrs'] = ['disabled','form',…]`, and `button.attributes.disabled` then holds only an MDN description. **The type lives in the category.**
  - About 301 HTML-namespace attribute entries have no type, and the merge fills in `Any`.
  - Merge semantics ([get-attr-specs-spec.ts L43-L96](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/utils/get-attr-specs-spec.ts#L43-L96)): selected categories, then element attributes spread over them, then `{name, type:'Any', ...}`.
  - Attribute `condition`s are real CSS selectors, about 60 unique ones: `[type='email' i]`, `:not([itemprop])`, `picture > source[srcset]`, `a[href] img`, `:is(:not([type]), …)`. Content models are selector-based DSLs (`{"oneOrMore": ":model(phrasing):not(:model(interactive), :has(:model(interactive)))"}`), so they need a selector engine.
- **Boolean attributes** (relevant to #425):
  - 35 HTML-namespace entries are typed `"Boolean"`, covering 32 distinct names.
  - These are exactly the 30 distinct names the WHATWG attribute index marks "Boolean attribute", plus `focusgroupstart` (experimental) and `input[switch]` (non-standard). The index was checked by scraping [indices.html#attributes-3](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#attributes-3), dated "Last Updated 22 September 2026".
- **Enum attributes.** There are 41 enum-typed entries, for example `form[method]={enum:["post","get","dialog"], invalidValueDefault:"get"}`.
  - The checker defaults are `caseInsensitive = true` and `disallowToSurroundBySpaces = true` ([types/src/enum.ts L8-L9](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/types/src/enum.ts#L8-L9)).
  - These defaults are code semantics a Rust consumer must copy.
- **Extending the spec.** `ExtendedSpec = {cites?, def?, specs?: ExtendedElementSpec[], directivePatterns?, acceptedAttrNames?}` ([L43-L53](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/types/index.ts#L34-L53)). `schemaToSpec` ([schema-to-spec.ts L5-L133](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/ml-spec/src/utils/schema-to-spec.ts#L5-L133)) merges it as follows:
  - `def['#globalAttrs']['#extends']` is folded into `#HTMLGlobalAttrs`, so framework attributes apply to every element that includes it; SVG elements do too.
  - Pattern lists are concatenated.
  - Element specs **only patch existing elements**. `specs: [{name:'foo'}]` is silently dropped **(ran)**.
  - The merge is a "silent, unconditional override" with no conflict detection, tracked in issue [#3893](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/3893).
  - In config, the mapping is `"specs": {"<regex>": "<package or ./file.js>"}` ([properties.md L245-L259](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/website/docs/configuration/properties.md#L245-L259)).
- **`@markuplint/types`** ([types.schema.json](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/types/types.schema.json)). The value DSL has 6 forms:
  - keyword;
  - `list` (`token` + `separator: space|comma`, plus `unique`, `ordered`, `number` and `caseInsensitive`);
  - `enum` (plus `invalidValueDefault`, `missingValueDefault` and `sameStates`);
  - `number` (`float|integer`, `gt/gte/lt/lte`, `clampable`);
  - `directive` (regex prefixes plus a token type);
  - `pattern` (`/re/` or an exact string).

  The keyword set is `Boolean`, 86 "extended" types (`URL`, `DOMID`, `BCP47`, `MIMEType`, `Srcset`, `JSON`, `DateTime`, …) and 1,204 CSS syntaxes. The *forms* are data, but every keyword is **JS code**:
  - `JSON` runs `JSON.parse` ([defs.ts L129-L145](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/types/src/defs.ts#L129-L145)).
  - `FunctionBody` is "NO IMPLEMENT PLAN NOW" ([L191-L201](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/types/src/defs.ts#L191-L201)).
  - CSS types go through `css-tree`'s lexer.
  - `Boolean` always validates, "Valid because an attribute is exist" ([rules/src/attr-check.ts L127-L130](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/attr-check.ts#L127-L130)). So `checked="false"` is only a style warning, although WHATWG says "The values "true" and "false" are not allowed on boolean attributes" ([§2.3.2](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/common-microsyntaxes.html#boolean-attributes)).

### 4. How `@markuplint/html-spec` is generated

- **Where the generator lives.** The separate `@markuplint/spec-generator` package ended at v4.18.0 (2026-04-22). v5 moved it into `html-spec/generator/` ([CHANGELOG L92, L107-L110](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/CHANGELOG.md#L92-L110)). It runs as `node build.ts` → `index.json` → `oxfmt`.
- **Scope.** The generator's job is "external data fetching and enrichment ONLY" ([generator/index.ts L8-L20](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/index.ts#L8-L20)).
- **What it scrapes with cheerio:**
  - The MDN page for each element (`…/Web/{HTML,SVG,MathML}/Reference/Element(s)/<name>`, [html-elements.ts L90](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/html-elements.ts#L90)). From it come the description, content categories, attribute `<dt>` lists and the experimental/deprecated/obsolete/non-standard badges ([scraping.ts L41-L212](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/scraping.ts#L41-L212)).
  - The MDN SVG and MathML index pages, for their deprecated lists.
  - The W3C WAI-ARIA 1.1 and 1.2 TRs, the 1.3 editor's draft, Graphics-ARIA and DPub-ARIA ([aria.ts L53, L125-L146](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/aria.ts#L125-L146)).
  - ARIA-in-HTML ([L443](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/aria.ts#L443)).
  - The HTML Living Standard itself is **not** fetched.
- **What is written by hand.** The types, enums, conditions, content models, ARIA mappings and `globalAttrs` are hand-authored in 212 `src/*.jsonc` files, with spec URLs as comments. Hand-written data "overrides same-named MDN-scraped data"; MDN only fills gaps ([html-spec/CLAUDE.md L15-L20](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/CLAUDE.md#L15-L20); [html-elements.ts L54-L64](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/html-elements.ts#L54-L64)).
- **Guard rails:**
  - The element count must stay ≥ 85% of the previous run, empty descriptions must stay ≤ 50%, and a list of required elements and roles must be present ([validate.ts L13-L53](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/html-spec/generator/validate.ts#L13-L53)).
  - Regeneration must be idempotent.
  - Mass flag flips are to be treated as "a scraper regression, not an MDN update".
- **Cadence.** A cron job runs at `0 0 * * *` and `0 12 * * *` (twice a day) plus manual dispatch. It runs `yarn up:gen` and opens or refreshes a PR on `auto/update-html-spec` with a change summary ([update-html-spec.yml L3-L7, L51-L52, L110-L120](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/.github/workflows/update-html-spec.yml#L3-L120)). There have been 23 such PRs. Recent merges were on 2026-08-13, 08-30 and 09-11, and [#4042](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/4042) has been open since 09-12. In practice data lands every 1–4 weeks.
- **What this means for djangofmt.** The precedent is "scrape + hand-written overrides + an idempotency check + a CI bot PR". None of markuplint's framework data is generated: alpine-spec and htmx-spec are hand-written TS with no tests ("conformance is enforced at build time by the `ExtendedSpec` type", [alpine-spec L13-L17](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/alpine-spec/src/index.ts#L13-L17)).

### 5. Relevant rules, options, edge cases, issues

- **`invalid-attr` in v5** ([rule-aliases.ts L87-L107](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/rule-aliases.ts#L87-L107); [migration invalid-attr.md L8-L26](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/website/docs/migration/v4-to-v5/rules/invalid-attr.md#L8-L26)). v5 split it into four rules; the old name still works as a deprecated alias until v6.
  - `no-unknown-attr`: the name is not in the spec.
  - `no-disallowed-attr`: the name is known but not allowed here (`noUse`, an unmet `condition`, or `is` on an autonomous custom element).
  - `no-invalid-attr-value`: the value fails its type.
  - `no-restricted-attr`: only the user's `disallowAttrs`.
- **Shared exemptions** ([attr-eligibility.ts L17, L42-L93](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/attr-eligibility.ts#L17-L93)):
  - Names matching `/^(?:data-.+|adapt-.+|aria-.+|role)$/` are exempt, so `data-hx-*` is never checked.
  - The rest go through lookup, then a case-mismatch check, then `noUse`, then the conditional type, then `condition`.
- **Options** ([no-unknown-attr L79-L129](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-unknown-attr/index.ts#L79-L129)):
  - `allowAttrs`: exact names, or `{name, value: Type|{pattern}}`.
  - `ignoreAttrNamePrefix`.
  - `allowToAddPropertiesForPretender`.
  - Elements that are not `html` (custom elements) or have no spec are skipped. So are elements with `possibleToAddProperties`.
  - The maintainer's advice for element-restricted `data-*` attributes is `nodeRules` plus `disallowAttrs` (see [#3353](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/3353)).
- **`no-boolean-attr-value`** ([index.ts L17-L55](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-boolean-attr-value/index.ts#L17-L55)):
  - The category is `style`, severity `warning`, and it is fixable.
  - It fires when `spec.type === 'Boolean'` and anything follows the name. That includes `=""` and `="false"`; `required=""`, `disabled="disabled"` and `checked="false"` were all reported **(ran)**.
  - Dynamic values are skipped. The fix removes the `=value` tokens.
  - Its README says it is "Not included in any preset: HTML permits a boolean attribute to carry any value" ([README L12-L14](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/packages/%40markuplint/rules/src/no-boolean-attr-value/README.md#L10-L14)). HTML does in fact forbid any value other than `""` or the attribute's own name.
- **`permitted-contents` and `<template>`:**
  - `template` has `contentModel: {contents: true}`.
  - No rule counts template roots.
  - The only Alpine-aware structural behaviour is the x-for loop block described in §2.
- **Issues** (all 4,040 issues and PRs were listed via the API and filtered locally):
  - [#1156](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/1156) and [#1405](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/1405): the original support requests.
  - [#682](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/682) and [#1789](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/1789): the attribute/directive re-architecture ("directives, shorthand, modifiers, duplicability, aliases").
  - [#3264](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/3264) and [#3269](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/3269): the `directivePatterns` implementation.
  - [#4044](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/4044) and [#4047](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/4047) (Sept 2026): framework event directives were being reported by `no-event-handler-attr`. The fix skips every `isDirective`.
  - [#3893](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/3893): spec overlays can silently weaken HTML checks.
  - [#3050](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/3050) and [#3353](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/pull/3353): a Datastar spec written by **UnknownPlatypus** (djangofmt's maintainer). The PR was closed unmerged. The maintainer explained that "patterns resolve names, `def` declares types", said `data-*` cannot be restricted at the spec level, and suggested "an **external npm package**" for fast-moving frameworks.
  - No issue asks for Alpine plugins, htmx 4, hx-swap validation or the x-for-on-template regression. That is unverified beyond this title/body filter.

### 6. Reuse verdict for a Rust project

**Licences and attribution.**

- Every package is MIT, "Copyright (c) 2017-2024 Yusuke Hirao" ([LICENSE](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/7c3c510a3be6de65bb470bf723630129fb8f5946/LICENSE)). It is compatible with djangofmt's MIT but requires keeping the notice with "all copies or substantial portions".
- `index.json` also contains MDN-scraped `description` prose. MDN prose is "CC-BY-SA, v2.5 or any later version" ([MDN licensing](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/MDN/Writing_guidelines/Attrib_copyright_license)). **Drop descriptions** from anything generated.
- The underlying facts come from WHATWG: "Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 … portions … incorporated into source code … are licensed under the BSD 3-Clause License" ([acknowledgements](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/acknowledgements.html)).

**What is reusable, and how:**

| Artefact                                                  | Self-contained?                                                                                                                | Rust strategy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `html-spec/index.json` (tarball 241 KB, unpacked 1.95 MB) | The JSON is complete, but its meaning depends on ml-spec merge code, types keyword code and selector evaluation                | **Generate.** `djangofmt_dev` fetches `https://registry.npmjs.org/@markuplint/html-spec/-/html-spec-<pin>.tgz`, deserialises it with serde, reimplements `getAttrSpecs` (about 40 lines), and emits category sets plus per-element deltas: names, a Boolean flag, enums and deprecated/obsolete flags. A naive per-element expansion is about 310 KB of JSON. Serde structs already exist in markuplint v6 ([types.rs L111-L244](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/15a4d00120c668beb654a61a1057ecfe12ac9256/crates/markuplint-types/src/spec/types.rs#L111-L244)). |
| `condition` selectors (~60) and content models            | Data, but a DSL                                                                                                                | Reimplement. Either compile the small selector subset (`[attr=val i]`, `:not`, `:is`, `>`, descendant) into predicates, or leave it out at first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| alpine-spec / htmx-spec                                   | Data, but shipped as JS; `node -e 'import(...).then(m=>JSON.stringify(m.default))'` yields 2.9 KB and 2.1 KB of JSON **(ran)** | Too small, stale and loosely typed to automate against. Take the *design* and the v4 htmx value grammars instead. Porting gotchas: JS regex semantics (`'i'` by default, `$n` lowercased), and Rust `regex` "lacks … look-around and backreferences" ([docs.rs/regex 1.13.1](https://docs.rs/regex/latest/regex/)), which breaks `^hx-on([:-])htmx\1(.+)$`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `@markuplint/types` checkers (URL, BCP47, Srcset, CSS, …) | JS code                                                                                                                        | Reimplement, or watch v6's `crates/markuplint-types`. It is a Rust port (MIT; `url`, `mime`, `language-tags`; checkers such as `"JSON" => check_json`), but it is `publish = false` at version `0.0.0` on an unreleased branch ([Cargo.toml L1-L5](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/blob/15a4d00120c668beb654a61a1057ecfe12ac9256/crates/markuplint-types/Cargo.toml#L1-L5); RFC [#3178](https://github.com/markuplint/markuplint/issues/3178)).                                                                                                                       |

### 7. Other machine-readable HTML sources (brief)

- **html-validate 11.16.0** (MIT, GitLab [`552e0d34`](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/elements/html5.ts)):
  - The metadata is a 3,729-line hand-maintained **TS module**, not JSON. It uses helper calls (`allowedIfAttributeHasValue`), regex enums, and 49 per-node callback functions such as `focusable(node) {…}` and `interactive(node) {…}`.
  - Attributes carry `boolean`, `enum`, `omit`, `deprecated`, `required` and `list`. Only 29 of WHATWG's 30 boolean names are present; `allowfullscreen` is missing from `iframe`.
  - Its experimental `no-unknown-attributes` rule (since 10.17.0) hard-skips `/^:/, /^@/, /^ng-/i, /^v-/i, /^x-/i, /^\[/, /^#/` ([no-unknown-attributes.ts L10](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/rules/no-unknown-attributes.ts#L10)). So it ignores all Alpine attributes and does not know htmx.
  - A generator would have to execute JS and drop the callbacks.
- **VS Code custom data** (`@vscode/web-custom-data` 0.6.3, MIT code; MDN-derived text is CC BY-SA 2.5 per [README](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-custom-data/blob/01df47ad7cc0b140624fc941ff57618c36ed46a1/web-data/README.md)):
  - `browsers.html-data.json` (458 KB) has 116 tags, 150 global attributes and 41 `valueSets`. Boolean attributes use valueSet `"v"`.
  - It is stale for our purposes. `v` covers 23 names including the obsolete `scoped`, `seamless` and `typemustmatch`, and treats `hidden` as boolean. It has no boolean flag on `inert` or `nomodule`, and no `playsinline`, `alpha` or `shadowroot*`.
  - It is completion-oriented, with no element-attribute rules.
- **`@webref/elements` 2.9.0** (MIT, [w3c/webref](https://github.com/w3c/webref/blob/6ecf093462c44ddccba2d6b11587113f628bbcda/LICENSE)): per-spec JSON (`html.json` has 142 elements) listing `{name, href, interface, obsolete?}`. It has **no attributes**, so it is only useful as a canonical element list.
- **MDN browser-compat-data 8.1.2** (CC0-1.0): `html.elements.<el>.<attr>.__compat` gives `spec_url`, `status {experimental, standard_track, deprecated}` and browser support, e.g. [template.json](https://github.com/mdn/browser-compat-data/blob/d6f9d6f68e18bec1990d74d710a611c7717d6c5d/html/elements/template.json). It has **no value types, boolean flags or enums**. It is good for deprecation and experimental flags, and the most permissive licence here.
- **WHATWG attribute index** ([indices.html#attributes-3](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/indices.html#attributes-3), CC BY 4.0 / BSD-3 for code):
  - One scrapeable table with 181 rows giving attribute, elements, description and value.
  - 34 rows say "Boolean attribute" and 38 list quoted keywords.
  - It is the primary source for booleans and enums. A generator could cross-check markuplint's data against it.

### Reuse recommendation for djangofmt

1. **Use `@markuplint/html-spec` for HTML data**, pinned to a version:
   - A `djangofmt_dev` subcommand downloads the npm tarball, reads `index.json`, applies the `getAttrSpecs` merge, and generates the `djangofmt_html_spec` tables.
   - The tables hold element and attribute names (for unknown-attribute detection), Boolean attributes (for #425), enums with markuplint's case-insensitive and no-surrounding-spaces defaults (to replace the hard-coded `form[method]` in `invalid_attr_value.rs`), and deprecated/obsolete flags.
   - Leave out descriptions and ARIA to avoid the CC-BY-SA text. Ship a `LICENSE-markuplint` notice and a header on generated files.
   - Add a test that checks the boolean set against the WHATWG index.
   - Leave `condition` selectors and content models for later.
   - Treat `="true"`/`="false"` on Boolean attributes as a correctness issue, not just style.
2. **Copy markuplint's name-resolution design, not its data, for Alpine and htmx:**
   - Keep an ordered table of patterns that maps each raw attribute name to a canonical name and flags (`:x`→`x` with a bound value, `@e`/`x-on:e`→ an event directive, `hx-on*` → an event), before the HTML lookup. Then `:hreff` is still caught and `x-cheeseburger` falls through to "unknown".
   - Two points of departure from markuplint:
     - (a) do not blanket-exempt directives from rules. Keep a per-directive registry with allowed elements (`x-for`, `x-if` and `x-teleport` only on `<template>`; `x-model` only on form controls; no `x-html` on `<template>`), a "value is JavaScript" flag for the safe-filter rule, value grammars and known modifiers;
     - (b) keep the raw name for case and duplicate checks. That way `@click` and `@click.prevent` are not duplicates, and `@myEvent` can be warned about.
   - Write the patterns so they work without backreferences.
3. **Seed htmx value grammars from v4.18.3's `htmx-parser/src/spec.ts`** (MIT): hx-vals `JSON | js:`, the `true|false` enums, the selector-plus-keyword grammars and the `hx-ext` list. Then update them for htmx 4: swap `hx-disable`/`hx-ignore`, add `:inherited`/`:append`, `hx-status:<code>`, `hx-method`, `hx-action`, `hx-config` and bare `hx-on`, and treat `<hx-partial>` as an element that allows `hx-target` and `hx-swap`. Do not use v5's `htmx-spec` types.
4. **Generate attribute lists from each library's own source, as the maintainer asked:** Alpine's `directive('…')` registrations across `packages/*/src`, and htmx's per-attribute doc files. markuplint gives no precedent for this, since its framework data is hand-written. Borrow its html-spec pipeline hygiene instead: pinned inputs, hand-written overrides that win, an idempotent regeneration check and scheduled CI PRs.
5. **Track markuplint v6's Rust `markuplint-types` crate** as a possible future source of URL, BCP47, MIME, JSON and CSS value validators. It is MIT but unreleased, so do not take a dependency now.

## E. Prior art: other tools and data formats

Scope: tools other than markuplint that know htmx or Alpine attribute names, and file formats that could carry attribute definitions into djangofmt. Everything was read on 2026-09-23 from the source, tarball or registry linked next to it. "Unverified" marks what was neither read nor run.

Versions examined: htmx.org 2.0.11 (tag `v2.0.11`, `fa978b2`) and 4.0.0 (tag `v4.0.0`, `4195bc0`), alpinejs 3.17.4 (`20ee0e9`), async-alpine 2.0.3 (repo `5df98f0`), JetBrains/web-types `36d0773`, vscode-html-languageservice `fcdf373`, custom-elements-manifest 2.1.0 (`d2f79f0`), html-validate 11.16.0 (`552e0d3`), HTMLHint npm 1.9.2 (repo `3cf648e`), djLint 1.46.2 (`3522886`), Biome 2.5.14 (`3260602`). Extension commits are given per tool. Install counts and dates come from the VS Code Marketplace gallery API and the JetBrains Marketplace API, both queried on 2026-09-23.

### Comparison: tools that know htmx or Alpine attributes

"Unknown" means the tool warns on an unrecognised name. "Values" and "Structure" mean diagnostics, not completion.

| JetBrains IDE + htmx web-types (2.0.11 / 4.0.0)                 | first-party IDE data | IDE's generic check (behaviour unverified)    | none (no value data)                                                             | none                                                            | web-types JSON: generated from docs in 2.x, hand-written and CI-checked in 4.x | 0BSD, 2026-09-22 / 2026-08-28                                |
| HTMX Support, JetBrains #20588 (0.0.10)                         | IDE plugin           | no                                            | completion only                                                                  | no                                                              | Kotlin list of 36 names                                                        | Apache-2.0, 2024-06-08                                       |
| HTMX Pro, JetBrains #24844 (2026.2.1)                           | paid IDE plugin      | "built-in inspections" (unverified)           | unverified                                                                       | unverified                                                      | closed source                                                                  | proprietary, 2026-07-20                                      |
