| SuperHTML (`23ef2f4`)                                           | LSP / validator      | errors on every non-spec, non-`data-*` name   | no                                                                               | no                                                              | built-in HTML model                                                            | MIT, commit 2026-08-11                                       |

`eslint-plugin-htmx` does not exist on npm (registry 404, 2026-09-23). No html-validate plugin for htmx or Alpine exists either: the npm search `html-validate` returns only the vue, angular, ember, markdown and jest-snapshot transforms.

### Notes: htmx tools

- **htmx `upgrade-check`** ships in htmx.org 4.0.0 as `dist/scripts/upgrade-check.{js,py}` with a `bin` entry ([package.json L23-L25](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/package.json#L23-L25)). It is documented as `npx htmx.org@__VERSION__ upgrade-check -- ./path/to/project/root` ([docs.md L178-L188](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs.md#L178-L188)). Its tables are hand-written:
  - `REMOVED_ATTRS` holds 8 htmx 2 names with fixes, e.g. `"hx-vars": "use hx-vals with js: prefix"`. `RENAMED_ATTRS` maps `hx-disable`→`hx-ignore` and `hx-disabled-elt`→`hx-disable` ([upgrade-check.py L21-L35](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L21-L35)).
  - `EVENT_RENAMES` covers the events ([L51-L76](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L51-L76)).

  It blanks out `{% %}`/`{{ }}` before parsing ([L241-L251](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L241-L251)) and flags the old `hx-swap` `show:#sel:pos` syntax ([L329-L337](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/upgrade-check.py#L329-L337)). It is the only first-party list, in code form, of what 4.x removed or renamed.
- **JetBrains with htmx's web-types.** The htmx docs say "htmx ships one first-party integration, for JetBrains IDEs" ([editor-support.md L9](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/editor-support.md#L9)). The IDE finds the file through the `web-types` field of `htmx.org`'s package.json. CDN users download the file and add a `web-types` field to their own `package.json` ([L36-L52](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/editor-support.md#L36-L52)). The file contents are covered under "Machine-readable sources" below. The community "HTMX Support" plugin "does not know the htmx 4 attributes" ([L54-L57](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/editor-support.md#L54-L57)). Its data is a Kotlin list of 36 `hx-` strings ([AttributeUtil.kt](https://github.com/hugohomesquita/htmx-jetbrains/blob/5e0d0e6c1ec096f6a4fa5ca4575d47176db7aff2/src/main/kotlin/com/github/hugohomesquita/htmxjetbrains/AttributeUtil.kt)).
- **HTMX Pro** is paid: product code `PHTMXPRO` with a 14-day trial, per the JetBrains Marketplace API for plugin 24844. It advertises "built-in inspections that guide you toward best practices" ([honkanen.dev/htmx](https://honkanen.dev/htmx/)). Its sources are closed, so what it checks is unverified.
- **HTMX Toolkit** ([atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit @ `09846fe`](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/tree/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411), 1,017 installs) finds attributes with one regex over the whole document: `\b(hx-[a-zA-Z0-9_:.-]+)…` ([htmlParser.ts L320](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/blob/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411/src/utils/htmlParser.ts#L320)). Its checks:
  - Unknown `hx-*` names, accepting `hx-on:`/`hx-on-` as dynamic ([diagnosticProvider.ts L102-L130](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/blob/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411/src/providers/diagnosticProvider.ts#L102-L130)), with a Levenshtein ≤ 3 suggestion ([L286](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/blob/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411/src/providers/diagnosticProvider.ts#L286)).
  - Value checks for 5 attributes ([L165-L190](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/blob/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411/src/providers/diagnosticProvider.ts#L165-L190)). For `hx-swap`, only the first token is compared with 9 styles ([attributes.ts L411-L421](https://github.com/atoolz/htmx-vscode-toolkit/blob/09846feea4099167e28bf91bb73352fc7cbbe411/src/data/attributes.ts#L411-L421)).

  Its data map holds 37 htmx 2 names and none of htmx 4's (`hx-action`, `hx-config`, `hx-method`, `hx-ignore` and `hx-status` are absent). The htmx docs nonetheless list "Support for htmx 2.x and 4.x" ([editor-support.md L67](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs/editor-support.md#L67)). By code reading, it would flag htmx 4 attributes as unknown.
- **htmx-django-intellisense** ([difegam @ `eddbda5`](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/tree/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1), 4 installs) is the closest precedent to the maintainer's idea.
  - A dev tool regenerates `htmx.catalog.json` "from the pinned HTMX 2/4 upstream tags" ([tools/README.md L14](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/README.md#L14)). It downloads GitHub tag archives and reads the attribute docs ([catalog.py L888-L958](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/catalog.py#L888-L958)), pinned at `2.0.10` and `4.0.0` ([L20-L21](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/catalog.py#L20-L21)).
  - It overlays hand-curated tables: values with a `strict` flag ([L76-L95](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/catalog.py#L76-L95)), `:append`-able attributes, deprecations, and name patterns ([L663-L701](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/catalog.py#L663-L701)). One pattern is `^hx-on(?:::[a-z0-9_.:-]+|:[a-z0-9_.:-]+|--[a-z0-9_.-]+|-[a-z0-9_.-]+)$` ([L671](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/catalog.py#L671)).
  - `check-pins` fails CI when a pinned tag disappears or a newer release exists ([pins.py L1-L11](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/tools/src/htmx_django_intellisense/pins.py#L1-L11)). The `2.0.10` pin is already behind htmx 2.0.11, released 2026-09-22.
  - Diagnostic codes are `unknown-attribute`, `version-mismatch`, `deprecated-attribute`, `invalid-value` and two Django-partial codes ([diagnostics.ts L41-L114](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/src/diagnostics.ts#L41-L114)).
  - Values are checked only for strict sets (`hx-boost`, `hx-encoding`, `hx-method`). A value that contains `:`, `{{` or `{%` is skipped ([L14-L16](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/src/diagnostics.ts#L14-L16), [L66-L88](https://github.com/difegam/htmx-django-intellisense/blob/eddbda55e3911d591f1f7e03868aba02938ae7d1/src/diagnostics.ts#L66-L88)).
- **htmx-lsp** keeps static Rust tables: `HX_TAGS` holds 32 htmx 2 names with `include_str!` markdown, and `HX_ATTRIBUTE_VALUES` is a phf map ([mod.rs L61-L107](https://github.com/ThePrimeagen/htmx-lsp/blob/a05bf01d33710f1e86e3e4165badda9826a7a9bb/lsp/src/htmx/mod.rs#L61-L107)). Completion triggers only on names starting with `hx-` ([L37](https://github.com/ThePrimeagen/htmx-lsp/blob/a05bf01d33710f1e86e3e4165badda9826a7a9bb/lsp/src/htmx/mod.rs#L37)), and the diagnostics variant is commented out ([handle.rs L55](https://github.com/ThePrimeagen/htmx-lsp/blob/a05bf01d33710f1e86e3e4165badda9826a7a9bb/lsp/src/handle.rs#L55)). The newest release on crates.io is 0.1.0 (2023-10-03), per the crates.io API.
- **Custom-data-only extensions.** These give completion and hover but no diagnostics.
  - otovo-oss.htmx-tags has 60,041 installs; its repo is archived. `build-data.py` downloads htmx tag v1.9.6 and turns `www/content/attributes/*.md` into `globalAttributes` ([L56-L90](https://github.com/otovo/htmx-tags/blob/f3d9de5028a5f7f4125e5757cd98d8f26b4352a8/build-data.py#L56-L90)).
    - Its only value set never attaches. The set is named `swap`, while the test compares attribute names such as `hx-swap` ([L54](https://github.com/otovo/htmx-tags/blob/f3d9de5028a5f7f4125e5757cd98d8f26b4352a8/build-data.py#L54), [L89](https://github.com/otovo/htmx-tags/blob/f3d9de5028a5f7f4125e5757cd98d8f26b4352a8/build-data.py#L89)), so the shipped JSON has no `valueSet` references.
    - danicc097.htmx-v2-tags is the same script re-pinned to 2.0.2 ([L56](https://github.com/danicc097/htmx-tags/blob/0c8d03510835cc1ba5ade721c931a93927c91d8d/build-data.py#L56)).
  - pfeif.hx-complete is hand-written htmx 2 data. It lists each of its 35 names twice, once as `data-hx-*`, because the format has no prefixes ([README L25-L32](https://github.com/pfeif/hx-complete/blob/81255146bf798a144b1343557d2ab919e1a7b1f6/README.md#L25-L32)).

### Notes: Alpine tools

- **IntellijAlpine** (114,622 downloads) keeps hand-written Kotlin lists:
  - 23 directives, including the deprecated `x-spread` ([AttributeUtil.kt L25-L49](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/kotlin/com/github/inxilpro/intellijalpine/attributes/AttributeUtil.kt#L25-L49)).
  - The template-only directives `x-if`, `x-for` and `x-teleport` ([L58-L62](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/kotlin/com/github/inxilpro/intellijalpine/attributes/AttributeUtil.kt#L58-L62)).
  - Prefixes, and modifier lists for events, model, time units, transitions, keys, intersect and resize ([L64-L133](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/kotlin/com/github/inxilpro/intellijalpine/attributes/AttributeUtil.kt#L64-L133)).

  `x-anchor` and `x-sort` are missing from the Kotlin sources. Third-party libraries plug in through a Kotlin extension point, `alpinePlugin` ([plugin.xml L19](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/resources/META-INF/plugin.xml#L19)); alpine-ajax, alpine-wizard and alpine-tooltip ship as such plugins ([L47-L49](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/resources/META-INF/plugin.xml#L47-L49)). There is no data-file mechanism. The plugin has no inspections: "Code Inspections" is listed under future improvements ([README L53](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/README.md#L53)). It returns attribute descriptors only for recognised directives and prefixes ([AttributesProvider.kt L14-L23](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/kotlin/com/github/inxilpro/intellijalpine/attributes/AttributesProvider.kt#L14-L23), [AttributeInfo.kt L54](https://github.com/inxilpro/IntellijAlpine/blob/e32cbcbe508ea801b53a1702ea60371d440fa03d/src/main/kotlin/com/github/inxilpro/intellijalpine/attributes/AttributeInfo.kt#L54)). Whether the IDE's generic unknown-attribute inspection then flags `x-cheeseburger` is unverified.
- **Alpine.js Tools** reports `unknown-directive`. A regex finds `x-…` names inside opening-tag ranges and strips `:arg` and `.mod`. The base name is then checked against:
  - 18 core directives;
  - `intersect`, `collapse`, `sort`, `mask`, `trap` and `anchor`;
  - the user setting `alpinejsTools.extraDirectives`

  ([diagnosticProvider.ts L46-L70](https://github.com/ConnorOnTheWeb/alpinejs-tools/blob/9a7a6c930d724787f9532d8ec944306f25d2201c/src/diagnosticProvider.ts#L46-L70), [L202-L224](https://github.com/ConnorOnTheWeb/alpinejs-tools/blob/9a7a6c930d724787f9532d8ec944306f25d2201c/src/diagnosticProvider.ts#L202-L224)). `x-resize` is missing, so it gets flagged. The extension checks neither `@`/`:` shorthands in HTML, nor modifiers, nor values. Its custom-data file holds 30 hand-written names ([alpine.html-data.json](https://github.com/ConnorOnTheWeb/alpinejs-tools/blob/9a7a6c930d724787f9532d8ec944306f25d2201c/customData/alpine.html-data.json)).
- **Alpine.js Toolkit** runs regex-based structural checks:
  - `x-if`/`x-for`/`x-teleport` on a tag other than `<template>` ("only works on a <template> tag") ([diagnosticProvider.ts L54-L87](https://github.com/danieledep/vscode-alpinejs-toolkit/blob/420a34d6d88e21ca5f4fcfc3e40aabba89fe9d54/src/diagnosticProvider.ts#L54-L87));
  - `<template x-for|x-if>` that does not have exactly one root element ([L89-L120](https://github.com/danieledep/vscode-alpinejs-toolkit/blob/420a34d6d88e21ca5f4fcfc3e40aabba89fe9d54/src/diagnosticProvider.ts#L89-L120));
  - an `x-data` component name that is not found in the workspace ([L122-L148](https://github.com/danieledep/vscode-alpinejs-toolkit/blob/420a34d6d88e21ca5f4fcfc3e40aabba89fe9d54/src/diagnosticProvider.ts#L122-L148)).
- **Alpine.js IntelliSense by adrianwilczynski** (299,304 installs, Alpine 2 era) is generated by a C# script from hand-written lists ([buildCustomData.csx](https://github.com/AdrianWilczynski/AlpineIntelliSense/blob/2ac4f72360c7ab2d6d491b15e308760c9a49d289/tools/buildCustomData.csx)). Custom data has no patterns, so 53 of its 79 names are `x-on:<event>` literals and 7 are `x-bind:<attr>` literals ([customData/html.json](https://github.com/AdrianWilczynski/AlpineIntelliSense/blob/2ac4f72360c7ab2d6d491b15e308760c9a49d289/customData/html.json)). The pcbowers extension ships 22 hand-written names ([html.html-data.json](https://github.com/pcbowers/alpine-intellisense/blob/6854d1612eeae7d192f26c2209c97dba39756c34/syntaxes/html.html-data.json)).
- **eslint-plugin-alpinejs** 1.3.2 ([npm](https://www.npmjs.com/package/eslint-plugin-alpinejs)) has no repository field, and its description reads "coming soon". Its tarball's [`dist/index.js`](https://unpkg.com/eslint-plugin-alpinejs@1.3.2/dist/index.js) (L12941-L13105) contains three rules on top of `@html-eslint/parser`: `prefer-template-x-if`, `no-unused-xrefs` and `no-raw-dom-access`. Only `x-if` is checked; `x-for` is not. The sibling package `eslint-plugin-alpine` 1.0.0 is only a README placeholder.
- **prettier-plugin-django-alpine** 1.3.0 ([npm](https://www.npmjs.com/package/prettier-plugin-django-alpine)) chooses JS formatting with two regexes: `^:[\w\d-]+|x-(data|show|bind|text|html|model|modelable|if|id)…` for expressions and `@[\w\d-]+|x-(init|on|effect)…` for statements ([`alpine.mjs`](https://unpkg.com/prettier-plugin-django-alpine@1.3.0/alpine.mjs) L4-L6). It validates nothing.

### Notes: generic HTML tools

- **djLint** has no htmx or Alpine rule. H037 reads whole names including `.` and `:`, so `x-on:click.prevent` is not misread as a duplicate ([H037.py L1-L7](https://github.com/djlint/djLint/blob/3522886b0ee5211f7c91891efa8ee00a6878a529/src/djlint/rules/H037.py#L1-L7)). The opt-in `format_attribute_js_json` selects attributes with a heuristic regex ([settings.py L460-L472](https://github.com/djlint/djLint/blob/3522886b0ee5211f7c91891efa8ee00a6878a529/src/djlint/settings.py#L460-L472)):
  - it covers `on[a-z]+`, `x-[a-z\-]+`, `@[a-z\-]+`, `:[a-z\-]+`, `v-…` and Angular forms;
  - it has no directive list, and `x-on:click` or `@click.prevent` do not match it.
- **html-validate** has an experimental rule, `no-unknown-attributes`, added in 10.17.0 ([docs L9, L31-L45](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/docs/rules/no-unknown-attributes.md)). It is opt-in: no preset under [`src/config/presets/`](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/tree/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/config/presets) enables it.
  - It skips any name matching `/^:/, /^@/, /^ng-/i, /^v-/i, /^x-/i, /^\[/, /^#/` ([no-unknown-attributes.ts L10-L14](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/rules/no-unknown-attributes.ts#L10-14)), so `x-cheeseburger` passes.
  - `hx-*` is not skipped, so htmx attributes are reported unless element metadata declares them ([L34-L48](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/rules/no-unknown-attributes.ts#L34-48)).
- **HTMLHint**'s rule set ([src/core/rules](https://github.com/htmlhint/HTMLHint/tree/3cf648ea19cb6db9cfb16bf26246cfcc49aedffd/src/core/rules)) has no unknown-attribute rule, and the source does not mention htmx or Alpine.
- **Biome**'s HTML parser has Vue, Svelte, Astro and Angular syntax ([syntax/](https://github.com/biomejs/biome/tree/3260602e03dd44b79afbd36ec77a71a1e87b7ed7/crates/biome_html_parser/src/syntax)). Vue syntax is gated by the `vue` option ([mod.rs L73](https://github.com/biomejs/biome/blob/3260602e03dd44b79afbd36ec77a71a1e87b7ed7/crates/biome_html_parser/src/syntax/mod.rs#L73)). The `biome_html_*` crates contain nothing for Alpine or htmx. Useful analogues:
  - `noVueUndeclaredDirectives` takes a `globals` option listing extra directive names ([no_vue_undeclared_directives.rs L66-L93](https://github.com/biomejs/biome/blob/3260602e03dd44b79afbd36ec77a71a1e87b7ed7/crates/biome_html_analyze/src/lint/nursery/no_vue_undeclared_directives.rs#L66-L93)).
  - `useVueValidVOn` checks modifiers against a built-in list extended by a `modifiers` option ([use_vue_valid_v_on.rs L137-L142](https://github.com/biomejs/biome/blob/3260602e03dd44b79afbd36ec77a71a1e87b7ed7/crates/biome_html_analyze/src/lint/correctness/use_vue_valid_v_on.rs#L137-L142)).
  - `useVueVForKey` and `noVueVIfWithVFor` sit in the same `correctness/` group.

  The license is `MIT OR Apache-2.0` ([Cargo.toml L12](https://github.com/biomejs/biome/blob/3260602e03dd44b79afbd36ec77a71a1e87b7ed7/Cargo.toml#L12)).
- **SuperHTML** reports every attribute outside its element and global model, except `data-*`, as `invalid_attr` ([Element.zig L541-L556](https://github.com/kristoff-it/superhtml/blob/23ef2f44ca0df2d2e05a0be3874370553c5b591d/src/html/Element.zig#L541-L556)). Alpine syntax and un-prefixed htmx would therefore error unless run with `--syntax-only` ([README L22](https://github.com/kristoff-it/superhtml/blob/23ef2f44ca0df2d2e05a0be3874370553c5b591d/README.md#L22)). This comes from reading the code; it was not run.

### Interchange formats for attribute definitions

| Capability                                | VS Code custom data 1.1                  | JetBrains web-types 2.0                           | Custom Elements Manifest 2.1.0 | html-validate metadata                    |
| ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| Exact names                               | yes                                      | yes                                               | yes, per element only          | yes                                       |
| Name patterns                             | no                                       | regex and template sequences                      | no                             | glob (`*` = `.+`)                         |
| Directive arguments and modifiers         | no                                       | yes (Vue reference)                               | no                             | no                                        |
| Value enums                               | completion lists (`values`, `valueSets`) | `type: "enum"` with nested `values`               | TypeScript type text only      | `enum` of strings, regexes, named regexes |
| Value kind (none / plain / JS expression) | no                                       | `value.kind`: `no-value` / `plain` / `expression` | no                             | `boolean`, `omit`                         |
| Element restriction                       | attributes under `tags[]`                | nesting, `exclusive-contributions`                | inherent (element-centric)     | per-element keys                          |
| Deprecation                               | no field                                 | `deprecated`, `obsolete`, `since`                 | `deprecated`                   | `deprecated`                              |
| Published by htmx                         | no (community only)                      | **yes**, in the npm tarball of 2.x and 4.x        | no                             | no                                        |
| Published by Alpine or plugins            | no (community only)                      | no                                                | no                             | no                                        |
| Published by Async Alpine                 | no                                       | no                                                | no                             | no                                        |

- **VS Code HTML custom data.** The top-level keys are `version`, `tags[]`, `globalAttributes[]` and `valueSets[]`. An attribute has `name`, `description`, `valueSet`, `values[]` and `references` ([customData.schema.json](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-html-languageservice/blob/fcdf373c4c30579f668bf9198270573ede893497/docs/customData.schema.json)).
  - Files load through the `html.customData` setting or `contributes.html.customData` ([vscode-custom-data README L14-L15](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-custom-data/blob/01df47ad7cc0b140624fc941ff57618c36ed46a1/README.md#L14-L15)). They feed completion and hover only ([customData.md](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-html-languageservice/blob/fcdf373c4c30579f668bf9198270573ede893497/docs/customData.md)).
  - Names are matched exactly after lowercasing (`a.name.toLowerCase() === attribute`, [dataProvider.ts L179](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-html-languageservice/blob/fcdf373c4c30579f668bf9198270573ede893497/src/languageFacts/dataProvider.ts#L179)). That is why the extensions above enumerate literal names. It is the easiest format to read, and it cannot express `x-on:*`, `hx-on:*`, modifiers or prefixes.
  - The standard HTML dataset is published as `@vscode/web-custom-data` 0.6.3 (MIT) and contains no htmx or Alpine entries.
- **JetBrains web-types** ([README L7](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/README.MD#L7): "framework-agnostic format"). `name` and `version` are required. `contributions` spans the html, css and js namespaces.
  - A contribution can carry `pattern`, `deprecated`, `obsolete`, `since`, `priority` and `proximity`. An attribute adds `value` with `kind` (`no-value` / `plain` / `expression`), `type` (including `"enum"` and `"of-match"`), `required` and `default` ([schema L955-L1010](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/schema/web-types.json#L955-L1010), [L492-L505](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/schema/web-types.json#L492-L505)).
  - A pattern is either `{regex, case-sensitive}` or a template sequence. Templates can use symbol references (`items`), `or`, `required: false`, `repeat`, `unique` and `delegate` ([L593-L667](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/schema/web-types.json#L593-L667)). The JetBrains docs list seven pattern kinds ([Poly Symbols, Patterns](https://plugins.jetbrains.com/docs/intellij/polysymbols-implementation.html#patterns)).
  - The format can express Alpine's shapes. JetBrains' Vue reference models `v-<dir>[:<arg>][.<mod>]*` and `@<event>[.<mod>]*` ([vue@2.6.0 L134-L210](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/examples/references/vue@2.6.0.web-types.json#L134-L210)). Each directive gets its own argument pattern and modifier list, e.g. `on`: stop, prevent, capture, self, keyCode/keyAlias, native, once, passive ([L699-L769](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/examples/references/vue@2.6.0.web-types.json#L699-L769)). That is the structure of `x-on:keyup.enter` and `@click.prevent`.
  - Enum example: [vue@3.2.0 L571-L587](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/examples/references/vue@3.2.0.web-types.json#L571-L587).
  - Discovery: an npm package's `web-types` field, a project `package.json`, or an IDE-plugin extension point ([Including Web Types](https://plugins.jetbrains.com/docs/intellij/polysymbols-web-types.html#including-web-types)). Libraries that don't ship web-types can be published under the `@web-types` npm scope ([README L56-L67](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/README.MD#L56-L67)). Its `packages/` folder holds only Vue, Quasar, Lit and similar libraries, nothing for htmx or Alpine (grep).
  - Costs for a Rust reader:
    - References such as `/js/events` resolve against the IDE's DOM data.
    - `#...`/`$...` are completion markers.
    - `delegate`, `priority` and `proximity` only affect IDE behaviour.
    - The schema does not specify a regex flavour for name patterns. The Vue reference uses a lookahead, `(?!\.).*` ([L719](https://github.com/JetBrains/web-types/blob/36d07738e23d2bfeebc4057e14b85546435b8c1c/examples/references/vue@2.6.0.web-types.json#L719)), which the Rust `regex` crate rejects. htmx's two regexes are plain.
    - crates.io has no web-types or custom-data parser crate (search on 2026-09-23).
- **Custom Elements Manifest.** Package → modules → declarations. A `CustomElement` has `tagName`, `attributes[]` (name, summary, description, `type.text` as a TS type string, default, fieldName, deprecated), events, slots and CSS parts/properties/states ([schema.d.ts L224-L302](https://github.com/webcomponents/custom-elements-manifest/blob/d2f79f0d22c4d48a68628cf867c2319576ffc5d2/schema.d.ts#L224-L302)). Tools find it through the package.json `customElements` field ([README L28-L33](https://github.com/webcomponents/custom-elements-manifest/blob/d2f79f0d22c4d48a68628cf867c2319576ffc5d2/README.md#L28-L33)).
  - It has no global attributes and no name patterns. It could describe htmx 4's `<hx-partial>` at most.
  - htmx.org 2.0.11 and 4.0.0 declare no `customElements` field (npm).
  - The last schema release is 2.1.0 (2024-05-16) and the last repo commit is from 2024-10-02.
- **html-validate element metadata** (not asked, but a working linter format). It is JSON or JS keyed by element, with `"*"` for global attributes ([elements.md L842](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/docs/usage/elements.md#L842)).
  - Each attribute can have `enum` (strings, regexes or named regexes), `boolean`, `omit`, `list`, `required`, `deprecated` and `reference` ([elements.md L359, L432](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/docs/usage/elements.md#L359)).
  - Keys containing `*` are case-insensitive globs where `*` matches one or more characters ([L549-L568](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/docs/usage/elements.md#L549-568); [migrate.ts L70-L75](https://gitlab.com/html-validate/html-validate/-/blob/552e0d3475aaeae289750b78773689d00bd816e7/src/meta/migrate.ts#L70-75)).

### Machine-readable sources a generator could pin (ranked)

**htmx**

1. **The web-types file in the `htmx.org` npm tarball.** It is first-party, versioned with every release, and 0BSD.
   - Paths: 4.0.0 `"web-types": "dist/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json"` ([package.json L10](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/package.json#L10)); 2.0.11 `editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json` ([package.json L27](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/package.json#L27)).
   - Contents of 4.0.0: 49 attributes, the `<hx-partial>` element ([L10-L16](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L10-L16)), 58 htmx events ([L412](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L412)) and 6 CSS classes. Three entries use patterns:
     - `hx-on` as `hx-on:` + JS event or `hx-on::` + htmx event ([L77-L104](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L77-L104));
     - `hx-status` with regex `hx-status:(\d{3}|\d{2}[xX]|\d[xX]{2})` ([L245-L252](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L245-L252));
     - `hx-live:*` with regex `hx-live:.+` ([L334-L341](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L334-L341)).
   - Contents of 2.0.11: 36 attributes, with `hx-vars` marked `deprecated: true` ([L302-L308](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L302-L308)), and one pattern, `hx-on` ([L124-L148](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L124-L148)).
   - Maintenance in 4.x: the file is hand-edited in `src/editors/` and copied by `build:editors` ([package.json L34](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/package.json#L34)). CI job `content` ([ci.yml L57-L61](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/.github/workflows/ci.yml#L57-L61)) runs `check_web_types.py`. That script fails when the version differs from package.json, a doc URL has no local page, or the core attributes, elements or htmx events differ from the reference docs ([L147-L173](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/scripts/content/check_web_types.py#L147-L173)).
   - Maintenance in 2.x: `scripts/generate-web-types.mjs` builds the file from `www/content/attributes/hx-*.md` ([L9-L19](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/scripts/generate-web-types.mjs#L9-L19)). It detects the "is inherited" and "has been deprecated" phrases and hard-codes the `hx-on` pattern ([L57-L108](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/fa978b24e75fb03c137bf2cdae4fef0e711cf8a1/scripts/generate-web-types.mjs#L57-L108)).
   - Gaps:
     - No attribute in either file has a `value`.
     - The 4.x `:inherited` modifier appears only as prose in `description-sections` ([L54-L58](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json#L54-L58)), and `:append` ([docs.md L682-L694](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs.md#L682-L694)) is absent.
     - Two runtime settings change names, and neither is modelled: the default `data-hx-` prefix ([docs.md L1986](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs.md#L1986)) and `metaCharacter` ([L2006](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/www/src/content/docs.md#L2006)). The file contains no `data-hx` entry.
     - The dash spellings `hx-on-…`/`hx-on--…` accepted by the third-party tools above are not modelled.
     - Extension attributes are not cross-checked. The bundled hx-multipart extension reads `hx-multipart:connect` and `hx-multipart:close` ([src/ext/hx-multipart.js L381-L382](https://github.com/bigskysoftware/htmx/blob/4195bc0dc26b612ea5bea46f5914c6386eadeba3/src/ext/hx-multipart.js#L381-L382)), and neither name is in the file.
2. **The htmx docs markdown at a pinned tag**: `www/src/content/reference/01-attributes/*.md` in 4.x, `www/content/attributes/*.md` in 2.x. This is the input to htmx's own 2.x generator and 4.x CI check, and to the otovo and htmx-django-intellisense builders. Value grammars exist there only as prose.
3. **The htmx source** (`src/htmx.js`, `src/ext/*.js`). It decides runtime behaviour, e.g. which attributes an extension reads, but it is not a data format.
4. **Third-party catalogues**: htmx-django-intellisense's `htmx.catalog.json` (Apache-2.0, curated values), HTMX Toolkit, hx-complete, htmx-lsp, `typed-htmx` (ISC, 2024) and `jsx-htmx` (AGPL-3.0-only, which rules it out for an MIT project). They are good for cross-checks, but none is authoritative and all lag behind.

**Alpine, including plugins and Async Alpine**

1. **Nothing first-party is machine-readable.** The alpinejs 3.17.4 tarball contains only `src/` and `dist/` JS. `alpinejs` and `@alpinejs/*` 3.17.4 declare no `web-types` or `customElements` field. The async-alpine 2.0.3 tarball holds README, LICENSE and dist JS. Neither repo contains web-types, custom data or a CEM (grep at `20ee0e9` and `5df98f0`).
2. **The Alpine source at a pinned tag.** Directives are registered with `directive('<name>', …)` under `packages/*/src`. The prefix can be changed: `prefixAsString = 'x-'` and `setPrefix` ([directives.js L6-L14](https://github.com/alpinejs/alpine/blob/20ee0e923b3f34e6d6467a31d1b97dd43c85a41d/packages/alpinejs/src/directives.js#L6-L14)). Async Alpine builds its names with `Alpine.prefixed('load-src')` and similar calls, around `const directive = 'load'` ([async-alpine.js L9-L11](https://github.com/Accudio/async-alpine/blob/5df98f088709e45d90fac4652fcfff6c9371529b/src/async-alpine.js#L9-L11)). A script can extract these; the Alpine section of this study has the details. Modifiers are parsed inside each directive and not declared anywhere.
3. **Third-party lists**: IntellijAlpine (MIT, Kotlin) and Alpine.js Tools (MIT, TS and custom data). They are hand-maintained and each has gaps (no `x-anchor`/`x-sort`; no `x-resize`).

**Not useful for htmx or Alpine names.** `@webref/events` 1.26.0 lists 442 spec-defined events, and `@webref/elements` 2.9.0 and `@vscode/web-custom-data` 0.6.3 cover standard HTML only. None of their tarballs mentions htmx or Alpine. They could at most feed hints for `x-on:`/`hx-on:` event names, and both libraries accept custom events anyway.

### Takeaways for djangofmt

- **htmx can be generated from htmx's own file.** Pin `htmx.org` (4.x path `dist/editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json`, 0BSD) in the dev-only generator. To support both majors, add 2.x's `editors/jetbrains/htmx.web-types.json`; htmx-django-intellisense merges both and reports version mismatches. Read the names and translate the three patterns: a `regex` entry becomes a Rust regex, and an `hx-on` template becomes a prefix match. For 4.x the file is kept in sync with the docs by htmx's CI, which is the cheapest route to what the maintainer asked for. Keep a small hand-written overlay for what the file lacks:
  - the `:inherited`/`:append` suffixes;
  - `hx-multipart:*`;
  - removed and renamed htmx 2 names, which `upgrade-check.py` already lists;
  - all value grammars.
- **Alpine needs a source scraper or a hand-written table.** No first-party data exists. Every surveyed Alpine table is hand-written and already stale somewhere (IntellijAlpine lacks `x-anchor`/`x-sort`; Alpine.js Tools lacks `x-resize`; HTMX Toolkit lacks htmx 4 entirely). Whatever djangofmt picks, a drift guard earns its cost. Examples: htmx-django-intellisense's `check-pins`, which fails when a newer upstream tag exists, and htmx's `check_web_types.py`, which diffs the file against the docs. As a `just` recipe, such a guard would make stale tables fail loudly.
- **Validate values only where the grammar is closed, and skip templated values.** Neither ecosystem publishes value grammars. Where values are checked strictly at all, it is closed sets only: `hx-boost`, `hx-encoding` and `hx-method` in htmx-django-intellisense, and the first token of `hx-swap` in HTMX Toolkit. htmx-django-intellisense and htmx's own checker also skip or blank values containing `{{`/`{%`. djangofmt's Django-mode values with template tags need the same bail-out.
- **Extra libraries should come from pyproject lists, not an interchange file.** User-registered directives (Async Alpine, in-house ones) are handled in prior art by plain name lists in config: Biome's `globals` and `modifiers`, and Alpine.js Tools' `extraDirectives`. html-validate adds glob keys such as `data-*`. That fits `[tool.djangofmt.lint.<rule>]`, e.g. `extend-directives = ["load", "load-src"]` plus glob or regex patterns. Optional prefix settings would mirror Alpine's `setPrefix` and htmx's `prefix`/`metaCharacter`.
- **If a file format is ever needed, choose web-types.** VS Code custom data cannot express patterns: tools enumerate 53 `x-on:<event>` names or duplicate every `data-hx-*` name. CEM is element-centric and has no global attributes. web-types is the only format that expresses Alpine's `x-on:<event>.<mod>*` shape, as the Vue reference proves, and it is the only format htmx publishes. However, nothing in the Alpine ecosystem ships web-types. Its full pattern engine is IDE-oriented, with symbol references, completion markers and delegate/priority semantics, and its regexes may use lookaround that Rust's `regex` rejects. A reader limited to exact names, `regex` patterns and literal-prefix templates would cover htmx's file; defer anything more until a user needs it.
- **Unknown-name checks are prefix-scoped in every surveyed tool.** They look only at names under a library prefix (`hx-*`, `x-*`). Generic linters either skip framework syntax (html-validate skips `x-*`, `@*` and `:*`) or reject it outright (SuperHTML). `@event`/`:attr` shorthands take arbitrary event and attribute names, so on those only modifiers can be checked, much as Biome's `useVueValidVOn` does for Vue.
- **Most proposed structural rules have no prior art.** Among the surveyed tools, only Alpine.js Toolkit (`x-if`/`x-for`/`x-teleport` on `<template>`, exactly one root) and eslint-plugin-alpinejs (`prefer-template-x-if`) implement Alpine structural rules. None of the surveyed tools checks `:key` on `x-for`, capital letters in `x-on` event names, or `hx-vals` being JSON or `js:`-prefixed. Those rules have no upstream table to generate from. Biome's Vue rules (`useVueVForKey`, `noVueVIfWithVFor`) are the nearest implemented designs.
